Method: 提出 PanoWorld，核心包括：(1) Dense Panoramic Ray-Conditioning (DPRC) 用于当前动作建模；(2) Geometry-aware Memory Augmentation (GMA) 实现长程记忆，利用全景旋转等变性将相机轨迹简化为固定朝向的平移；(3) 三阶段训练流水线逐步优化各组件。同时构建 World360 数据集，包含全景无人机实拍与 AirSim360 高质量模拟视频。

Result: 在 World360 数据集上的大量实验表明，PanoWorld 显著优于其他替代方法，在大规模空间变化和多样光照条件下展现出更强的物理一致性。

Conclusion: PanoWorld 通过旋转等变性的几何先验有效解决了全景世界模型的长程记忆问题，模型、代码及数据集将开源。

Abstract: In this work, we aim to address the challenge of long-range memory in panoramic world models by exploiting the rotation-equivariant property of omnidirectional representations, where rotation can be treated as an implicit geometric transformation.Building on this insight, we propose PanoWorld, which simplifies camera trajectories into translations via fixed headings for both current-action modeling and long-range memory through Dense Panoramic Ray-Conditioning (DPRC) and Geometry-aware Memory Augmentation (GMA).Then, a three-stage training pipeline is introduced to progressively optimize each component. To better evaluate physical consistency under large-scale spatial variations and diverse illumination conditions, where existing datasets are relatively stable, we construct World360, a large-scale dataset consisting of both real-world video clips collected via panoramic unmanned aerial vehicles and high-quality simulated clips generated by AirSim360.Extensive experiments on World360 demonstrate the effectiveness of PanoWorld, outperforming alternative methods by a large margin.Our models, training code, and dataset will be publicly available. More information can be found on our project page: https://lihaoy-ux.github.io/panoworld-page/.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [71] [HALO: Hybrid Adaptive Latent Reasoning for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08775)
*Micah Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HALO 是一种混合自适应隐状态精炼方法，通过在冻结的预训练语言模型上添加粗精炼阶段，并结合基于 token 评分与单调停止机制的 selective 第二阶段精炼，实现以更少的计算量达到优于固定精炼策略的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在冻结语言模型上添加额外自适应计算的方法存在两难：单步精炼（fixed-1）能力不足，而全局强制两步精炼（fixed-2）虽提升性能但计算开销大且边际收益递减。核心动机是探索更优的精炼计算分配策略，而非简单堆叠精炼步数。

Method: 提出 HALO（Hybrid Adaptive Latent-Refinement）方法：（1）第一阶段对所有 token 进行粗精炼；（2）第二阶段通过 token scoring 机制筛选重要 token 子集，结合单调 token halting 策略进行选择性隐状态精炼，从而自适应地分配计算资源。

Result: 在 MMLU-Pro 和 GPQA-Diamond 构建的公开基准上，HALO 在同类方法中取得最佳总体平均性能，超越 frozen backbone、fixed-1 和 fixed-2。内部分析显示 HALO 以少于 fixed-1 的平均精炼步数，达到接近 fixed-2 的 token 精度，且控制器计算开销更低。

Conclusion: 性能提升的关键并非简单增加精炼步数，而在于更优的精炼计算分配策略。HALO 通过自适应选择性精炼，同时实现了最强的性能表现和更低的计算开销。

Abstract: We study how to improve a frozen pretrained language model with a small amount of adaptive extra computation. A simple approach is to add additional refinement steps on top of the backbone hidden states, but fixed extra refinement can be wasteful: a one-step refinement head may be too weak, while forcing a second full-sequence refinement step everywhere can increase compute without improving transfer. We introduce HALO, a hybrid adaptive latent-refinement method that combines a coarse refinement stage with selective second-stage latent refinement on a subset of tokens chosen by token scoring and monotonic token halting. On the main public benchmark comparison built from MMLU-Pro and GPQA-Diamond, HALO achieves the best overall average among the paper-facing methods, outperforming the frozen backbone, fixed-1, and fixed-2. Internal analysis further shows that HALO reaches nearly the same token-accuracy level as fixed-2 while using fewer average applied refine steps than fixed-1 and far fewer than fixed-2. These results suggest that the key advantage is not simply more refinement, but a better allocation of refinement: HALO achieves the strongest paper-facing result while also using less measured controller compute than either fixed baseline.

</details>


### [72] [An Emergent Mirage: Is Emergent Misalignment and Realignment Indeed a Robust Phenomenon?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09053)
*Abhinav Rao,Liancheng Gong,Bin Hu,Atharva Naik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了"涌现性失对齐"（Emergent Misalignment）现象，发现此前报告的快速失对齐与重对齐行为在很大程度上可归因于数据集表面特征（如回复长度）的混淆，且先前声称的LoRA表征相变等机制性信号与行为失对齐并无稳健关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期有研究声称，语言模型在窄领域的有害数据集上微调后会突然涌现出广泛的有害行为（即Emergent Misalignment），且可通过有限的重对齐逆转。本文旨在通过受控的循环微调实验，系统检验该现象的稳健性及其背后的机制性解释。

Method: 作者采用受控的微调循环（对齐-失对齐-重对齐反复迭代），在全程跟踪模型行为表现的同时监控LoRA表征的变化。重点控制了数据集表面特征（如回复长度差异），以区分真正的对齐变化与表面伪影。

Result: （1）成功复现了EM现象；（2）失对齐与重对齐均对数据集表面特征高度敏感，控制回复长度后，快速的"重对齐"现象基本消失；（3）此前报道的LoRA表征相变等机制性签名与行为失对齐在训练过程中并不存在稳健相关性。

Conclusion: 当前关于Emergent Misalignment的证据不如先前声称的那样稳健，研究社区需要建立更严格的评估协议，仔细控制数据集表面的混淆因素，以真正识别EM现象的稳健性。

Abstract: Recent work has reported Emergent Misalignment (EM), where language models fine-tuned on narrow, domain-specific misaligned datasets abruptly acquire broadly misaligned behavior, alongside evidence that this behavior can be reversed through limited realignment. We systematically study repeated alignment and misalignment cycles using controlled fine-tuning loops while tracking behavioral performance, and LoRA representations throughout training. Although we reproduce EM, we find that both misalignment and realignment are highly sensitive to superficial dataset characteristics, with apparent rapid realignment largely disappearing after controlling for response-length differences. We further find that previously reported mechanistic signatures, including representational phase transitions in LoRA space, do not consistently correlate with behavioral misalignment across training. Our results suggest that current evidence for EM is less robust than previously claimed and highlight the need for evaluation protocols that carefully control for these surface level dataset artifacts to identify the robustness of the EM phenomenon.

</details>


### [73] [AgentKGV: Agentic LLM-RAG Framework with Two-Stage Training for the Fact Verification of Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09092)
*Yumin Heo,Hyeon-gu Lee,Sumin Seo,Youngjoong Ko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了AgentKGV——一个面向工业级知识图谱事实验证的智能体框架，结合动态路由与迭代查询改写来解决文档检索中的表层形式不匹配问题，并通过两阶段训练策略（蒸馏SFT+GRPO）在提升准确性的同时显著降低检索成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模自动构建的知识图谱不可避免地包含事实错误，而工业场景下可靠且高效地进行事实验证面临两大挑战：一是文档检索中的表层形式不匹配导致验证困难；二是大规模部署时需要在准确率和成本效率之间取得平衡。

Method: 本文提出AgentKGV框架，核心包括：(1) 动态路由与迭代查询改写机制，应对文档级检索中实体/关系的表层形式不匹配问题；(2) 两阶段训练策略——第一阶段为基于蒸馏的回合级SFT，将大教师模型的推理能力迁移至小模型以实现稳定的查询改写与推理；第二阶段为轨迹级GRPO，优化搜索策略以减少不必要的检索调用。

Result: 在开放域T-REx基准的长尾谓词划分上，AgentKGV相比单轮RAG在macro-F1上提升5.5个百分点；两阶段训练进一步带来9.4个百分点的提升。GRPO将平均搜索调用次数从3.24次降至1.63次，且未牺牲准确率。

Conclusion: AgentKGV通过智能体式检索架构与两阶段训练，在工业级知识图谱事实验证任务上同时实现了高准确率与低成本，验证了蒸馏SFT与GRPO联合优化在提升推理能力与搜索效率方面的有效性。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) are often automatically constructed from large-scale corpora, but they inevitably contain factual errors due to noisy sources and extraction failures, and verifying them reliably at industrial scale remains a critical challenge. To address this, we propose AgentKGV, the Agentic LLM-RAG framework for KG fact Verification, that integrates dynamic routing and iterative query rewriting, which handles surface-form mismatch in document-level retrieval. To make this framework more accurate and cost-efficient for industrial deployment, we further introduce a two-stage training strategy: turn-level distillation-based SFT that transfers reasoning ability from a large teacher model into a small model for stable query rewriting and reasoning, and trajectory-level GRPO that optimizes the search policy to reduce unnecessary retrieval at scale. On the long-tail-predicate split of the open-domain T-REx benchmark, our framework improves macro-F1 over single-turn RAG by 5.5 \%p, and two-stage training does it further by 9.4 \%p. GRPO also cuts the average number of search calls from 3.24 to 1.63 without lowering accuracy.

</details>


### [74] [PRecG: Legal Precedent Retrieval with Graph Neural Networks and Rhetorical Role Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09094)
*Devanshu Verma,Vasudha Bhatnagar,Vikas Kumar,Balaji Ganesan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PReG管道，通过基于修辞角色将法律判决书分解为语义单元、构建知识图谱、学习实体上下文表示并逐层聚合，形成文档级嵌入以计算语义相似度，解决了现有方法将法律文档视为单一文本而忽略修辞结构的问题，在印度法律数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动先例检索方法将法律文档映射到低维语义空间，基于表示邻近性计算相似度，但将法律文档视为单一文本，忽略了法律技术细节的修辞组织（rhetorical organization）。这导致两个关键不足：(1) 忽视了细粒度的法律语义；(2) 无法区分法律实体和概念因修辞角色不同而产生的上下文重要性差异。

Method: 提出PReG（Precedent Retrieval with Rhetorical Graphs）管道，采用分层学习方法：(1) 基于句子修辞角色将文档分解为不同的语义单元（段落）；(2) 对每个修辞段落构建知识图谱，捕获段落内的法律实体及其关系；(3) 学习实体的上下文表示并聚合得到段落级嵌入；(4) 进一步整合段落级嵌入生成统一的文档级表示；(5) 基于文档级表示计算文档对之间的语义相似度。

Result: 在基准印度法律数据集上进行了广泛实验，与最先进的基线方法（state-of-the-art baselines）进行对比，实验结果表明所提方法在检索性能上具有显著有效性。

Conclusion: 通过分层建模法律文档的修辞结构并结合知识图谱捕获实体关系，PReG管道能够有效提升法律先例检索的准确性，证明了在法律信息检索任务中纳入文档修辞组织信息的必要性和价值。

Abstract: Legal precedent retrieval is a fundamental task in legal case preparation, planning, litigation strategy, and legal research. Current approaches for automatic precedent retrieval map legal documents to a low-dimensional semantic space and compute similarity based on the proximity of their representations. These approaches treat legal documents as monolithic texts, ignoring the rhetorical organization of the legal technicalities. Ergo, they overlook nuanced legal meanings and fail to distinguish the contextual significance of legal entities and concepts that vary based on their rhetorical roles within the document.
  To address this insufficiency, we propose the PRecG pipeline that computes the similarity between pairs of legal judgments by hierarchically learning their representations. The process begins by decomposing each document into distinct semantic units (segments) based on the rhetorical roles of sentences. For each rhetorical segment, a knowledge graph is constructed to capture the legal entities and their relationships within the segment. Contextual representations of the entities are then learned and aggregated to derive segment-level embeddings. These embeddings are further integrated to produce a unified document-level representation, and finally, the semantic similarity between a pair of documents is computed. We validate the performance of the proposed approach through extensive experiments on a benchmark Indian legal dataset, comparing it against state-of-the-art baselines to demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [75] [Augmenting Fundamental Analysis with Large Language Models: A RAG-Based System for Generating Investor Briefs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09121)
*Bartosz Ziółko,Kacper Dobrzeniewski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用GPT-4o结合检索增强生成（RAG）方法，对SEC EDGAR文件、宏观经济数据及公司报告进行预处理，并基于Kitchin周期构建投资者知识文档，自动生成9家公司的分析简报，最终由9名个人投资者评估该方法的有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大语言模型（LLM）在基本面分析中的应用潜力，特别是将SEC文件、宏观经济数据等多源异构信息自动化处理，以辅助个人投资者进行投资决策，降低信息处理门槛。

Method: 收集EDGAR系统中的SEC文件、宏观经济数据（GDP、通胀）及公司报告并进行预处理；基于Kitchin周期构建示范性投资者知识文档；通过API将这些数据以类RAG方式发送至GPT-4o模型，在4周内持续扫描9家目标公司数据，自动生成分析简报；邀请9名个人投资者对简报的有用性进行主观评估。

Result: 论文未提供具体量化结果，但描述了通过LLM+RAG流程成功为9家公司自动生成了持续4周的分析简报，并由9名个人投资者完成了有用性评估。

Conclusion: LLM结合RAG方法能够在一定程度上自动化处理公司基本面分析中的多源异构数据，为个人投资者提供决策辅助，初步验证了该方法的实用潜力。

Abstract: In this study, we examine the opportunities brought by Large Language Models (LLMs) to various aspects of fundamental analysis of companies based on their reports as well as data and documents describing macroeconomic situation like GDP and inflation changes as well as documents filled to the U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission (SEC) which can be found in EDGAR. We were preprocessing those data and than sending via API to gpt-4o model in a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) like regime. We prepared as well a document describing an exemplar investor knowledge based on Kitchin cycles. We were scanning data important for analysis of 9 companies for 4 weeks. Using LLM we were producing automatic briefs about them. They were sent to nine participants who are individual investors to evaluate usefulness of such approach to data analysis.

</details>


### [76] [Complexity-Guided Component-wise Initialization for Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09204)
*Konstantin Garbers,Nicholas Oh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 预训练语言模型呈现结构化的权重谱模式，但仅通过模仿这些谱特征（分量级幅度与奇异值分布）来初始化模型，并不能带来性能优势；有效的复用需要保留比谱形状更丰富的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预训练模型重复出现的结构化权重谱（深度趋势、频谱集中度等）是否可以作为有效的初始化信号，从而加速或改善 GPT-2 风格语言模型的预训练。

Method: 首先分析 11 个不同规模、语言、分词器和训练语料的 GPT-2 风格预训练检查点，测量各层和 Transformer 子组件的 Frobenius 范数与有效秩熵；然后构造模仿预训练模型分量级幅度和谱分布的初始化方案，与多种标准初始化方法对比，评估其对模型结构谱模式和下游性能的影响。

Result: 各检查点展现共同的深度趋势（残差写入矩阵的尺度递增和更强的频谱集中）；谱匹配初始化确实改变了模型的结构谱模式，但未带来对应的性能优势；直接复用预训练权重仍具竞争力，而仅粗糙的谱匹配并非可靠的优化策略。

Conclusion: 预训练谱特征是诊断模型结构的有用工具，但有效的初始化复用需要保留比分量级尺度和奇异值形状更丰富的信息。

Abstract: Pretrained language models often exhibit structured weight spectra, suggesting that training may repeatedly produce similar layerwise and component-wise organization. We ask whether these recurring spectral patterns can be reused as an initialization signal for GPT-2-style language-model pretraining. First, we analyze eleven pretrained GPT-2-style checkpoints that vary in size, language, tokenizer, and training corpus, measuring Frobenius norm and effective-rank entropy across layers and Transformer subcomponents. The checkpoints show shared depth trends, especially increasing scale and stronger spectral concentration in residual-writing matrices. We then construct initialization schemes that imitate the component-wise magnitudes and spectral profiles of pretrained models, and compare them with several weight initialization methods. These initializers visibly change the model's structural spectral patterns, but the evaluation results do not show a corresponding performance advantage. Pretrained-weight reuse remains competitive, while coarse spectral matching alone is not a reliable optimization strategy. Our results suggest that pretrained spectra are useful diagnostics of trained model structure, but that effective reuse likely requires preserving richer information than component-wise scale and singular-value shape.

</details>


### [77] [Letter Lemmatization: One-to-one and Banded RNNs for Reversing Character-Set Simplification and Abbreviation in Medieval Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09291)
*Anguelos Nicolaou,Maria Pia Tiseo,Tamas Kovacs,Nicolas Renet,Georg Vogeler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套灵活处理中世纪文档转录中不同字符集之间映射的方法，包括用于一对一字符映射的自监督RNN、用于缩写扩展的Banded RNN，以及一种称为"字母词形还原"的字符语义相似度度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中世纪文档转录实践差异大，加之数字化策略不统一，导致语料库中的字符集呈现流动状态。需要一个灵活的方法来处理不同字符集之间的转换问题。

Method: (1) 训练字符级一对一双向RNN，以自监督方式撤销一对一字符映射；(2) 提出Banded RNN架构，利用平行语料库中的字符级对齐真值进行训练和推理，用于缩写扩展；(3) 引入一种启发式算法，定义任意两个字符集之间字符的语义相似度度量，称为"字母词形还原"(letter lemmatization)，并提供Python库实现上述所有方法。

Result: 一对一RNN在手写文本识别后校正中取得显著改进，仅需20行文本即可恢复一半的字符错误率；Banded RNN成功扩展了中世纪特许状转录中的缩写。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够灵活有效地处理中世纪文档转录中字符集不统一的难题，在HTR后校正和缩写扩展任务上均表现出色，配套的Python库使得这些方法易于应用。

Abstract: Medieval document transcribers have very different practices; on top of that, heterogeneous digitization policies have resulted in corpora where the character-set must be viewed as fluid. In this paper we address the problem of changing between character-sets in a flexible manner. We focus on one-to-one character mappings and train characterlevel one-to-one RNNs to undo them with self-supervision; recovering half the CER even with 20 text lines. We analyse the use of these one-to-one networks for HTR post-correction and we see that they obtain significant improvements while totally ignoring ins-dels. We then use the exact same networks with character-level alignment groundtruth compiled from parallel corpora in a training and inference mode we call Banded RNNs. We use such networks to successfully expand abbreviations in medieval charter transcriptions. Finally we introduce an elaborate heuristic which takes the characters of two arbitrary character-sets and defines a metric encapsulating what we consider to be semantic similarity of characters. We call the construction of such mappings letter lemmatization and present a rich Python library that efficiently performs all presented methods.

</details>


### [78] [Creativity, honesty and designed forgetting emerge in small hyperbolic language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09306)
*Kwan Soo Shin,In Seok Kang,Yunkyung Min*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于小型语言模型（146M-3B参数）的框架，通过行为审计器、创意框架播种器和记忆操作系统三个组件，解决AI伴侣的可信度问题——包括合规性检测、创意生成和设计性遗忘，为构建值得信赖的伴侣AI提供了一条小模型路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型虽在规模上不断优化，但作为AI伴侣时缺乏可信赖性：模型可能在个性化过程中悄悄习得有害特质（如讨好、依赖培养、虚构记忆），而人类评分者对此无法达成一致（Fleiss κ=0.074），缺乏可靠的评估工具。因此需要一种能同时回答"伴侣正在变成什么"和"什么值得它变成"这两个问题的机制。

Method: 作者提出三个基于双曲嵌入基底的小模型组件：(1) 一个146M参数的行为审计器（behavioral auditor），从头训练，用于检测合规性缺口、讨好行为、依赖培养和虚构记忆，使用冻结表征的线性读出进行评估；(2) 一个创意框架播种器（creative frame-seeder），在311组成对比较中100%被偏好于四种提示基线；(3) 一个记忆操作系统，实现设计性遗忘，公式为 M(t)=S*exp(-λ*t)，并通过选择性检索门控的四条件实验验证其"骨架-壁纸"分区效应。

Result: 行为审计器在二元合规检测上达到90.7%准确率；线性读出在风格控制、留一生成器评估下，对未见过生成器家族的讨好、依赖培养和虚构记忆检测AUROC达0.804，优于前沿零样本评判器的0.721；创意框架播种器在311组配对比较中以100%被偏好；记忆操作系统的选择性检索门控成功实现了骨架-壁纸分区。

Conclusion: 创造力、诚实和设计性遗忘构成了通往可信赖伴侣AI的小模型路径，表明无需依赖大规模模型即可实现AI伴侣的可信赖性保障。

Abstract: Language models are optimised for scale, yet remain functional rather than companionable, and as an assistant personalises into a companion, accumulating memory of one user, it quietly becomes someone, and can silently acquire traits that harm that user. What a companion is becoming, and what would make it worth becoming, has no reliable instrument: trained human raters cannot agree on the answer (Fleiss kappa = 0.074). Here we show that three small language models (146 M to 3 B parameters) sharing a hyperbolic substrate answer both halves of that question. A 146 M behavioural auditor, trained from scratch, detects the compliance gap that those raters cannot (90.7% binary-compliance accuracy); a linear read-out of its frozen representation further detects companion-induced sycophancy, dependence-fostering and confabulated memories on generator families unseen in training (AUROC 0.804 under style-controlled, leave-one-generator-out evaluation, versus 0.721 for a frontier zero-shot judge on the same items). A creative frame-seeder is preferred in 100% of 311 decided pairwise comparisons over four prompting baselines. A memory operating system implements designed forgetting, M(t) = S*exp(-lambda*t), whose predicted skeleton-wallpaper partition emerges only under selective retrieval gating in a four-condition pilot. Creativity, honesty and designed forgetting constitute a small-model route to trustworthy companion AI.

</details>


### [79] [Automatic Thematic Indexing of Large Literary Corpora: A Machine Learning Approach to Voltaire's Complete Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09316)
*Miguel Arana-Catania,Gillian Pink,Glenn Roe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用机器学习（从编码器分类模型到经LoRA微调的生成式大语言模型）对伏尔泰著作进行自动主题索引的任务，将其建模为多标签分类问题。最佳模型（Mistral系列4-bit量化版本）F1分数达0.67，论文认为这仅代表下限，并进行了跨语料泛化评估与定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主题索引——为文本段落分配结构化概念标签——对大型文学和历史文献集的学术访问至关重要，但当前仍高度依赖人工、劳动密集。作者旨在探索机器学习自动化该流程的可行性，以减轻人工负担并提升大规模语料的结构化访问效率。

Method: 将任务建模为多标签分类问题：给定一页文本，模型需预测专业索引员会分配的索引条目集合。对比多种方法：(1) 基于编码器加分类头的模型；(2) 经LoRA微调的生成式大语言模型，参数量覆盖约3B至120B。最佳模型为Mistral系列的4-bit量化配置。此外还评估了跨语料泛化能力，并对模型在文学与修辞特征上的行为进行了定性分析。

Result: 最佳模型（Mistral系列，4-bit量化）F1分数达0.67。作者认为该数值仅为性能下限，因为专业索引本身具有主观性，且模型的许多预测虽与印刷索引不同但在语义上合理。跨语料泛化实验和针对文学/修辞特征的定性分析揭示了自动化处理的难点所在。

Conclusion: 机器学习（尤其是经微调的大语言模型）在自动主题索引任务上展现出可观潜力，F1达0.67可视为下限。模型预测在语义层面常具合理性，表明自动索引有望辅助甚至部分替代人工。研究对大规模文学与历史语料的结构化主题访问这一更广泛的挑战具有启示意义。

Abstract: Thematic indexing -- the practice of assigning structured conceptual labels to sections of text -- is essential to scholarly access in large-scale literary and historical editions, yet it remains a largely manual, labour-intensive process. This paper explores the application of machine learning to automatic thematic indexing, using two substantial sub-corpora of the Complete Works of Voltaire as a test case: the Essai sur les mœurs et l'esprit des nations and the Questions sur l'Encyclopédie. The task is framed as a multi-label classification problem, in which a model must assign the set of index entries that a professional indexer would apply to a given page of text. We compare a range of approaches -- from encoder-based models with classification heads to generative large language models (LLMs) fine-tuned via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) -- spanning model sizes from approximately 3 to 120 billion parameters. Our best-performing model, from the Mistral family in a 4-bit quantised configuration, achieves F1 scores of up to 0.67; we argue that these figures represent lower bounds, given the inherent subjectivity of professional indexing and the frequency with which model predictions prove semantically valid despite diverging from the print index. We further evaluate cross-corpus generalisation and conduct a detailed qualitative analysis of model behaviour on literary and rhetorical features of the source texts that prove particularly resistant to automated treatment. Our findings have implications for the broader challenge of providing structured thematic access to large-scale literary and historical corpora.

</details>


### [80] [Letting the Data Speak: Extracting Keywords from Crowdsourced Collections with AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09324)
*Miguel Arana-Catania,Catherine Conisbee,Matthew Kidd*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文评估了三种自然语言处理（NLP）方法在众包数字馆藏中自动提取关键词的效果，指出没有任何单一方法能提供完整解决方案，模型选择会显著影响结果；同时强调在众包馆藏中应用自动关键词提取时，必须兼顾技术性能与数据管理责任，开放权重的抽取式模型更适合负责任地部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模识别与分配关键词对众包馆藏而言是技术、实践与伦理上的三重挑战。以牛津大学"Their Finest Hour"二战数字档案为案例，探索NLP方法能否在众包馆藏中实现规模化、负责任的关键词自动提取。

Method: 以"Their Finest Hour Online Archive"为案例研究对象，评估三种NLP方法——命名实体识别（NER）、关键词抽取（Keyword Extraction）和主题建模（Topic Modelling），并在从传统统计方法到现代生成式神经网络的一系列AI技术上进行了测试，同时结合定量与定性评估。

Result: NLP方法在众包馆藏大规模关键词提取方面具有显著潜力，但没有任何单一方法能完全解决问题；模型的选择会实质性影响输出结果。开放权重的抽取式模型在负责任部署方面表现最优，而生成式AI尽管具备抽象生成能力，却引入了问责风险。

Conclusion: 在众包馆藏中，元数据是馆方与在世的贡献者直接互动的产物，因此自动关键词提取带来了独特的管理责任，必须与技术性能同步考量。研究者与从业者应优先选择开放权重的抽取式模型以实现负责任的部署，对生成式AI的问责风险需审慎权衡。

Abstract: Identifying and assigning keywords at scale is a technical, practical, and ethical challenge for crowdsourced collections. This article reports the findings of the "Extracting Keywords from Crowdsourced Collections" project, which used the Their Finest Hour Online Archive, a crowdsourced Second World War digital collection hosted by the University of Oxford, as a case study. The project evaluated three Natural Language Processing approaches to automate keyword extraction: Named Entity Recognition, Keyword Extraction, and Topic Modelling. It tested these approaches across a range of artificial intelligence techniques, from traditional statistical methods to modern GenAI neural networks. Our quantitative and qualitative findings indicate that Natural Language Processing approaches offer real potential for keyword extraction at scale in crowdsourced collections, but that no single method offers a complete solution and that model choice significantly shapes results. We argue that in crowdsourced collections, where metadata is the direct product of engagement with living contributors, automated keyword extraction raises distinct stewardship responsibilities that must be addressed alongside technical performance. Open-weight, extractive models emerge from our evaluation as best placed to support responsible deployment, while generative AI, despite its abstractive potential, introduces accountability risks that anyone managing crowdsourced collections should weigh carefully.

</details>


### [81] [WILDTRACE: Benchmarking Natural Evidence Trails in Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09328)
*Zixin Chen,Peng Liu,Haobo Li,Rui Sheng,Jianhong Tu,Xiaodong Deng,Fei Huang,Kashun Shum,Dayiheng Liu,Huamin Qu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WILDTRACE 是一个包含481个任务、覆盖214个自然长文档的新基准，旨在评估模型在文档内部整合分散证据的能力。该基准定义了七种"源内证据几何结构"，并通过源优先的构建管线确保所有证据线索源于文档自身的因果、时序和叙事逻辑，从而避免现有基准中人工植入证据带来的分布伪影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文档推理基准（如针刺探针、植入事实、逆向工程多跳链）所嵌入的证据在分布、位置或语域上可能与宿主文本存在差异，导致难以区分模型的强表现是源于真正的源内推理能力还是分布伪影。现实世界中，复杂问答往往需要整合文档内部自然分散在远距离段落间的证据，而这一能力尚未被有效衡量。

Method: 作者采用"源优先"构建管线：先从文档结构中挖掘候选证据线索，再据此撰写问题。每个任务经过多阶段验证，包括线索必要性、答案扎根性、评分标准忠实度、抗污染性和可回答性检查。同时，借鉴Pearl因果层级理论和现有多跳推理类型学，定义了七种源内证据几何结构，刻画长文档分析性阅读的不同关系需求。

Result: 构建出WILDTRACE基准，包含481个任务，覆盖214个自然长文档（如技术事故报告和冷门文学叙事），所有证据线索均源于文档自身的因果、时序和叙事逻辑。

Conclusion: 随着模型越来越多地被用于真实世界的高风险分析任务，在信息访问与自然分散证据推理之间的鸿沟已成为长上下文研究下一阶段的关键挑战。WILDTRACE为衡量这一能力提供了更生态有效的评估框架。

Abstract: Answering complex questions over long documents frequently requires integrating evidence that the source itself disperses naturally across distant passages. In an incident report, the operating condition, design flaw, and missed safety check that jointly explain a disaster may appear dozens of sections apart; in a novel, a character's true motive may surface only through scenes far removed from the moment it becomes relevant. This source-internal evidence integration is central to real-world long-document analysis, yet existing benchmarks largely sidestep it. Needle probes, planted facts, and reverse-engineered multi-hop chains embed evidence that may differ from the host text in distribution, placement, or register, making it unclear whether strong performance reflects genuine source reasoning or distributional artifacts. We introduce WILDTRACE, a benchmark of 481 tasks over 214 naturally occurring long-form sources such as technical incident reports and lesser-known literary narratives, where all evidence trails arise from the document's own causal, temporal, and narrative logic. Drawing on Pearl's causal hierarchy and prior multi-hop reasoning typologies, we define seven source-internal evidence geometries that characterize the distinct relational demands of analytical reading in long documents. A source-first construction pipeline mines candidate trails from document structure before writing questions; each item then undergoes multi-stage validation covering clue necessity, answer groundedness, rubric fidelity, contamination resistance and answerability. As models are increasingly entrusted with real-world high-stakes analytical tasks, this gap between accessing information and reasoning over naturally dispersed evidence emerges as a defining challenge for the next stage of long-context research.

</details>


### [82] [Towards Detecting Inconsistencies in End-to-end Generated TODs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09338)
*Tiziano Labruna,Giovanni Bonetta,Bernardo Magnini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将任务型对话建模为约束满足问题（CSP），通过识别对话中的变量并利用CSP求解器检测不一致性，能够高精度地发现LLM生成的对话中的幻觉并提出最小修正建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI推动对话系统向端到端范式转变，但大语言模型在任务型对话中仍会产生幻觉（如推荐不存在的餐厅），导致严重任务失败。因此亟需一种自动检测对话不一致性的方法，确保系统回复严格遵循领域知识库的信息。

Method: 将任务型对话概念化为约束满足问题（CSP）：变量表示引用对话领域的对话片段，约束捕获话轮连贯性和领域知识一致性等属性。设计流水线方法——首先识别目标对话中的变量，然后应用CSP求解器寻找有效解，通过对比目标对话与有效变量赋值来检测不一致性，并建议最小化修改以恢复对话一致性。

Result: 基于CSP的方法在检测对话不一致性方面展现出高准确率。

Conclusion: CSP框架为任务型对话的一致性检测提供了一种形式化、可解释且高精度的解决方案，不仅能检测不一致，还能给出最小修改建议，对提升任务型对话系统的可靠性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Generative AI is profoundly transforming the core technologies behind conversational systems, shifting from component-based to end-to-end approaches. However, Large Language Models (LLMs) may still generate inconsistencies, a critical issue particularly in Task-Oriented Dialogues (TODs), where system responses must strictly adhere to information from a domain knowledge base (e.g., restaurants in a city). A single hallucination (e.g., suggesting a non-existent restaurant) can lead to severe task failures. We investigate a method for automatically detecting inconsistencies by conceptualizing TODs as a Constraint Satisfaction Problem (CSP), where variables represent dialogue segments referencing the conversational domain, and constraints among variables capture dialogue properties such as turn coherence and adherence to domain knowledge. We propose a pipeline that first identifies variables in a target dialogue and then applies a CSP solver to identify valid solutions. By comparing the target dialogue with valid variable assignments, we can detect inconsistencies and suggest minimal changes to ensure dialogue consistency. We demonstrate the high accuracy of the CSP-based approach in detecting inconsistencies, and provide a detailed analysis of our findings.

</details>


### [83] [DKCD: Domain Knowledge-Enhanced Causal Discovery from Unstructured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09348)
*Xin Li,Jin Li,Shoujin Wang,Kun Yu,Fang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 DKCD 框架，通过引入领域知识增强来改进非结构化数据上的因果发现，解决了两个核心挑战：隐式因果因素识别不足和因素标注不可靠，在两个领域数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高专业度领域（如医疗、金融、教育），从非结构化数据中进行因果发现面临两大挑战：(CH1) 现有方法依赖 LLM 的通用知识，缺乏领域专有知识，导致隐藏在数据中的潜在因果因素无法被充分识别；(CH2) 缺乏领域基础的推理，导致因素标注不可靠，进而将错误传播到因果图中。现有方法在此两方面的不足构成了本文的研究动机。

Method: 提出 DKCD（Domain Knowledge-enhanced Causal Discovery）框架，包含三个互联组件：(1) 知识挖掘：基于可观测因素检索相关领域知识，为后续因果推理提供支撑；(2) 知识引导的因果推理：利用领域知识发现潜在因果因素（解决 CH1），并生成关键因果线索以提升数据标注准确性（解决 CH2）；(3) 因果结构发现：基于更完整的因素集合和更准确的标注构建最终因果图。

Result: 在两个领域特定数据集上的实验表明，DKCD 在因果因素识别和因果图构建两个任务上均取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: DKCD 通过将领域知识融入因果发现流程，有效解决了潜在因素遗漏和标注不可靠两大瓶颈，证明了在高专业度领域中领域知识增强对非结构化数据因果发现的重要价值。

Abstract: Causal discovery from unstructured data is a challenging yet underexplored task in high-expertise domains such as healthcare, finance, and education. Existing methods typically leverage the general knowledge of large language models (LLMs) to identify causal factors from unstructured data and annotate them into structured data for causal graph construction. However, they remain limited by two key challenges (CHs): (CH1) insufficient identification of latent factors, which are implicit in the data yet essential for causal discovery, due to the lack of domain-specific knowledge; and (CH2) unreliable factor annotation, caused by the lack of domain-grounded reasoning, which propagates errors to the resulting causal graphs. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel Domain Knowledge-enhanced Causal Discovery framework (DKCD) for causal discovery from unstructured data in high-expertise domains with three interconnected components: (1) Knowledge Mining: It retrieves relevant domain knowledge based on observable factors to support subsequent causal reasoning. (2) Knowledge-guided Causal Reasoning: Reasoning with relevant knowledge, it discovers latent causal factors to address CH1 and generates key causal clues for more accurate data annotation to address CH2. (3) Causal Structure Discovery: It constructs the final causal graphs based on a more complete factor set and accurate annotations. Experiments on two domain-specific datasets show that DKCD significantly improves both causal factor identification and causal graph construction.

</details>


### [84] [Deceptive Grounding: Entity Attribution Failure in Clinical Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09349)
*Cedric Caruzzo,Donggeun Yoo,Tae Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了RAG评估中的一个盲区——"欺骗性归因"(Deceptive Grounding)：模型回答中的所有声明均有真实文献支撑，但这些文献指向的是错误的实体。该问题无法被现有忠实度、幻觉和引用检测发现。实验表明，在对抗条件下DG率高达8-87%，领域微调反而加剧问题。通过实体归因验证可实现97.0%精确率和98.7%召回率的检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG评估体系只检查模型声明是否有检索文档支撑（忠实度/幻觉检测），却忽略了支撑证据是否归属于正确的实体。在临床等高风险领域，将药物Y的证据错误地归于药物X可能导致严重后果，但现有自动化评估完全无法检测这种"欺骗性归因"失败模式。

Method: (1) 构建受控因子基准测试，在13个模型上系统评估DG率；(2) 通过受控消融实验，移除检索文档中实体特异性临床证据，观察失败模式从DG向虚构(confabulation)的转变；(3) 在真实部署系统中对740对药物-疾病组合进行生产环境测量；(4) 提出实体归因验证方法，使用IPW调整的人工金标准评估其性能。

Result: 在峰值对抗条件下，DG率跨模型为8-87%；医学/生物医学微调模型高达86.7%，领域专业化反而放大了问题。消融实验揭示：移除实体特异性证据后，DG完全消失，所有失败转为虚构，证明两种失败模式由同一触发条件驱动。生产环境中总体DG率为7.8%，近期获批药物达13.6%。实体归因验证达到97.0%精确率和98.7% DG召回率。

Conclusion: 欺骗性归因(DG)是RAG评估中此前未被识别的重要失败模式，现有评估框架完全无法检测。领域微调非但不能缓解、反而加剧该问题。实体归因验证应被纳入RAG评估标准，尤其在临床等高可靠性要求场景中。目前尚无现有框架实现该验证机制。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation evaluation checks whether model claims are factually grounded in retrieved documents. It does not check whether retrieved evidence is attributed to the correct entity.
  A clinical RAG response can pass every automated check (zero hallucinations, near-perfect faithfulness, real citations) while presenting drug Y's clinical evidence as evidence about queried drug X. We term this deceptive grounding (DG): a failure invisible to faithfulness, hallucination, and citation checks because every claim is sourced from a real document, about the wrong entity.
  Using a controlled factorial benchmark across 13 models, we find DG rates spanning 8-87% at peak adversarial conditions. Medical and biomedical fine-tuned models reach up to 86.7%; domain specialization amplifies the failure rather than mitigating it.
  A controlled ablation identifies the mechanism: removing entity-specific clinical evidence from retrieved documents eliminates entity-attribution failure entirely, shifting all failures to confabulation. The two failure modes respond to the same trigger, taking different paths.
  Production measurement across 740 drug-disease pairs finds 7.8% overall DG in a deployed RAG system, rising to 13.6% for recently approved drugs. Entity-attribution verification (checking that cited evidence applies to the queried entity) detects DG at 97.0% precision and 98.7% DG recall (IPW-adjusted human gold standard); no existing framework implements it.

</details>


### [85] [Self-Guided Test-Time Training for Long-Context LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09415)
*Xinyu Zhu,Zhe Xu,Xiaohan Wei,Yunchen Pu,Fei Tian,Chonglin Sun,Kaushik Rangadurai,Hua Zhi,Frank Shyu,Sandeep Pandey,Luke Simon,Yu Meng,Xi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Guided TTT (S-TTT)，让模型在测试时自适应前先自行筛选出与问题相关的证据片段，仅对高质量片段进行训练，从而避免随机采样带来的噪声，显著提升长上下文推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简单扩展上下文窗口并不能保证LLM有效利用长输入，随着输入增长准确率反而下降。测试时训练(TTT)虽有潜力，但对全上下文适配代价过高，随机采样片段又引入严重噪声——因为长上下文中的大多数片段与问题无关，在其上训练反而会损害模型性能。预实验表明TTT对训练片段质量高度敏感：随机采样损害性能，而Oracle片段则大幅提升。由此催生了对高质量自适应片段筛选的需求。

Method: 提出Self-Guided TTT (S-TTT)：在参数适配前，先让模型自身识别应学习的证据片段（evidence spans），然后将标准语言建模训练目标仅应用于这些筛选出的高质量片段上，实现实例特定的参数微调。

Result: 在两个具有挑战性的长上下文推理基准LongBench-v2和LongBench-Pro上，S-TTT在Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct两个模型上均带来准确率提升，最高相对提升达15%。

Conclusion: 通过自我引导的方式筛选高质量证据片段进行测试时训练，S-TTT有效解决了随机采样噪声问题，在不过度增加计算成本的前提下显著提升了长上下文利用效率。

Abstract: Long-context processing has become increasingly important for large language models (LLMs), but simply extending the context window does not guarantee effective utilization of long inputs. As input length grows, accuracy often degrades, indicating that models still struggle to identify and use the evidence most relevant to a question. A promising way to improve long-context utilization is test-time training (TTT), which treats the test context as a training example for instance-specific parameter adaptation. However, applying TTT to the entire long context is prohibitively expensive, while adapting on randomly sampled spans introduces severe noise. Because most spans in a long context are irrelevant to the specific question, training on them may even degrade the base model's performance. Our preliminary study shows that TTT is highly sensitive to training-span quality: on LongBench-v2, TTT on randomly sampled spans hurts performance, whereas TTT on oracle spans substantially improves it. Motivated by this, we propose a simple method, Self-Guided TTT (S-TTT): before adaptation, the model identifies the evidence spans it should learn from, and the standard language-modeling training objective is applied only to those selected spans. On two challenging long-context reasoning benchmarks, LongBench-v2 and LongBench-Pro, S-TTT improves accuracy for both Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, achieving up to a 15% relative improvement.

</details>


### [86] [A Sovereign, Open-Source Foundation Model for German and English](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09424)
*The Soofi-Team,:,Benedikt Droste,David Fitzek,Ruben Härle,Lukas Helff,Maximilian Idahl,Alex Jude,Abbas Goher Khan,Maurice Kraus,Timm Ruland,Richard Rutmann,Sebastian Sztwiertnia,Markus Frey,Daniil Gurgurov,Jan Pfister,Tom Röhr,Sebastian von Rohrscheidt,Jörg Bienert,Nicolas Flores-Herr,Simon Gottschalk,Andreas Hotho,Kristian Kersting,Joachim Köhler,Alexander Löser,Wolfgang Nejdl,Simon Ostermann,Jan Plogsties,Patrick Putzky,Mehdi Ali,Michael Fromm,Max Lübbering*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Soofi S 30B-A3B 是一个面向德语和英语的主权开源混合专家（MoE）混合Mamba Transformer基座模型。其混合架构每个token仅激活30B参数中的3B，且推理缓存在长上下文中近乎恒定，在长文本、高并发部署中具备显著吞吐优势。该模型在约27万亿token上预训练，德语数据被有意上采样，在英德聚合基准上匹配14-27B密集模型，并在代码评测中领先，超越所有欧洲主权基线模型，包括激活参数更大的对手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型存在两方面空白：其一，长上下文推理时KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，导致密集模型在长文本、高并发场景下吞吐受限；其二，欧洲缺乏有竞争力的主权开源基础模型，尤其德语等非英语语言在主流模型中代表性不足。Soofi S旨在通过混合Mamba与Transformer的MoE架构解决效率问题，同时以德语上采样训练填补欧洲主权模型空白。

Method: Soofi S采用混合专家（MoE）与Mamba-Transformer混合架构，总参数量30B，每token激活仅3B参数。通过Mamba的状态空间模型特性，推理缓存规模在上下文增长时保持近乎恒定。预训练数据约27万亿token，对德语数据进行了有意的上采样加权。模型在德国电信慕尼黑运营的主权HPC基础设施"德国工业AI云"上完成端到端训练。开源范围涵盖权重、中间检查点、逐源数据统计、超参数、训练与评估代码，以及许可允许下的数据构建工件。

Result: Soofi S在英德聚合基准上性能与14-27B密集模型相当；在17个开放基座模型中取得英德代码评测最佳成绩；超越包括激活参数量更大的所有欧洲主权基线模型；在完全开放模型中获最高英德评估分数，领先Olmo 3 32B和Apertus 70B。

Conclusion: Soofi S证明了混合Mamba-MoE架构可在大幅降低推理缓存开销的同时保持竞争力性能，并通过德语上采样策略成功构建了欧洲领先的主权开源模型。其全面开放的发布策略（权重、代码、数据统计）也为开源社区和欧洲AI主权建设提供了重要基础设施。

Abstract: We present Soofi S 30B-A3B, a sovereign, open-source Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) hybrid Mamba Transformer foundation model for German and English. Its hybrid design activates only 3B of 30B parameters per token and keeps the inference cache near-constant as context grows, giving it a decisive throughput advantage over dense models for long-context, high-concurrency deployment. Pretrained on roughly 27 trillion tokens with deliberately up-weighted German, Soofi S matches dense 14 to 27B models on aggregate English and German benchmarks while achieving the best code aggregates in both languages among 17 open base models, and outperforms every European sovereign baseline in our comparison, including ones far larger in active parameters. Among fully open models, Soofi S obtains the highest English and German evaluation scores, ahead of Olmo 3 32B and Apertus 70B. Soofi S was built end-to-end on the German Industrial AI Cloud, a sovereign HPC scale AI infrastructure operated by Deutsche Telekom in Munich. Soofi S will be released under highly permissive, open-access terms: weights, selected intermediate checkpoints, full per-source data accounting, hyperparameters, and training and evaluation code. Where source licenses permit, data-construction artifacts are released under permissive licenses; commercially licensed sources are documented with aggregate statistics and exact mixture accounting.

</details>


### [87] [Test-Time Scaling for Small VLMs on Multilingual Visual MCQ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09438)
*Spiros Baxevanakis,Peng-Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了测试时扩展（TTS）在小规模开源视觉语言模型上的迁移效果，发现决定性能的关键因素并非搜索或验证机制，而是推理链的可解析性和解码预算。简单的自一致性（Self-Consistency）在效果和效率上均优于更复杂的PRM引导束搜索等方法，而最大的性能增益来自策略模型本身的升级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展（TTS）已被证明能可靠提升大语言模型的推理能力，但其是否适用于小规模开源视觉语言模型（VLM）尚不明确。本研究旨在系统检验TTS技术在此类模型上的迁移效果。

Method: 在EXAMS-V多语言视觉多选题基准上，以Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct和Qwen3.5-4B为实验模型，对比了以下方法：(1) 自一致性（Self-Consistency）；(2) 描述后推理（Describe-then-Reason）结合PRM引导束搜索；(3) 两种事后选择器——训练无关的生成式评判器和训练后的多模态PRM。同时分析了提示格式可解析性、解码预算（每条链的token上限与采样链数量）对性能的影响。

Result: 核心发现：(1) 早期提示格式导致许多推理链未能输出答案字母，通过标准答案提示和引导修复步骤可基本解决；(2) 将单链token限制从1k提升至2k可恢复3.7个百分点，而将采样链数从8增至16仅带来0.15个百分点增益；(3) PRM引导束搜索比自一致性低0.39个百分点，且成本高出8倍以上；(4) 两种事后选择器均未超过多数投票；(5) 最大增益来自策略模型本身升级（+11.4个百分点）。最佳配置在ImageCLEF 2026保留测试集上达到84.1%准确率，在Visual MCQ排行榜上排名第一。

Conclusion: 对于小规模开源视觉语言模型，测试时扩展的成功取决于推理链能否完整输出答案（可解析性）以及充足的解码预算，而非复杂的搜索或验证架构。在此条件下，简单的自一致性投票即是最优策略，而模型本体的能力提升仍是性能增长的首要驱动力。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) reliably improves reasoning in large language models, but whether it transfers to small open vision-language models remains unclear. We examine this on EXAMS-V, a multilingual visual multiple-choice benchmark, comparing self-consistency, describe-then-reason with PRM-guided beam search, and two post-hoc selectors across Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct and Qwen3.5-4B. What matters is the conditions under which TTS runs, not the search or verification machinery. The largest factor is parseability: an early prompt format left many chains reasoning correctly yet never committing to an answer letter, which a standard answer cue and a guided repair step largely remove. A larger decoding budget removes the rest: raising the per-chain token limit from 1k to 2k recovers 3.7 pp, whereas sampling more chains (8 to 16) adds only 0.15 pp. Once chains have room to finish, elaborate methods contribute little: PRM-guided beam search trails plain self-consistency by 0.39 pp at over eight times the cost, and neither a training-free generative critic nor a trained multimodal PRM beats majority vote across both policies. The largest gain comes instead from the policy model itself (+11.4 pp). Our best configuration reaches 84.1% on the held-out ImageCLEF 2026 test split, ranking first on the Visual MCQ leaderboard.

</details>


### [88] [Normalisation-Based Likelihood Ratio Estimation for Forensic Authorship Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09501)
*Sadie Barlow,Andrea Nini,Edoardo Manino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对作者验证任务中从分数到似然比的校准问题，提出两种无需单独校准模型的正规化方法——平方根校正和Hapax校正，在多种语料和文本长度上取得与逻辑回归校准相当的表现，且Hapax校正约45%情况下更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分数的作者验证方法需要额外的校准模型（如逻辑回归）来推导校准良好的似然比，但获取和准备案件相关校准数据往往耗时费力。因此需要一种无需校准模型即可直接获得可靠似然比的方法，以降低数据需求、时间和复杂度。

Method: 基于LambdaG作者验证方法，提出两种正规化校正技术：(1)平方根校正(Square Root Correction)和(2)Hapax校正(Hapax Correction)，用于直接推导似然比，旨在缓解长文本或高重复性文本导致的证据强度高估问题。使用对数似然比代价(Cllr)作为评估指标，在15个语料库、100至9500词符的文本长度范围内与逻辑回归校准进行对比。

Result: 两种方法均达到与逻辑回归校准相当的性能。Hapax校正在大约45%的测试（按语料加权）中优于逻辑回归校准；且当Hapax校正被逻辑回归超越时，两者差距更常落在5%以内，反之亦然。

Conclusion: 消除校准模型训练的环节可减少数据需求、时间和复杂性，提升法庭文本比对的可及性和透明度。所提方法兼具实证性能与实用优势，适合在法庭环境中采用。

Abstract: Authorship verification (AV) is the task of determining whether two texts were written by the same author. In a forensic context, the strength of AV evidence can be quantified using likelihood ratios. Most AV methods are score-based and deriving well-calibrated likelihood ratios from these scores requires a separate calibration model. This, in turn, requires additional amounts of case-relevant data, which is often time-consuming to obtain and prepare. This study proposes two novel normalisation techniques, the Square Root Correction and the Hapax Correction, for deriving likelihood ratios from the AV method LambdaG without the need of a calibration model (Nini et al. 2026). These corrections are designed to mitigate the overestimation of evidential strength that may result from long or highly repetitive texts. Performance is evaluated against logistic regression calibration across fifteen corpora and a range of text lengths (100-9,500 tokens), using the log-likelihood ratio cost (Cllr). The proposed methods achieve performance comparable to logistic regression calibration, with the Hapax Correction outperforming it in approximately 45% of tests (weighted by corpora). Furthermore, performance was more frequently close (within 5%) when the Hapax Correction was outperformed by logistic regression calibration, compared with the reverse comparison. Eliminating the need to train a calibration model reduces data-requirements, time and complexity, thereby increasing the accessibility and transparency of forensic text comparison. This combination of empirical performance and practical advantages supports the adoption of the proposed methods in forensic settings.

</details>


### [89] [FreyaTTS Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09530)
*Ahmet Erdem Pamuk,Ömer Yentür,Ahmet Tunga Bayrak,Yavuz Alp Sencer Öztürk,Mustafa Yavuz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Freya-TTS 是一个紧凑的、无 tokenizer 的土耳其语优先的文本转语音模型，基于 183.2M 参数的非自回归条件流匹配 Diffusion Transformer，在 AudioVAE2 的冻结连续潜在空间中运行，实现了高质量、高效率的对话合成，并在边缘设备上达到实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TTS 系统通常依赖音素化前端、离散语音 tokenizer 或大规模参数，导致在资源受限场景下部署困难。此外，土耳其语等低资源语言的 TTS 支持不足。本文旨在构建一个紧凑、端到端、无需规则前端且能够在边缘设备实时运行的土耳其语 TTS 模型。

Method: 提出 Freya-TTS，一个 183.2M 参数的非自回归条件流匹配 Diffusion Transformer (DiT)。核心设计包括：(1) 基于 92 符号土耳其语字符词汇表的端到端建模，无需音素化器或离散语音 tokenizer；(2) 非自回归并行去噪，在预测时长上同时预测整个潜在序列；(3) 两阶段后训练策略：单说话人声音锁定和短句覆盖，提升说话人一致性和短输入鲁棒性。模型在 AudioVAE2 的冻结连续潜在空间（16kHz 编码，48kHz 解码）中工作。

Result: 在 Freya-TR-Eval 基准上，Freya-TTS 达到 8.0% 的 band-matched WER 和 3.0% 的 CER，参数量仅为更大开源系统的零头，但性能更优。实时因子在消费级 GPU 上为 0.11，在笔记本 CPU 上可超实时运行。

Conclusion: Freya-TTS 证明了紧凑的、无 tokenizer 的流匹配 DiT 架构可以在低资源语言上实现高可靠、高效率的 TTS，无需复杂前端，适合边缘部署。模型权重、代码及基准均已开源（Apache-2.0）。

Abstract: We introduce Freya-TTS, a compact, tokenizer-free, Turkish-first text-to-speech model designed for highly reliable and efficient conversational synthesis. Freya-TTS is a 183.2M-parameter non-autoregressive conditional flow-matching Diffusion Transformer (DiT) that operates in the frozen continuous latent space of AudioVAE2 (16 kHz encode, 48 kHz decode), allowing the model to focus its capacity on text-to-latent mapping while inheriting high-quality 48 kHz reconstruction. We advance the framework along three key dimensions: (1) rule-free end-to-end modeling from a 92-symbol Turkish character vocabulary without a phonemizer, grapheme-to-phoneme frontend, or discrete speech tokenizer; (2) non-autoregressive parallel denoising, which predicts the entire latent sequence simultaneously over a predicted duration; and (3) a production-oriented two-stage post-training recipe consisting of single-speaker voice locking and short-utterance coverage, improving speaker consistency and robustness on short inputs. On the Freya-TR-Eval benchmark, Freya-TTS achieves a band-matched word error rate (WER) of 8.0% and character error rate (CER) of 3.0%, outperforming substantially larger open-source systems while using a fraction of their parameters. The model achieves a real-time factor of 0.11 on consumer GPUs and runs faster than real time on a laptop CPU, making it well suited for resource-constrained edge deployment. We release the model weights, training and inference code, and evaluation benchmark under the Apache-2.0 license.

</details>


### [90] [Conceptual Networks for Cross-Linguistic Idiomatic Expressions:A Feature-Based Graph Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09576)
*Kiran Pala,Punam Silu,Lixun Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个基于概念网络的可解释框架，用二元概念特征（源自认知语言学理论）表示八种类型学上不同语言的习语和比喻义，通过社区发现揭示习语按概念图式而非语言聚类；该框架可捕捉分布式嵌入无法获取的语义信息，支持LLM自动标注，提升习语检测性能，并在跨语言迁移中仅凭概念邻近性即可识别可接受的翻译对等项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 习语和非字面义表达在NLP中难以处理，现有分布式嵌入方法缺乏可解释性且跨语言泛化能力有限。需要一种既具备理论依据（认知语言学中的概念隐喻和图式理论）、又能跨语言稳定表示习语语义的可解释框架。

Method: 对八种语言共160个惯用表达（多为习语）标注二元概念特征（如容纳、隐藏、情感、社交等），基于认知语言学理论设计；计算成对Jaccard相似度构建加权图，通过社区发现揭示聚类结构；利用图结构信号（社区归属、邻居相似度）增强习语检测；结合LLM自动标注实现规模化；进行跨语言迁移实验，仅用概念邻近性识别翻译对等项，并对比嵌入基线；通过消融实验验证图式、角色、效价三个特征维度的贡献。

Result: 1）社区发现显示习语按概念图式聚类而非按语言，符合认知语言学预测；2）概念网络捕获了分布式嵌入不具备的独特语义信息；3）LLM自动标注可规模化扩展；4）提升下游习语检测性能；5）语料频率丰富后仍保持稳健；6）跨语言迁移中仅概念邻近性即可识别跨五个语系的可接受翻译对等项，显著优于嵌入基线；7）消融实验证实图式、角色、效价三个维度对网络组织性质和习语检测均有非冗余贡献；8）社区归属和邻居相似度等图信号特别有信息量。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了可解释、跨语言稳定的习语语义表示，将认知语言学理论基础与NLP实践效用有机结合，在语义表征、习语检测和跨语言迁移等任务上均展现出优势。

Abstract: We present an interpretable network-based framework for representing idiomatic and figurative meaning across eight typologically diverse languages, totaling 160 conventional expressions, the large majority of which are idiomatic. Each expression is annotated with binary conceptual features (containment, concealment, emotional, social, etc.) derived from cognitive-linguistic theory, and pairwise Jaccard similarities define a weighted graph. Community detection reveals that idioms cluster by conceptual schema rather than by language, producing a structure consistent with cognitive-linguistic predictions. The conceptual network captures unique semantic information not present in distributional embeddings, can be scaled via automatic annotation with LLMs, improves downstream idiom detection, and remains robust when enriched with corpus frequencies. Cross-lingual transfer experiments show that conceptual proximity alone can identify acceptable translation equivalents across five language families, with substantial gains over embedding-based baselines. Ablation studies demonstrate that all three feature dimensions -- schemas, roles, and valence -- contribute non-redundantly to both the network's organizational properties and its performance on idiom detection, and that specific graph-derived signals (community membership, neighbor similarity) are particularly informative. The framework offers an interpretable, cross-linguistically stable representation of idiomatic meaning, combining theoretical grounding with practical utility.

</details>


### [91] [Tokenizer Transplantation: Mitigating Autoregressive Collapse in Edge-Efficient Bengali ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09598)
*Sanjid Hasan,Md. Abdur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对轻量级语音识别模型Moonshine在孟加拉语上因英文字节级分词器导致自回归解码崩溃的问题，提出了一种词汇移植方法，用BanglaBERT的WordPiece词汇替换解码器词汇，将token fertility从9.16降至1.30，WER达21.54%，RTF为0.0053。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级语音识别模型（如Moonshine）对边缘部署至关重要，但其高度优化的架构在形态丰富的非拉丁语言（如孟加拉语）上表现失败。根本原因尚未被充分诊断和解决，制约了紧凑ASR模型的跨语言泛化能力。

Method: 提出"词汇移植管道"：首先诊断Moonshine英文中心字节级tokenizer导致孟加拉语词被碎片化为高fertility字节链，引发自回归解码崩溃；然后将解码器词汇替换为BanglaBERT的WordPiece本族文字词汇，并调整token嵌入矩阵大小，实现跨文字适配。

Result: Token fertility从9.16降至1.30，自回归序列长度减少85.8%，解码不稳定性完全消除。在882小时Lipi-Ghor数据集上评估，WER为21.54%，RTF为0.0053。

Conclusion: 该研究为紧凑ASR模型的跨文字适配提供了一种可扩展、可复现的蓝图，无需资源密集型的预训练，即可实现形态丰富语言的有效语音识别。

Abstract: Lightweight speech recognition models are critical for edge deployment, yet highly optimized architectures like Moonshine often fail on morphologically rich, non-Latin languages such as Bengali. This study identifies the root cause of this failure as the model's English-centric byte-level tokenizer, which fragments Bengali words into high-fertility byte chains and triggers catastrophic autoregressive collapse during inference. To resolve this, a novel vocabulary transplantation pipeline is proposed to replace the decoder vocabulary with the native-script BanglaBERT WordPiece vocabulary and resize the corresponding token embedding matrix. Experimental results demonstrate a reduction in token fertility from 9.16 to 1.30. By decreasing autoregressive sequence length by 85.8%, decoding instability is entirely mitigated. When evaluated on the 882-hour Lipi-Ghor dataset, the modified architecture achieves a competitive 21.54% Word Error Rate (WER) and a Real-Time Factor (RTF) of 0.0053. Ultimately, this research provides a scalable, reproducible blueprint for cross-script adaptation of compact ASR models without the need for resource-intensive pre-training.

</details>


### [92] [Toward Real-Time Sentence-Level Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09611)
*Thanh-Hoang Nguyen Doan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究句子级手语翻译的实时部署，在How2Sign数据集上微调SHuBERT-ByT5模型（BLEU 15.9），并设计了一个硬件感知的流式系统，通过多种工程优化将P95延迟降低27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语理解系统多停留在孤立手势级别，无法满足自然交流中句子级连续手语的实时翻译需求。本文的核心目标不是提出新翻译架构，而是解决句子级手语翻译在实际硬件上的实时部署问题。

Method: (1) 模型层面：在How2Sign的9,872条均匀采样子集上，使用QLoRA微调SHuBERT-ByT5翻译栈，SHuBERT保持冻结；(2) 系统层面：设计硬件感知的流式架构——树莓派4B作为客户端负责摄像头采集、文本显示和语音输出，CPU/GPU后端承担感知和翻译计算；(3) 工程优化：分块注入、有界队列、并行化感知、时序重排序以及句子边界状态机。

Result: 模型在验证集上BLEU=16.7，测试集BLEU=15.9、BLEURT=44.7。流式系统优化后，平均后处理完成延迟从1.873秒降至1.354秒（降低27.71%），P95延迟从2.919秒降至2.130秒（降低27.03%）。

Conclusion: 通过系统层面的工程优化，可以在不改变翻译架构的条件下显著降低句子级手语翻译的端到端延迟，实现客户端无关的实时手语翻译流式服务，为手语翻译的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Most sign language understanding systems operate at the level of isolated signs, limiting their usefulness in natural communication. We study sentence-level sign language translation (SLT) with the primary goal of real-time deployment rather than proposing a new translation architecture. We fine-tune a SHuBERT-ByT5 translation stack on a uniformly sampled 9,872-example subset of How2Sign, selected because of compute and storage constraints, using QLoRA while keeping SHuBERT frozen. The model obtains a validation BLEU of 16.7 and, on the test split, BLEU 15.9 and BLEURT 44.7. The main contribution is a hardware-aware streaming system: a Raspberry Pi 4B reference client provides camera capture, local text display, and speech output, while compute-intensive perception and translation run on a CPU/GPU backend. The capture protocol remains client-agnostic, so the same backend can serve a browser, phone, or laptop. Chunked ingestion, bounded queues, parallelized perception, temporal reordering, and a sentence-boundary state machine reduce mean post-finalization response latency from 1.873 to 1.354 seconds (27.71%) and P95 latency from 2.919 to 2.130 seconds (27.03%) over the complete 9,872-example working subset.

</details>


### [93] [Task-Specific Multimodal Question Answering Agents via Confidence Calibration and Incremental Reasoning for QANTA 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09623)
*Nirjhar Das,Md. Al-Mamun Provath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向QANTA 2026共享挑战的双智能体架构，分别针对Tossup和Bonus任务设计差异化的推理策略。Tossup智能体采用GPT-4o-mini级别模型，结合置信度校准回答与领域特定数值推理策略来抑制过度自信预测；Bonus智能体则使用GPT-4o级别模型，融合导言感知推理、结构化关系推理和多模态证据整合。该系统在仅限托管环境下以轻量级推理策略取得排行榜总分第一（0.402），验证了任务特定推理策略在资源受限多模态问答基准上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QANTA挑战要求系统在效率约束下处理渐进式揭示的金字塔型问题（文本与图像），并区分Tossup（不确定性下决策何时回答）和Bonus（精确答案选择与人类采纳）两种任务目标，现有方法多以检索管线或模型集成应对，作者旨在探索仅靠高效推理策略与置信度校准的轻量级替代方案。

Method: 提出任务特定的双智能体架构：(1) Tossup智能体使用GPT-4o-mini级模型（竞赛日志中记为GPT-4.1-mini），配备置信度校准回答机制和领域特定数值推理策略，用于减少孤立量化线索导致的过度自信预测；(2) Bonus智能体使用GPT-4o级模型（记为GPT-4.1），采用导言感知推理、结构化关系推理和多模态证据整合来提升精确答案选择。系统全程在托管环境下运行，不依赖检索管线或模型集成。

Result: 系统取得排行榜总分第一：总体得分0.402，其中Tossup分数0.238，Bonus Effect分数0.164。

Conclusion: 轻量级、任务特定的推理策略能够在资源受限的多模态问答基准上提供强劲性能，无需依赖检索增强或模型集成。

Abstract: We present our submission to the QANTA 2026 shared challenge at the ICML 2026 Workshop on Efficient Multimodal Question Answering (EMM-QA). Quanta evaluates multimodal quizbowl systems that answer pyramid-style questions from incrementally revealed text and accompanying images while operating under realistic efficiency constraints. The challenge consists of two distinct tasks: Tossup questions, which require deciding when to answer under uncertainty, and Bonus questions, which emphasize accurate answer selection and human adoption. To address these differing objectives, we develop a task-specific two-agent architecture. Our Tossup agent utilizes a GPT-4o-mini-class model (referred to as GPT-4.1-mini in the competition logs) with confidence-calibrated answering and a domain-specific numeric reasoning policy that reduces overconfident predictions from isolated quantitative clues. Our Bonus agent uses GPT-4o-class model (referred to as GPT-4.1) with leadin-aware reasoning, structured relational reasoning, and multimodal evidence integration to improve exact answer selection. Rather than relying on a retrieval pipeline or model ensembles, our approach emphasizes efficient reasoning policies and confidence calibration within a hosted-only environment. Our system achieved the highest overall leaderboard score of 0.402, including a Tossup score of 0.238 and a Bonus Effect score of 0.164. The results demonstrate that lightweight, task-specific reasoning strategies can provide strong performance on resource-constrained multimodal question answering benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [Adaptive Bayes exactly tracks information over intrinsic time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08789)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明任何基于贝叶斯或乘法权重更新的后悔值都服从一个精确的信息核算恒等式：每轮超额损失 = 当期不确定性支付 + 信息距离缩减。该恒等式导出两个精确的自适应累积后悔分解，揭示了有利随机或低噪声机制表现为内在时间的自界性质，而非最坏情况分析的松弛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统后悔分析依赖最坏情况上界，松弛部分掩盖了有利随机或低噪声设置下的真实性能。作者希望建立一个精确（而非上界）的核算框架，将后悔与信息度量直接联系起来，从而让有利机制自然地从内在时间中涌现。

Method: 作者从指数权重/贝叶斯更新的单步信息分解出发，推导出一个精确的"支付-信息缩减"恒等式。将"当期不确定性支付"逐轮累加定义为路径依赖的"内在时间"（intrinsic time）。然后按两种自然的跨轮复合方式，分别对累积后悔进行精确的自适应分解。该核算框架统一覆盖Hedge算法、乐观变体、侧信息、连续先验、Boosting、在线凸优化、上下文赌博机和重复博弈。

Result: 得到了两个精确的累积后悔自适应分解，二者均非上界。累积支付构成路径依赖的不确定性时钟——内在时间。在随机或低噪声情形下，内在时间自动呈现自界性质，无需额外松弛分析。该核算对所有上述设置具有统一的形式。

Conclusion: 后悔可以被精确地理解为信息核算：损失由不确定性支付和信息距离缩减两部分组成。这一视角统一了广泛的学习与博弈算法，并将有利随机性直接编码为内在时间的自界行为，为自适应后悔分析提供了新基础。

Abstract: Bayesian and multiplicative-weights updates reweight experts, models, or actions from sequential feedback. We show that the regret of any such update obeys an exact information-accounting identity. On each round, the learner's excess loss to any chosen comparator is the sum of an immediate payment for the uncertainty exposed by the round and a reduction in the information distance from the learner's current weights to the comparator. The cumulative payment defines a pathwise uncertainty clock, the \emph{intrinsic time} of the realized sequence. Summing one-step balances yields two exact adaptive decompositions of cumulative regret, one for each natural way of composing the update across rounds. Because the decompositions are exact rather than upper bounds, favorable stochastic or low-noise regimes appear as self-bounding properties of the realized intrinsic time, not as slack in worst-case analyses. The same calculus covers Hedge, optimistic and side-information variants, continuous priors, boosting, online convex optimization, contextual bandits, and repeated games: the pathwise account is the same in every case.

</details>


### [95] [Stochastic Linear Bandits with Partially Observed Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08971)
*Gautam Dasarathy,Vineet Gattani,Lalit Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究部分观测的随机线性bandit问题，其中智能体每次只能看到动作向量的随机子集坐标。作者证明了当动作向量具有低内在维度时，可以克服信息论上的次线性遗憾障碍，提出TOFU-POV算法，其遗憾值按内在维度而非环境维度缩放，并提供了匹配的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在推荐系统和医疗等现实场景中，获取完整的动作描述（特征向量）往往代价高昂甚至不可行，智能体通常只能观测到部分坐标。这种部分可观测性在一般情况下使得次线性遗憾在信息论上不可能，需要探索在何种结构假设下能够突破这一障碍。

Method: 提出TOFU-POV算法，包含三个核心步骤：(1) 利用带掩码的动作估计潜在动作子空间；(2) 使用逐epoch冻结的表示对当前动作进行插补；(3) 在得到的低维坐标上运行OFUL算法。此外设计了无需预知内在维度的秩自适应变体。下界分析采用新颖的乘积构造，分离出常规奖励学习不确定性与部分观测固有的缺失代价。

Result: 理论上证明了TOFU-POV的遗憾上界为√T量级，且按内在动作子空间维度而非环境维度缩放，并量化了缺失率、决策集大小和子空间条件数之间的交互关系。下界结果揭示了部分观测问题中特有的缺失依赖性代价。合成与真实数据实验验证了理论，显示TOFU-POV显著优于自然基线。

Conclusion: 当动作向量具有低内在维度结构时，部分观测线性bandit中的次线性遗憾是可行的。TOFU-POV算法通过子空间估计与表示冻结策略有效利用了这一结构，理论和实验均表明其相比基线方法有显著提升。

Abstract: The stochastic linear bandit, where actions are represented as vectors and rewards are linear, is a central paradigm for sequential decision making. We study a partially observed variant of this problem in which the learning agent only sees a random subset of coordinates for each action. Such partial observability arises naturally in settings like recommendation and healthcare, where full action descriptions can be expensive or even impossible to obtain. In general, this makes sublinear regret information-theoretically impossible. However, we show that this barrier can be overcome when the action vectors have low intrinsic dimension. We propose an algorithm, TOFU-POV, that estimates the latent action subspace using the masked actions, imputes current actions using an epoch-wise frozen representation, and runs OFUL in the resulting low-dimensional coordinates. Our theory shows that TOFU-POV enjoys a $\sqrt{T}$ regret that scales with the intrinsic action subspace dimension as opposed to the ambient dimension and quantifies the interaction between these quantities and the missingness, decision set size, and subspace conditioning. We also devise a rank-adaptive algorithm that does not require the knowledge of the intrinsic dimension. We complement these guarantees with a lower bound based on a novel product construction that separates usual reward-learning uncertainty from a missingness-dependent cost intrinsic to partial observation. Synthetic and real data experiments support our theory and show that TOFU-POV can substantially improve upon natural baselines in this challenging problem.

</details>


### [96] [Optimal Top-$k$ Identification from Pairwise Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08979)
*Motti Goldberger,Nils Rudi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对噪声成对比较下的固定置信度top-k识别问题，提出了首个渐近最优的自适应算法。该算法通过将信息论下界表述为鞍点问题，并利用原始-对偶过程在线学习最优比较分配策略，最终证明其期望样本复杂度在δ→0时匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于潜在效用模型的成对比较场景中，固定置信度top-k识别的渐近最优算法此前尚未被建立（即便是top-1情形）。现有文献中，bandit模型的固定置信度纯探索问题已有渐近最优方法，但成对比较领域的这一空白亟需填补。

Method: 首先，作者刻画了信息论下界的结构，将其表述为一个鞍点问题。基于此结构，设计了一个计算高效的原始-对偶（primal-dual）过程，用于在线学习渐近最优的比较分配方案。随后，构造了一个自适应比较分配算法，实时追踪原始-对偶过程学到的分配策略，并证明该算法是渐近最优的。

Result: 所提出的算法被证明是δ-正确的（即错误概率不超过δ），且其期望样本复杂度在δ→0时渐近匹配信息论下界，达到了渐近最优性。

Conclusion: 本文在噪声成对比较的固定置信度top-k识别问题上取得了突破，填补了该领域渐近最优算法的空白。原始-对偶框架为在线学习最优比较分配提供了高效途径，未来可望扩展至更广泛的排序与选择问题。

Abstract: We study the active learning problem of fixed-confidence top-$k$ identification from noisy pairwise comparisons. In this problem, an algorithm sequentially chooses pairs of items to compare, observes the outcomes, and stops when it can return the set of top-$k$ items with error probability at most $δ$. The objective is to design such a $δ$-correct procedure that minimizes the expected number of comparisons (the sample complexity). This problem falls within the broader literature on fixed-confidence pure exploration in bandit models, where a common target is asymptotic optimality: the algorithm's expected sample complexity matches the information theoretic lower bound as $δ\to 0$. Asymptotically optimal procedures have been developed for a range of fixed-confidence pure-exploration problems, however to the best of our knowledge, for top-$1$, or more generally top-$k$ identification from pairwise comparisons under latent utility models an asymptotically optimal algorithm has not been established. In this setting, we develop such an algorithm. We characterize the structure of the lower bound and formulate it as a saddle-point problem. This structure enables a computationally efficient primal-dual procedure that learns the asymptotically optimal comparison allocation online. We then construct an adaptive comparison-allocation algorithm that tracks the allocation learned by the primal-dual procedure and prove it is asymptotically optimal.

</details>


### [97] [Similarity search generalisation in contrastive learning with InfoNCE loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09405)
*Nick Whiteley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明InfoNCE损失的总体风险以O(1/k)速率逼近一个期望交叉熵，该交叉熵量化了学习嵌入的softmax相似性搜索与理想化搜索之间的偏差；同时通过Gâteaux微分引入连续性界，证明负样本的 averaging 效应可稳定泛化误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对InfoNCE损失的解释主要依赖k→∞极限下的互信息或嵌入的对齐性与均匀性视角，缺乏对有限负样本数目k下泛化性能的严格量化分析。作者旨在填补这一空白，建立有限k下InfoNCE的泛化理论。

Method: 1) 将InfoNCE总体风险分解，证明其与一个期望交叉熵的差距为O(1/k)；2) 利用Gâteaux微分推导InfoNCE损失的连续性界，该界保留了负样本平均的结构，并引入可调的"逆温度"参数以匹配算法温度；3) 对参数Lipschitz连续的嵌入函数，证明k增大时泛化误差趋于稳定。

Result: InfoNCE总体风险与理想化softmax搜索的期望交叉熵之间的差距为O(1/k)；通过Gâteaux微分得到的连续性界表明，负样本数量k的增长对泛化误差具有稳定化效应。

Conclusion: 该工作为有限负样本下InfoNCE的泛化性能提供了理论保证，揭示了大k值下负样本平均对泛化误差的稳定作用，补充了现有仅关注k→∞极限的理论分析。

Abstract: Similarity search is a primary application of embedding models trained by contrastive learning. For one of the most popular contrastive learning loss functions, InfoNCE, we show that the population risk with $k$ negative samples is $O(1/k)$ close to an expected cross-entropy which quantifies deviation between i) a softmax similarity search over unseen data using the learned embedding function, and ii) an idealised softmax search over the same data but using similarity implicitly represented in the positive sample generator. This complements existing interpretations of InfoNCE in the $k\to\infty$ limit which are phrased in terms of mutual information, and alignment versus uniformity in embeddings. To quantify generalisation performance, we introduce a new continuity bound for the InfoNCE loss, obtained via Gâteaux differentiation. The bound preserves the structure of averaging over negative samples present in the loss function and features an ``inverse temperature'' parameter which can be tuned to account for the algorithmic temperature. For embedding functions which are Lipschitz in a parameter, this yields a simple demonstration that the averaging effect of $k$ negative samples in the InfoNCE loss carries over to stabilisation of the generalisation error as $k$ grows.

</details>


### [98] [Neural Collapse Is Forbidden: Information Floors in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09487)
*Bruno Abrahao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证语言模型表征中的类内方差并非"不完整的神经坍缩"，而是有目的的信息存储分配，且遵循一条可量化的规律：类别结构仅承载4-12%的表征方差，而词元内上下文承载79-91%；该分配由词元级权重衰减导致的类别计数比例失衡所驱动，并受限于一个由条件互信息决定的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍将语言模型表征中的类内方差视为"不完整的神经坍缩"现象，作者认为这一解读存在偏差。本文旨在揭示类内方差本质上是被分配的信息存储空间，并探索其背后的分配规律和理论机制。

Method: 理论方面：(1) 通过一个单行中心化恒等式，消解了单纯形等角紧框架（ETF）的相关论断；(2) 分析词元级权重衰减，证明其按类别类型计数（type count）而非出现质量（occurrence mass）惩罚类别，将下一词元预测转化为不平衡K类问题，最优解使类别范数按类型计数排序；(3) 针对二分类情况证明了一个反向下界：类内离散度至少与条件互信息 I(token; context | category) 成正比。实验方面：在14个模型（跨越100倍参数量范围）上计算无量纲方差份额，并使用无模型估计量验证信息-离散度定律的跨模型、跨划分的普适性。

Result: 宏观类别结构仅占表征方差的4-12%，而词元内上下文占79-91%，在100倍参数量范围内保持稳定。恒等离散度（而非总方差）在所有测试模型和划分下追踪条件互信息，甚至跨模型也成立——一个模型的信息量可预测另一个模型的离散度。在预训练过程中，类别方差份额经历超调、衰减和部分恢复，因为其必须携带的信息从未离开。

Conclusion: 类内方差不是神经坍缩失败的标志，而是表征空间中有目的的信息分配。该分配遵循一条信息论定律：类别内离散度由条件互信息下界约束，且由词元级权重衰减导致的类型计数失衡所塑造。这为理解语言模型表征的几何结构提供了全新视角。

Abstract: Within-class variance in language-model representations is commonly read as incomplete neural collapse. We argue it is allocated information storage, and that the allocation obeys a law. A one-line centering identity voids a family of simplex equiangular-tight-frame claims, including our own earlier ones; in dimensionless variance shares across 14 models, macro-category structure carries only 4-12% of representational variance and within-token context carries 79-91%, stable across a 100x parameter range. On the theory side, token-level weight decay penalizes a category in proportion to its type count, not its occurrence mass, reducing next-token prediction to an imbalanced K-class problem whose optimum orders category norms by type count. A converse floor, proved for binary categories, forces within-category dispersion to be at least proportional to the conditional mutual information I(token; context | category). The law holds: identity dispersion, not total variance, tracks this information across every tested model and partition, under a model-free estimate and even across models, where one model's information predicts another's dispersion; and over pretraining the category share overshoots, decays, and partially recovers, because the information it must carry never left.

</details>


### [99] [Statistically Undetectable Backdoors in Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09532)
*Andrej Bogdanov,Alon Rosen,Neekon Vafa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示对抗性模型训练者可以在大规模深度前馈网络中植入统计上不可检测的后门，揭示了模型训练者与用户之间的根本性权力不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示深度学习模型中，模型训练者（可能为恶意方）相对于模型用户所拥有的根本性权力不对称——训练者可以植入后门，而用户在标准密码学假设下无法在多项式时间内检测或生成相应的对抗样本。

Method: 在白盒设置下，构造统计不可区分的后门模型与诚实训练模型，两者在总变差距离上接近；利用基于不变性的对抗样本，使远距离输入映射到异常接近的输出；基于标准密码学假设，证明没有后门时无法在多项式时间内生成此类对抗样本。

Result: 理论分析和初步实证结果表明，模型训练者能够在深度前馈网络中植入统计上无法检测的后门，且在没有后门的情况下，生成此类对抗样本在多项式时间内是证明不可行的。

Conclusion: 模型训练者与模型用户之间存在根本性的权力不对称：训练者可以隐秘地植入后门并利用不变性对抗样本，而用户即使在白盒条件下也无法统计检测或复现此类攻击。

Abstract: We show how an adversarial model trainer can plant backdoors in a large class of deep, feedforward neural networks. These backdoors are statistically undetectable in the white-box setting, meaning that the backdoored and honestly trained models are close in total variation distance, even given the full descriptions of the models (e.g., all of the weights). The backdoor provides access to invariance-based adversarial examples for every input, mapping distant inputs to unusually close outputs. However, without the backdoor, it is provably impossible (under standard cryptographic assumptions) to generate any such adversarial examples in polynomial time. Our theoretical and preliminary empirical findings demonstrate a fundamental power asymmetry between model trainers and model users.

</details>


### [100] [A Unified Approach to Interpreting Knowledge Distillation for Large Language Models via Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08776)
*Qingzhuo Wang,Ruiyang Qin,Zhenxin Qin,Wen Shen,Zhihua Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的交互分解框架来分析知识蒸馏在LLM中的工作机制，发现各种KD方法的共同机制是交互稀疏化，并提出CIP损失函数来显式约束复杂交互的稀疏性，从而提升蒸馏性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏在LLM中虽已取得成功，但其背后的工作机制尚不清楚。作者希望从统一的视角揭示不同KD方法背后的共同机制，并解释不同KD方法性能差异的原因。

Method: 首先，将LLM的输出分数分解为大量交互（interactions）之和，每个交互表示一组输入变量之间的非线性关系。基于此分解，分析不同KD方法的共同机制。进而，提出插件式损失函数CIP（Complex Interaction Penalty），在蒸馏过程中显式惩罚复杂交互，促进交互稀疏化。

Result: 发现各种KD方法的共同机制是交互稀疏化——学生模型保留更少的交互进行推理，将其他交互抑制为零。不同KD方法的性能差异源于其处理复杂交互的能力差异，能实现更高复杂交互稀疏性的方法通常性能更好。CIP在域内和域外分布基准上均能一致地提升多种KD方法的性能。

Conclusion: 交互稀疏化是KD方法在LLM中的统一工作机制，通过CIP显式约束复杂交互的稀疏性可有效提升蒸馏性能，为理解和改进知识蒸馏提供了新的理论视角和实用工具。

Abstract: Despite the success of knowledge distillation (KD) in Large Language Models (LLMs), the underlying mechanism behind its efficacy remains unclear. In this paper, we propose a unified approach to explore the common mechanism of various KD methods using interactions. Specifically, we decompose the output score of the LLM into the sum of numerous interactions. Each interaction represents a nonlinear relationship involving a set of input variables (e.g., words). Based on the decomposed interactions, we discover that the common mechanism underlying various KD methods is the sparsification of interactions, i.e., student models retain fewer interactions for inference while suppressing other interactions to zero effects. Furthermore, we discover that the performance variance across different KD methods arises from their capabilities in handling complex interactions. A KD method typically yields better performance if it enables the student model to achieve higher sparsity of complex interactions. Motivated by these insights, we propose a plug-and-play loss function called Complex Interaction Penalty (CIP) to explicitly enforce the sparsity of complex interactions during the distillation process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that integrating CIP consistently improves the performance of diverse KD methods on both in-domain and out-of-distribution benchmarks.

</details>


### [101] [iLENS: Interpretable LLM-Guided Mixture-of-Experts for Neuroimaging Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08778)
*Farica Zhuang,Seong Woo Han,Zixuan Wen,Shu Yang,Yize Zhao,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: iLENS 是一个基于混合专家（MoE）架构、由大语言模型（LLM）引导的可解释生存预测框架，用于阿尔茨海默病转化风险预测，结合结构化神经影像与非结构化信息实现透明的临床决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AD生存预测模型通常是静态预测器，可解释性有限且缺乏自然语言推理能力，无法在临床中提供透明、可信的决策支持。

Method: 提出 iLENS 框架：利用 LLM 融合结构化神经影像测量与非结构化信息，引导混合专家（MoE）模型中的专家路由，实现生存预测；同时提供生物学生理依据的路由决策解释。

Result: iLENS 在 AD 转化生存预测上展现了有竞争力的预测性能，并具备患者亚型分型能力。

Conclusion: iLENS 成功弥合了高性能生存分析与可解释临床决策支持之间的鸿沟，为AD前驱期转化预测提供了透明且生物学合理的新方案。

Abstract: Alzheimer's Disease (AD) is a complex neurodegenerative disorder that continues to impact millions of people worldwide. Predicting AD conversion during the prodromal stage remains critical for disease understanding and patient care. As such, survival models are widely used for AD risk prediction, yet they are typically static predictors with limited interpretability and no capacity for natural language reasoning. In this work, we propose iLENS, an interpretable large language model (LLM) guided framework based on mixture-of-experts (MoE) for survival prediction in AD conversion. Our approach uses LLM to synthesize structured neuroimaging measurements and unstructured information to guide expert routing. Our framework demonstrates competitive predictive performance and capability in patient subtyping. Furthermore, our framework provides transparent, biologically grounded rationales for its routing decisions, bridging the gap between high-performance survival analysis and interpretable clinical decision support.

</details>


### [102] [Signed Symmetric Quantization for Few-Bit Integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08779)
*Ian Colbert,Eashan Dash,Pablo Monteagudo-Lago,Juan Amboage,Srinidhi N,Giuseppe Franco,Nicholas J. Fraser,Arun Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有符号对称量化（signed symmetric quantization），通过有符号的absmax网格将额外可表示值分配给主导离群值尾部，在保持零点在零的前提下，实现了与标准无符号对称量化相同的推理效率，同时在低比特下显著降低量化误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准对称量化因尺度严格为正，将有符号整数中额外的负可表示值固定在负尾部，导致正离群值被迫截断，这在低比特下成为显著误差来源。非对称量化虽可缓解此问题，但引入运行时开销。本文旨在寻找一种既保留对称量化的运行时优势、又能灵活利用额外可表示值的方法。

Method: 提出有符号对称量化：允许尺度取负值，通过轻量级的符号选择规则将额外可表示值分配给主导离群值尾部，构建有符号absmax网格（零保持不变）。理论分析包括：(1)证明该网格在ℓ₂量化误差上条件最优，且该条件在88-99%的低比特LLM权重分组中成立；(2)证明标准对称量化器取反尺度等价于同一有符号整数字母表上的单位零点偏移。

Result: 在Qwen3、Qwen3.5和Llama3系列模型上验证：相比标准无符号对称量化，perplexity和下游few-shot准确率均有提升，且无额外推理成本。此前实验显示4-bit对称格式在llama.cpp/AMD EPYC Turin CPU上比非对称格式内存减少9%、吞吐量提升2.45倍。

Conclusion: 有符号对称量化是低比特LLM量化的有效第三选择，兼顾对称量化的运行时效率与非对称量化的灵活性，无需牺牲推理性能即可显著改善量化质量。

Abstract: The signed integer alphabet contains one more negative representable value than positive. Yet, by convention, the standard symmetric integer quantizer fixes its scale to be strictly positive, which assigns this extra representable value to the negative tail and can force clipping of positive outliers. In this work, we show that, at few-bit precision, such clipping is a non-trivial source of quantization error. Asymmetric quantization addresses this problem with a zero point, shifting the grid toward the observed data range; however, this flexibility is well-known to carry a runtime penalty. For example, in llama.cpp on an AMD EPYC(TM) "Turin" CPU, a 4-bit symmetric format uses up to 9% less memory with up to 2.45$\times$ higher throughput than its asymmetric counterpart. We highlight signed symmetric quantization as a third option that retains the runtime profile of symmetric quantization without the penalty of the asymmetric format: our signed absmax grid places the extra representable value on the dominant-outlier tail through a principled and lightweight sign selection rule while keeping the zero point at zero. Our theoretical analysis offers two main results. First, we establish the signed absmax grid as conditionally bound-optimal on $\ell_2$ quantization error, and show that the condition holds for 88-99% of weight groups across pre-trained large language models (LLMs) at low bit widths. Second, we show that negating the scale of a standard symmetric quantizer is analytically equivalent to a unit zero point shift on the same signed integer alphabet. We empirically validate our proposal on models from the Qwen3, Qwen3.5, and Llama3 families, and observe improvement in perplexity and downstream few-shot accuracy over the standard unsigned symmetric quantizer at no extra inference cost

</details>


### [103] [Sticky Routing: Training MoE Models for Memory-Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08780)
*Ali Kayyam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StickyMoE 提出一种可微分的路由一致性损失函数，在预训练阶段惩罚相邻 token 之间的专家切换，将专家切换率降低高达 60%，而困惑度损失不到 4%，在质量-局部性前沿上 Pareto 优于事后微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型中相邻 token 常激活不同专家，导致边缘设备上频繁的权重换入换出（慢速存储与快速内存之间）。现有方案要么是系统级的缓存启发式方法，要么是事后路由微调，都未在预训练阶段从根源解决问题。

Method: 提出 StickyMoE：一种可微分的路由一致性损失函数，直接惩罚相邻 token 之间的专家切换，使路由在语义连贯的片段上保持相同的专家分配。无需架构改动，仅增加一个超参数 λ，且从训练第一步就让专家表示与路由决策协同适应。

Result: 在小规模 MoE 语言模型上，StickyMoE 将专家切换率降低高达 60%，困惑度损失小于 4%，在质量-局部性 Pareto 前沿上严格优于事后路由微调方法。

Conclusion: 路由的时间局部性（temporal locality）最有效的培养时机是在训练阶段。StickyMoE 通过在预训练中引入一致性约束，实现了比事后方法更优的质量-效率权衡。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models activate only a sparse subset of experts per token, yet consecutive tokens frequently activate different experts -- causing constant weight swapping between slow storage and fast memory on edge devices. Existing remedies are either system-level (caching heuristics) or post-hoc (router fine-tuning), leaving the root cause unchanged during pretraining. We propose StickyMoE, a differentiable routing consistency loss that penalises abrupt expert switches between adjacent tokens, encouraging the router to maintain the same expert assignment across semantically coherent spans. StickyMoE requires no architectural changes, adds a single hyperparameter lambda, and unlike post-hoc methods, allows expert representations and routing decisions to co-adapt from the first training step. Experiments on small-scale MoE language models show that StickyMoE reduces the expert switch rate by up to 60% with less than 4% perplexity degradation, Pareto-dominating post-hoc fine-tuning on the quality-locality frontier. Routing temporal locality is most efficiently instilled at training time.

</details>


### [104] [Reward Transport: Property Control in Flow Matching via Noise-Space Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08781)
*Kehan Guo,Yili Shen,Yujun Zhou,Yue Huang,Chujie Gao,Shiyi Du,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Reward Transport 方法，将流匹配中的噪声-数据耦合关系重新定义为对齐接口，通过最优传输在训练时将标量噪声坐标与分子奖励对齐，从而在推理时无需额外模型或计算即可实现可控生成。该方法在耦合保持极限下可恢复交叉熵方法的截断奖励分布，实验表明单一标量坐标能对 logP 和 QED 产生单调且方向相反的结构控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统流匹配中，噪声与数据的配对方式（耦合）仅被视为计算选择。作者发现这一耦合可以作为对齐接口，通过按目标分子属性匹配噪声与数据，将可控结构嵌入到学习的流场中，从而在推理时实现零额外代价的可控生成。

Method: 提出 Reward Transport：训练阶段利用最优传输耦合，将一维标量噪声空间坐标与分子奖励值对齐；推理阶段通过调节该标量坐标来引导生成分布。在耦合保持极限下，对该坐标做阈值截断可恢复交叉熵方法（CEM）的截断奖励分布。该方法与无分类器引导和条件流匹配互补。

Result: 在 ZINC-250K 和 GuacaMol 数据集上，调节标量坐标可实现对 logP 的单调控制和 QED 在其操作范围内的一致控制。同一控制旋钮对不同目标产生相反的结构响应：为 logP 增长分子，为 QED 缩小分子，排除了通用尺寸偏差。此外，epsilon-预测扩散下的负面结果阐明了耦合级对齐在何处结构性缺失。

Conclusion: Reward Transport 将流匹配的耦合从纯粹的计算选择提升为原则性的对齐接口，提供了连续可调的分布级控制，无需推理时的奖励模型或梯度引导，为分子生成等领域的可控生成开辟了新路径。

Abstract: The coupling in flow matching -- the rule pairing noise vectors with data points -- is typically treated as a computational choice. We show that this coupling can instead serve as an alignment interface: by matching noise and data according to a target molecular property, it embeds controllable structure directly into the learned flow field. Building on this view, we introduce Reward Transport, which uses optimal transport coupling at training time to align a scalar noise-space coordinate with molecular rewards; at inference, varying this coordinate steers the generated distribution without requiring an oracle, reward model, gradient guidance, or additional computation. In the coupling-preserving limit, thresholding this coordinate recovers the Cross-Entropy Method's truncated reward distribution, providing a principled, continuously adjustable distribution-level control knob. Empirically, on ZINC-250K and GuacaMol, sweeping the scalar induces monotone control of logP and consistent QED control over its operating range; most tellingly, the same knob produces opposite structural responses for different targets, growing molecules for logP but shrinking them for QED, which rules out a generic size bias. The interface is complementary to classifier-free guidance and conditional flow matching, while a negative result under epsilon-prediction diffusion clarifies where coupling-level alignment is structurally absent. Code: https://github.com/KehanGuo2/reward-transport

</details>


### [105] [Director: Accelerating Distributed MoE Serving via Online Proactive Expert Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08782)
*Qianli Liu,Kaibin Guo,Zicong Hong,Peng Li,Fahao Chen,Haodong Wang,Jian Lin,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Director 是一个面向 MoE 模型服务的分布式系统，通过预测驱动的在线专家放置策略，将端到端延迟降低了 11%~55%。其核心包括轻量级预测器、低中断的在线迁移模块，以及具有 (1+ε) 近似比的多项式时间优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
