Motivation: 现有专家放置方法依赖历史请求的激活模式，在面对多样且快速变化的请求时表现不佳，需要一个能在线、主动优化专家放置的方案。但实现该方案面临三大挑战：未来请求专家激活的不确定性、专家迁移成本高、以及优化的 NP-hard 复杂度。

Method: Director 采用三阶段流水线：(1) 使用轻量级级联预测器或低比特量化副本预测 incoming 请求的专家激活模式；(2) 在线迁移模块在计算密集阶段执行迁移，实现近零停机；(3) 核心是基于松弛的专家放置优化器，在容量约束下以多项式时间运行，并达到 (1+ε) 近似比。

Result: 在 Mistral、DeepSeek、Qwen 等主流 MoE 模型上的广泛实验表明，与现有工作相比，Director 将端到端延迟降低了 11%~55%。

Conclusion: Director 通过预测驱动、在线优化的方式有效解决了 MoE 服务中专家放置的动态适应问题，实现了显著的延迟降低，证明在线主动放置策略是应对多样化请求模式的有效范式。

Abstract: Expert parallelism has become the prevailing paradigm to serve Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. Its efficiency depends on the communication and computation latencies of the GPUs, which are linked to the placement of experts in the GPUs. Existing works for optimizing expert placement focus on leveraging past requests' expert activation patterns. However, they demonstrate deficiencies facing diverse and rapidly changing request patterns, calling for an online, proactive approach. Implementing such an approach requires addressing several challenges: the uncertainty associated with incoming requests' expert activation, the cost of expert migration, and the NP-hard complexity in optimization. Therefore, we present Director, a new distributed MoE serving system that minimizes end-to-end latency via prediction-driven, online expert placement. Director uses either a lightweight cascaded predictor or a low-bit quantized replica for expert activation patterns of incoming requests. An online migration module then enacts the changes with near-zero downtime by executing migrations in compute-bound phases, keeping disruption bounded. At its core, a relaxation-based expert placement optimizer operates under capacity constraints, runs in polynomial time, and achieves a $(1+ε)$ approximation ratio. Finally, we implement a prototype and demonstrate, through extensive experiments, a reduction in end-to-end latency of $11\sim55\%$ for popular MoE models (e.g., Mistral, DeepSeek and Qwen) compared to existing work.

</details>


### [106] [LieBN: Batch Normalization over Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08783)
*Ziheng Chen,Yue Song,Rui Wang,Xiao-Jun Wu,Nicu Sebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 LieBN，一个基于李群的黎曼批量归一化框架。它利用李群天然具有的左/右不变度量，在理论上保证了黎曼均值和方差的可控性，并在 SPD 流形、旋转矩阵群、满秩相关矩阵流形等九种几何结构上进行了实例化与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数黎曼归一化方法要么仅针对特定流形设计，要么无法有效归一化流形值样本分布。需要在李群上建立一个通用、理论有保障的黎曼批量归一化框架。

Method: 提出 LieBN 框架：利用李群上天然存在的左不变和右不变黎曼度量，在群结构下对切空间中的样本进行标准化（中心化与缩放），并通过群指数/对数映射在流形与切空间之间转换，从而实现对黎曼均值和方差的控制。论文进一步通过矩阵幂变形（matrix power deformation）在 SPD 流形上引入了新的右不变度量，并扩展了三种已有的李群结构。

Result: 在 SPD 流形（4 种几何）、旋转矩阵群（1 种几何）、满秩相关矩阵流形（4 种几何）共九种几何结构上进行了广泛实验，验证了 LieBN 框架的有效性。

Conclusion: LieBN 提供了一个适用于任意李群的通用黎曼批量归一化框架，具有理论收敛保证，且在不同流形和几何结构上均表现出色。代码已开源。

Abstract: Manifold-valued measurements are prevalent in various machine learning tasks. Recent advances have extended Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) to operate on manifolds, accompanied by normalization techniques tailored to different geometries, collectively referred to as Riemannian normalization. However, most existing Riemannian normalization methods are either designed for specific manifolds or fail to effectively normalize manifold-valued sample distributions. To address these limitations, we propose LieBN, a framework for Riemannian Batch Normalization (RBN) over Lie groups. Our approach leverages the theoretically convenient left- and right-invariant metrics, which naturally exist in every Lie group, and provides theoretical guarantees for controlling the Riemannian mean and variance. We instantiate LieBN across nine distinct geometries: four on the Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) manifold, one on the group of rotation matrices, and four on the manifold of full-rank correlation matrices. Notably, among the SPD metrics, we introduce a novel right-invariant metric and extend three existing Lie group structures via matrix power deformation. Extensive experiments on different manifolds validate the effectiveness of our framework. The code is available at https://github.com/GitZH-Chen/LieBN.git.

</details>


### [107] [HERO: A Heterogeneity-Aware Benchmark Library for Federated Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08784)
*Thinh T. H. Nguyen,Le-Tuan Nguyen,Minh-Duong Nguyen,Nhi Trinh,Anh Tran Nam Nguyet,Dung D. Le,Kok-Seng Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HERO 是一个面向联邦持续学习（FCL）的异构感知基准库，通过解耦任务划分、客户端数据划分和客户端任务顺序三个维度，提供可比较、可复现的评估框架，并在 CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet 和 OGB-MolPCBA 上验证了不同设置下方法行为的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 FCL 评测难以横向比较，因为各工作同时改变了数据集、任务划分、客户端数据分布、任务顺序、骨干网络和报告规则，导致无法判断性能差异来源于方法本身还是实验设置。因此需要一个统一、可控的基准来隔离这些混杂因素。

Method: HERO 将 FCL 基准构建解耦为三个独立维度：任务划分、客户端数据划分、客户端任务序列。HERO-Core 用 α 控制客户端数据倾斜度，用 ρ 控制任务顺序错配程度。在 CIFAR-100 和 TinyImageNet 上以最终平均准确率、平均遗忘率和后10%客户端准确率评估代表性 FCL 方法；另在 OGB-MolPCBA 上做基于图的 Domain-IL 可移植性案例研究。

Result: 方法在简单设置与异构设置下表现迥异；平均准确率可能掩盖弱客户端性能；任务顺序错配使方法偏好不同于同步评估场景；HERO 接口能有效暴露图像域外（图域）的域偏移难度。

Conclusion: HERO 通过解耦关键混杂因素，揭示了 FCL 评测中易被忽视的公平性与脆弱性，为后续研究提供了标准化、可复现且具备设置感知能力的评测基础设施。

Abstract: Federated continual learning (FCL) evaluates how distributed clients learn from changing data streams while retaining previously learned knowledge. Existing evaluations are difficult to compare because they often change datasets, task splits, client data splits, task orders, backbones, memory assumptions, and reporting rules simultaneously. We introduce \textbf{HERO}, a heterogeneity-aware benchmark library for FCL. HERO builds benchmark streams by separating three choices that are often coupled, namely the task split, the client data split, and the client task sequence. In HERO-Core, the main comparable benchmark, $α$ controls client data skew and $ρ$ controls task-order mismatch. We evaluate representative FCL methods on CIFAR-100 and TinyImageNet using final average accuracy, average forgetting, and bottom-10\% client accuracy. We also include a graph-based Domain-IL portability case study on OGB-MolPCBA, where scaffold-domain granularity changes the input distribution while the prediction task remains fixed. Our results show that method behavior changes across easy and heterogeneous settings, that average accuracy can hide weak bottom-client performance, that task-order mismatch favors different strategies from synchronized evaluation, and that the same HERO interface can expose domain-shift difficulty beyond image-based FCIL. HERO releases benchmark streams, configurations, method implementations, and reporting scripts to support reproducible and setting-aware FCL evaluation.

</details>


### [108] [DaDaDa: A Dataset for Data Pricing in Data Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08785)
*Qiheng Sun,Hongwei Zhang,Junxu Liu,Xiaokai Mao,Jinfei Liu,Kui Ren,Haibo Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DaDaDa数据集，包含来自9个主要数据市场的16,147个数据产品元数据，旨在解决数据产品定价缺乏标准化基准的问题，同时可支持数据产品分类与检索任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据交易日益普遍，但数据产品定价面临独特挑战：成本法因数据复制的近零边际成本而失效，收益法因数据收益不可预测而失效，而销售比较法虽可行却缺乏跨市场的标准化定价基准。

Method: 构建DaDaDa数据集，收集全球9个主要数据市场的16,147个数据产品元数据，利用该数据集训练定价模型以建立新数据产品的价格基准，并扩展应用于数据产品分类与检索。

Result: 实验及检索原型系统验证了DaDaDa在数据产品定价、分类和检索任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: DaDaDa作为首个面向数据产品定价的数据集，有效填补了数据市场标准化定价基准的空白，为数据产品定价及相关任务提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: High-quality data drives machine learning advances across industries. Recognizing the value of data, data transactions are increasingly common, giving rise to many data marketplaces, e.g., AWS Marketplace, Databricks, and Datarade. However, determining the appropriate prices for data products remains a significant challenge due to the unique properties of data products. Traditional pricing methods in economics can be categorized into the cost approach, the income approach, and the sales comparison approach. The cost approach fails in data pricing due to near-zero marginal cost from data replication, and the income approach fails due to inherently unpredictable data revenue. The sales comparison approach remains viable, yet its application is hindered by the absence of standardized pricing benchmarks for data products across marketplaces. To address this challenge, we introduce \texttt{DaDaDa}, the first dataset for data product pricing, containing metadata for 16,147 data products from 9 major data marketplaces worldwide. \texttt{DaDaDa} enables the training of pricing models, thereby establishing price benchmarks for new data products. In addition, \texttt{DaDaDa} can be utilized for other important tasks in data markets, such as data product classification and retrieval. Experiments and a retrieval prototype demonstrate the effectiveness of \texttt{DaDaDa} for pricing, classification, and retrieval of data products. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/ZJU-DIVER/DaDaDa.

</details>


### [109] [Accelerating GPU Inference of Large Language Models with Moderately Unstructured Sparse Weight Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08786)
*Tao Lu,Haoyu Wang,Zonghui Wang,Keshen Xiang,Jiaheng Zhang,Wenzhi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM推理中约50%中等稀疏度场景下，现有GPU稀疏矩阵乘法(SpMM)内核无法超越稠密计算的瓶颈，提出了一种三层矩阵存储格式及相应的SpMM内核设计，首次在配备HBM的现代GPU上实现了超越稠密矩阵乘法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理成本日益增长，稀疏化剪枝可加速推理，但为维持模型质量，稀疏度通常限制在约50%的中等水平。在该稀疏度下，现有所有GPU SpMM内核性能均不及稠密矩阵乘法，存在明显的性能洼地，亟需专门优化。

Method: 提出三层矩阵存储格式：①Sparse-TC层，使稀疏张量核心(sparse tensor cores)能加速SpMM；②Slot-Filling层，利用并行微分距离(parallel differential distance)进行矩阵压缩，并支持低成本的片上解码；③轻量级Residual层，保证SpMM计算正确性。基于该格式设计联合利用稀疏张量核心与CUDA核心的SpMM内核，构建高效执行流水线并实现片上计算与内存访问的重叠。

Result: 首次在配备HBM的现代GPU上超越稠密矩阵乘法性能。相比SpInfer (EuroSys'25最佳论文) 实现最高1.64倍内核级加速，相比FlashLLM (VLDB'24) 实现最高1.41倍端到端加速。

Conclusion: 通过创新的三层存储格式与协同利用稀疏张量核心和CUDA核心的内核设计，成功填补了中等稀疏度下GPU推理的性能空白，为LLM高效稀疏推理提供了首个实际可用的解决方案。

Abstract: With the growing deployment of large language models (LLMs), LLM inference cost has become a key challenge. Pruning techniques that introduce sparsity into weight matrices can accelerate inference. However, maintaining model quality typically limits pruning to moderate unstructured sparsity (around 50\%). At these sparsity levels, none of the existing GPU kernels for sparse matrix multiplication (SpMM) can outperform their dense counterparts. This paper proposes an efficient GPU inference method for LLMs with moderate sparsity. We propose a three-layer matrix storage format comprising: (i) a Sparse-TC layer enabling sparse tensor cores to accelerate SpMM; (ii) a Slot-Filling layer using parallel differential distance for matrix compression while supporting low-cost on-chip decoding; (iii) a lightweight Residual Layer ensuring correct SpMM computation. Building on this format, we design a SpMM kernel that jointly utilizes sparse tensor cores and CUDA cores. This design enables an efficient execution pipeline and overlaps on-chip computation with memory access. Evaluations show that our work is the first to outperform dense matrix multiplication on modern GPUs equipped with high-bandwidth memory (HBM). It achieves up to 1.64x kernel-level speedup over SpInfer (EuroSys'25, Best paper) and up to 1.41x end-to-end speedups over FlashLLM (VLDB'24). Our source code: https://github.com/moui0/cudac.

</details>


### [110] [Prompt-Driven Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08837)
*Sunshine Jiang,John Marangola,David Zhang,Raghuram Kowdeed,Ruiyang Luo,Nitish Dashora,Richard Li,Pulkit Agrawal,Zhang-Wei Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为 Prompt-Driven Exploration (PDE) 的方法，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）分析 rollout 视频并改写 prompt，实现 prompt 层面的 posterior sampling，从而在零奖励起步的情况下依然能学到成功策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习的探索方法（如动作空间加噪）只能产生局部扰动，难以帮助弱策略逃脱局部最优。LLM/VLA 模型虽能通过自然语言 prompt 引导全局行为变化，但在弱策略几乎从不成功的情况下，奖励过于稀疏，难以筛选出有效的 prompt。因此需要一种不依赖奖励信号来优化 prompt 的探索机制。

Method: PDE 的核心流程：每次 rollout 后，用 VLM 观察 rollout 视频，诊断策略的响应方式，并改写 prompt 以引导下一次更好的行为。这一过程本质上是经典 RL 探索框架 posterior sampling 在 prompt 层面的实现——VLM 隐式地维护一个关于有效 prompt 的分布，并根据观测到的 rollout 不断更新该分布。

Result: 在操作（manipulation）和推理（reasoning）任务上，PDE 即使在零奖励起步的条件下也能使 RL 学到成功的策略，并且在更广泛的场景中提升了采样效率。

Conclusion: PDE 为弱策略探索提供了一种全新的全局探索范式，将 posterior sampling 从传统动作空间迁移到自然语言 prompt 空间，有效缓解了稀疏奖励下的探索难题。

Abstract: Exploration is essential to RL since a policy cannot improve by repeatedly sampling the behaviors it already prefers. Standard methods inject stochasticity in the action space, but such jitter only yields rollouts close to the original. Escaping a weak policy often requires global perturbations that action noise cannot produce. Large language models (LLMs) and vision-language-action (VLA) models offer a pathway: they condition the policy on a natural language prompt, and since the rollout follows from it, modifying the prompt induces global changes. The challenge is finding prompts that induce useful global changes. With a weak policy that rarely succeeds, reward is too sparse to select on. Our idea is to refine prompts from the rollouts themselves: a vision-language model (VLM) reasons over the rollout video, diagnoses how the policy responded, and rewrites the prompt to elicit better behavior next time. This procedure realizes posterior sampling, a classical RL exploration framework, at the level of prompts: the VLM maintains an implicit distribution over useful prompts and updates it from observed rollouts. We call this strategy Prompt-Driven Exploration (PDE). Across manipulation and reasoning tasks, PDE enables RL to learn successful policies even from zero-reward starts, and improves sample efficiency more broadly. Our website is available at https://xinyunsunshine.github.io/prompt-rl.

</details>


### [111] [How are linear representations learned? Exact solutions to the dynamics of abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08843)
*William W. Yang,Andrew M. Saxe,Peter E. Latham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个研究训练过程中概念方向对齐（即"抽象"）的动力学框架。在线性网络中获得了抽象轨迹的精确解，揭示了数据与目标几何、网络深度及初始化尺度对抽象的影响规律；并将理论扩展到非线性网络，发现了非线性对抽象的衰减定律，最后在开源模型中验证了该定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究主要关注训练后概念方向是否存在（线性表示假说），但对训练过程中这些方向如何涌现的动力学过程缺乏理解。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统研究概念方向在训练中的对齐动力学。

Method: 首先在最小线性网络设定下，通过解析方法获得抽象全程轨迹的精确解；然后将理论扩展到非线性网络（erf和ReLU），分析非线性选择对抽象动力学的影响；最后在DINOv3、Gemma 4等开源模型中验证理论预测。

Result: (i) 数据和目标几何共同决定学习终点的抽象程度；(ii) 抽象能力随网络深度提升而改善；(iii) 初始化尺度控制训练中达到的最大抽象水平。非线性网络中，erf网络近似线性理论，ReLU网络的抽象更依赖输入几何而非目标几何；两种非线性均在激活值中相对于预激活值削弱了抽象（衰减定律），并在实际模型中得到了验证，应用于改进LLM线性探针的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文为"抽象"过程建立了完整的动力学理论，揭示了线性和非线性网络中概念方向对齐的解析规律，并实证验证了非线性衰减定律，对可解释性和模型控制具有理论和实践意义。

Abstract: In artificial and biological neural networks, concepts are often encoded as consistent linear directions in representation space. In deep learning, this idea is known as the linear representation hypothesis and underpins many interpretability and control methods based on linear probes, from concept detection to activation steering. Yet while prior work has studied whether such directions should exist $\textit{after}$ training, the dynamics of how they emerge $\textit{during}$ training remain poorly understood. Here, we develop a framework to study the alignment of concept directions during training - a process we call "abstraction". In a minimal linear network setting, we obtain exact solutions for the full trajectory of abstraction. These solutions reveal key analytic principles governing abstraction: (i) data and target geometry jointly determine abstraction at the end-of-learning, (ii) abstraction improves with network depth, and (iii) initialization scale controls the maximum abstraction reached during training. Extending our theory to nonlinear networks, we analyze how the choice of nonlinearity affects abstraction dynamics: erf networks approximate the linear theory, while abstraction in ReLU networks depends less on target geometry and more on input geometry. Across both, we prove a striking attenuation law: both nonlinearities weaken abstraction in activations relative to preactivations. We find evidence for this law in open models (DINOv3, Gemma 4) and apply our theory to improve linear probe generalization in LLMs. Together, our results provide a dynamical theory of abstraction with implications for interpretability and control.

</details>


### [112] [Optimizing Against Safety Representations: Activation-Guided Adversarial Suffixes and the Geometry of Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08883)
*Ege Çakar,Hannah Guan,Kayden Kehe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型的安全对齐机制，发现拒绝行为由激活空间中的低维方向介导。作者提出两种攻击方法：Activation-Guided GCG（直接针对内部拒绝方向）和Soft-GCG（基于Gumbel-Softmax的连续松弛），后者实现33倍加速且成功率更高。实验表明安全表征分布在前向传播各层而非单一位置，且更大模型对攻击更具抵抗力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究揭示LLM的安全对齐在内部表征层面存在脆弱性，拒绝行为由激活空间中的低维方向控制。但尚不清楚这些安全表征如何结构化、定位以及如何被优化过程访问。本文以此为动机，利用对抗性后缀攻击作为探针，深入探究表征层面的对齐机制。

Method: 提出两种新方法：(1) Activation-Guided GCG：将传统基于输出的损失替换为直接针对模型内部拒绝方向的损失，测试多个目标变体（全局抑制 vs. 单层-位置对抑制）；(2) Soft-GCG：利用Gumbel-Softmax将离散后缀优化松弛为连续优化问题，实现端到端可微攻击。实验跨不同模型规模进行评估。

Result: 全局抑制拒绝方向（跨所有层和位置）比针对单一层-位置对更有效，表明安全表征分布而非因果局域化。Soft-GCG相比标准GCG实现33倍加速且攻击成功率更高。规模实验显示：小模型仍易受攻击，而大模型在给定算力约束下对激活攻击和后缀攻击均具更强抵抗力。

Conclusion: 安全机制在当代模型中呈分布式编码，而非集中于单一位点。这些发现为设计更鲁棒、更具表征意识的 alignment 策略提供了具体指导：需在整个前向传播过程中加固安全表征，而非仅关注局部机制。

Abstract: Behavioral alignment in large language models often masks fragile internal safety representations. Recent work suggests that refusal behavior is mediated by low-dimensional directions in activation space. This raises questions about how such representations are structured, localized, and accessed by optimization. We study adversarial suffix attacks as a probe of representational alignment. We introduce Activation-Guided GCG, which replaces output-based objectives with losses that directly target a model's internal refusal direction. Across several objective variants, we find that suppressing refusal globally across all layers and positions is more effective than targeting a single layer-position pair. This suggests that safety representations are distributed across the forward pass rather than causally localized to a single site. We further introduce Soft-GCG, a continuous relaxation of discrete suffix optimization using Gumbel-Softmax. Soft-GCG achieves a 33 $\times$ speedup over standard GCG while improving attack success rates. Evaluating across model scales, we find that smaller models remain vulnerable while larger models resist both activation- and suffix-based attacks at our compute-constrained settings, consistent with larger and better safety trained models being harder to jailbreak. Together, our results clarify how safety mechanisms are encoded and can be broken in contemporary models. These insights provide concrete guidance for designing more robust and representation-aware alignment strategies.

</details>


### [113] [Pattern-Aware Graph Neural Networks for Handling Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08915)
*Minett Tran,Taehee Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种模式感知图神经网络，显式编码缺失模式（哪些特征缺失）与观测值，在7个UCI数据集上平均提升17%平衡准确率和22% F1-macro，并发现区分缺失模式本身比任务特定优化更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界数据集中缺失数据普遍存在，传统方法要么丢弃不完整样本，要么采用假设缺失是完全随机的填补技术，忽略了缺失模式本身可能包含的信息。作者认为缺失模式（即哪些特征缺失的组合）可能提供额外的有价值信号。

Method: 提出模式感知图神经网络（Pattern-aware GNNs），在观测值之外显式编码缺失特征信息。探索了四种编码策略：可学习嵌入（learned embeddings）、冻结随机嵌入（frozen random embeddings）、统计特征（statistical features）和层次化表示（hierarchical representations）。在7个具有自然缺失的UCI数据集上进行评估，并通过消融实验分析了注意力机制的作用。

Result: 模式感知方法相比基线取得显著提升：所有数据集平均提升17%平衡准确率和22% F1-macro。但效果因数据集而异——annealing数据集提升最显著（+80%平衡准确率），hepatitis和soybean仅提升4-5%。值得注意的是，简单随机模式嵌入（0.650）与可学习嵌入（0.663）性能接近，说明区分模式本身可能比任务特定优化更重要。消融实验表明，注意力机制有帮助但非关键：带模式感知的简单均值聚合已达0.640，而基于注意力的变体为0.645。

Conclusion: 显式编码缺失模式能显著提升模型性能；简单的随机嵌入策略即可接近可学习嵌入的效果，表明区分缺失模式比针对特定任务优化嵌入更为关键；注意力机制虽有助益，但模式信息的存在使得简单聚合方法也能取得有竞争力的结果。

Abstract: Missing data is ubiquitous in real-world datasets. Traditional methods either discard incomplete samples or apply imputation techniques that ignore potentially informative missingness patterns, implicitly assuming that missingness occurs randomly. However, missingness patterns might provide additional information. We propose pattern-aware graph neural networks that explicitly encode which features are missing alongside observed values. We used four encoding strategies -- learned embeddings, frozen random embeddings, statistical features, and hierarchical representations -- across seven UCI datasets with naturally occurring missingness. Our Pattern-aware methods achieve substantial improvements over baselines, with an average improvement of 17\% in balanced accuracy and 22\% in F1-macro across all datasets. The benefits vary significantly by dataset: annealing shows dramatic improvement (+80\% balanced accuracy), while hepatitis and soybean show minimal gains (+4--5\%). Notably, even simple random pattern embeddings perform comparably to learned embeddings (0.650 vs 0.663 balanced accuracy), suggesting that distinguishing between patterns may be more important than task-specific optimization. Our ablation study reveals that attention mechanisms, while helpful, are not critical when pattern information is available -- simple mean aggregation with pattern awareness achieves 0.640 balanced accuracy compared to 0.645 for attention-based variants.

</details>


### [114] [A Machine Learning Surrogate for Component Criticality Ranking in Interdependent Power-Communication Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08918)
*Sohini Roy,Xheni Hylviu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对电力-通信耦合基础设施的级联故障评估问题，以MIIM高保真仿真器为ground truth，训练了一个梯度提升代理模型，用于预测N-k事故的严重程度并导出组件关键性排序。在IEEE 118-bus系统上，代理模型的Spearman相关系数达到0.849（事故严重度）和0.853（组件关键性），逼近MIIM自身的可复现性上限（~0.85），且主要由层间依赖特征驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模N-k事故集合的级联故障评估在计算上极其昂贵，高保真仿真器无法直接用于韧性规划。需要开发一个低计算成本、高精度的代理模型来快速筛选和排序，仅将高保真仿真留给选择性验证。

Method: 以MIIM仿真器为ground truth生成训练数据；提取无信息泄露的结构特征（包括拓扑中心性度量和层间依赖信息）；使用梯度提升机（Gradient Boosting）训练代理模型，分别预测事故严重度和组件关键性排序；通过特征消融实验识别关键特征贡献。

Result: 在IEEE 118-bus系统上，代理模型事故严重度预测的Spearman相关系数为0.849，组件关键性排序为0.853，在三个独立采样数据集上表现稳定。MIIM自身的关键性排序复现性上限约0.85，代理模型已逼近该经验上限。全耦合网络拓扑中心性基线为0.60-0.69；特征消融表明代理模型的优势主要由层间依赖信息驱动。

Conclusion: 梯度提升代理模型可有效逼近MIIM仿真器精度上限，支持"代理模型快速排序+MIIM选择性验证"的两阶段韧性规划工作流，大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: Cyber-physical power systems are vulnerable to cascading failures caused by tight interdependencies between power and communication infrastructures. Evaluating these failures over large N-k contingency sets with a high-fidelity simulator is computationally prohibitive for resilience planning. Using the previously published Modified Implicative Interdependency Model (MIIM) as the ground-truth cascade simulator, this paper develops a machine-learning surrogate that predicts contingency severity from leakage-free structural features and derives a component-criticality ranking for prioritized hardening analysis. On the IEEE 118-bus system, the Gradient Boosting surrogate achieves Spearman correlations of 0.849 for per-contingency severity prediction and 0.853 for per-component criticality ranking, while remaining stable across three independently sampled datasets. MIIM-derived component criticality itself reproduces only to a Spearman of approximately 0.85 under the present sampling pipeline, and the surrogate operates at this empirical ceiling to within sampling variation. Topological centrality measures on the full interdependent network provide meaningful baselines (Spearman 0.60-0.69), and feature ablation shows that the surrogate's advantage is driven primarily by inter-layer dependency information. These results support a two-stage workflow in which the surrogate rapidly ranks candidate components and MIIM is reserved for selective verification.

</details>


### [115] [Training, Reading, and Editing Legible Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08946)
*Mark Oskin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可读性Transformer的训练方法，通过引入清晰度惩罚、逐通道方差下限和单元间去相关压力，将模糊的激活转化为清晰的、可解释的模糊集运算，同时保持与传统基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer的密集激活难以解释和编辑。先前工作尝试用有界算子构建可读模型，但训练中施加的清晰度惩罚存在失效模式——会将其坍缩为死常数而非锐化检测器。需要找到一种既能保持可读性又不损失质量的方法。

Method: 1) 通过恒等式 E[v(1-v)] = μ(1-μ) - var 分析清晰度惩罚的失效原因——该惩罚是方差最小化器，无法区分活检测器和常数；2) 提出逐通道方差下限作为修正，将其作为损失函数来恢复可读性和质量；3) 用可学习的逐单元分数替代手工设定的reserved-GELU划分，让模型自主分配87%的计算给清晰算子；4) 引入单元间去相关压力作为可读性旋钮，在无质量损失下权衡电路复用与独立性；5) 在旋转后的逐层框架中解读单元，分离检测（响应什么）与命名（解码为什么）。

Result: 78%的前馈操作数和50%的注意力值通道成为清晰的上下文检测器；逐头可读性从浅层的18%提升到深层的78%；深层编辑局部性提升50-184倍；可编辑显式合取（单神经元无法表达的概念）；去相关压力使概念收敛为单一、可精准编辑的单元，预测可解读为少数命名操作的简短解释。质量与常规基线持平。

Conclusion: 通过在训练中正确施加可读性压力（方差下限替代简单清晰度惩罚、学习性算子分配、去相关压力），可以构建出高度可解释且可局部编辑的Transformer，同时不牺牲模型质量，为可解释AI的实用化铺平道路。

Abstract: A transformer can be built from operators that are legible by construction -- bounded, named units that read as fuzzy set operations rather than dense activations -- but legibility must be pressed for during training, and the pressure has a failure mode. A crispness penalty meant to sharpen a bounded operator into a decisive detector instead collapses it into a dead constant. An identity, E[v(1-v)] = mu(1-mu) - var, shows why -- the penalty is a variance-minimizer blind to the difference between a live detector and a constant -- and names the fix: a per-channel variance floor, the target legibility metric written as a loss, which recovers both legibility and quality. A learned per-unit fraction then retires the hand-set reserved-GELU partition of prior work: given the choice the model keeps no unit as pure GELU and routes 87% of its load-bearing computation through crisp operators. The result is the most legible transformer we have built -- 78% of its feed-forward operands and 50% of its attention value channels are crisp-and-contextual detectors, and per-head legibility rises from 18% in shallow layers to 78% in deep ones. Read in the correct rotated per-layer frame, these units separate a clean detection (what a unit responds to) from a harder naming (what its output decodes to); and because the objective makes each unit crisp and sparse, edits to them are far more local -- 50-184x in the deep layers where the edit sites concentrate -- and can target explicit conjunctions a single neuron cannot express. Finally, a between-unit decorrelation pressure exposes a legibility dial: it trades a circuit's reuse for independence at no quality cost, turning concepts into single, surgically editable units and a prediction into a short explanation read off a handful of named operations. Quality holds at parity with a conventional baseline throughout.

</details>


### [116] [SafeExplorer: An Unbiased Policy Gradient for Reinforcement Learning with Recovery Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08925)
*Elham Daneshmand,Majid Khadiv,Glen Berseth,Hsiu-Chin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于PPO的无偏策略梯度估计方法，可在使用确定性或随机恢复策略时消除混合策略rollout带来的偏差，并通过闭合形式价值函数和模仿损失加速学习，在三个环境中将训练期摔倒次数降低26-233倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物理机器人上直接训练强化学习时，摔倒代价高昂且不可逆。现有方法在智能体离开安全区域时切换至恢复策略，但这种混合策略rollout会悄悄偏置每一个on-policy更新；而重要性采样修正面对确定性恢复策略时是病态的（ill-defined）。因此需要一种在确定性恢复策略下仍然有效的无偏估计方法。

Method: 核心是一个无偏策略梯度估计器，仅在安全时间步使用score function，从不评估恢复策略的密度，因此即使恢复策略是确定性的也保持有效。此外引入两个加速组件：(1) 当动态和恢复策略均为确定性时，为触发恢复的状态提供闭合形式的价值函数；(2) 仅在恢复成功时复制恢复动作的模仿损失。该方法作为PPO的即插即用（drop-in）修改实现。

Result: 在三环境、五种子的基准测试中，相比标准PPO，训练期摔倒次数在HalfCheetah上降低233倍，Ant上降低48倍，Unitree Go1上降低26倍，同时最终奖励匹配或超越PPO。在Ant上（恢复策略不可靠），该方法是唯一达到最佳最终奖励80%的方法。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持或提升最终性能的同时，显著降低了训练过程中的摔倒次数，特别适用于恢复策略确定性或不可靠的场景，为真实机器人上的安全强化学习训练提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Training reinforcement-learning agents directly on physical robots makes every fall costly, since a fall can damage the platform and cannot be undone like a simulator reset; the goal is therefore to minimize falls during training rather than trade them off against return, as constrained Markov decision process (MDP) formulations do. A standard mitigation hands control to a separate recovery policy whenever the agent leaves a designer-specified safe region (a subset of state space it should stay within), but the resulting mixed-policy rollouts silently bias every on-policy update, and the importance-sampling correction that would remove this bias is ill-defined whenever the recovery policy is deterministic. We address this bias with a drop-in modification of proximal policy optimization (PPO). Its core is an unbiased policy-gradient estimator that uses the score function only at safe timesteps and never evaluates the recovery policy's density, so it stays valid even when the recovery policy is deterministic, exactly where importance sampling breaks, and it empirically dominates importance sampling even when the recovery policy is stochastic. Because the recovery policy still makes credit assignment slow near the safe-region boundary, two further components accelerate learning: a closed-form value for recovery-triggering states when dynamics and recovery are deterministic, and an imitation loss that copies recovery actions only when recovery succeeds. On a three-environment, five-seed benchmark, the resulting algorithm reduces training-time falls by factors of 233x, 48x, and 26x on HalfCheetah, Ant, and Unitree Go1 over standard PPO, while matching or exceeding PPO's final reward, and on Ant, where the recovery policy is unreliable, it is the only method that reaches 80% of the best final reward.

</details>


### [117] [Sensitivity-Aware Thresholding and Token Routing for Activation Sparsification in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08991)
*Bishmoy Paul,Youngmin Yi,Hoeseok Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出两种提升LLM推理效率的方法：SATS（基于敏感度的稀疏化阈值校准）和token级条件路由，两者均在保持模型质量的同时优化了质量-吞吐量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM高效推理需要在保留模型质量的前提下，决定哪些计算可以被削减。现有基于百分位数的MLP激活稀疏化阈值校准方法未考虑不同层对稀疏化的敏感度差异，且统一对所有token施加相同修改的做法缺乏灵活性，需要更精细的裁剪策略。

Method: 方法一：SATS——使用局部MLP输出敏感度代理（而非激活百分位数）来逐层选择门控阈值，保留基于门控激活阈值化的稀疏化机制，但用敏感度感知的选择规则替代百分位数校准。方法二：轻量级token路由框架——在逐token基础上动态选择基路径或修改路径，而非对所有token统一施加修改计算。

Result: 在多个开源LLM上评估表明：SATS在匹配的实际稀疏度下优于基于阈值稀疏化的基线方法；token路由相比静态激活修改基线，实现了更优的质量-吞吐量权衡。

Conclusion: 改进的阈值校准方法和token级路由策略能够有效提升LLM推理中的质量-吞吐量权衡，为减少计算量同时保持模型质量提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Efficient inference in Large Language Models (LLMs) requires deciding where computation can be reduced while preserving model quality. We study this problem through multilayer perceptron (MLP) activation sparsification and token-level conditional routing. We first propose Sensitivity-Aware Thresholding for Sparsity (SATS), a threshold calibration method to choose layerwise gate thresholds using a local MLP output sensitivity proxy rather than calibrating thresholds directly from activation percentiles. While SATS retains the existing mechanism of sparsifying MLP activations by thresholding gate activations, it replaces percentile-based calibration with a sensitivity-aware selection rule. We then introduce a lightweight token routing framework that dynamically selects between a base path and a modified path on a per-token basis, rather than applying the modified computation uniformly to all tokens. We evaluate both methods on multiple recent open-weight LLMs. Our results show that SATS improves over the threshold-based sparsification baseline at matched actual sparsity and that token routing yields a more favorable quality-throughput trade-off than static activation modification baselines. Overall, our results suggest that improved threshold calibration and token routing can improve the quality-throughput trade-off in LLMs.

</details>


### [118] [BlockServe: Block-Grained Continuous Batching for High-Throughput Diffusion LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08930)
*Yuanjie Zhu,Liangwei Yang,Ke Xu,Weizhi Zhang,Shanghao Li,Zihe Song,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BlockServe 提出了一种面向扩散大语言模型（dLLM）的连续批处理框架，通过块级调度、混合状态执行和计算感知的准入控制器，解决了因收敛速度不均导致的吞吐量瓶颈问题，在多个基准上实现了 1.9-10.6 倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLM 在批处理推理时面临收敛异构性问题：不同序列以不同速率收敛，快序列被慢序列拖累，产生计算气泡和尾部延迟，严重制约了服务效率。

Method: BlockServe 提出三大核心机制：(1) 块级调度，在块边界即时驱逐已完成请求；(2) 混合状态执行，通过 gather-scatter 索引将双缓存和并行解码扩展至异构批次；(3) 计算感知的准入控制器，通过 token 预算填充来扩展有效批次容量。

Result: 在 Dream 和 LLaDA 模型上、五个基准测试中，BlockServe 相较于 Fast-dLLM 实现了 1.9-10.6 倍的吞吐量提升，且生成质量相当。

Conclusion: 块级调度是面向高吞吐量离线 dLLM 推理的基础性方法，BlockServe 成功验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Efficient serving of diffusion large language models (dLLMs) is hindered by convergence heterogeneity: when batching multiple requests, different sequences converge at different rates, causing faster requests to stall behind slower stragglers and introducing compute bubbles and tail latency. We present BlockServe, a continuous batching framework that integrates block-grained scheduling -- immediately evicting completed requests at block boundaries -- with mixed-state execution that extends dual cache and parallel decoding to heterogeneous batches via gather-scatter indexing. Furthermore, a compute-aware admission controller expands effective batch capacity through token-budgeted refill. On Dream and LLaDA across five benchmarks, BlockServe achieves 1.9--10.6$\times$ throughput over Fast-dLLM with comparable generation quality, establishing block-grained scheduling as a foundation for high-throughput offline dLLM inference.

</details>


### [119] [TSRouter: Dynamic Modality-Model Selection for Time Series Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08940)
*Fangxu Yu,Tao Feng,Dehai Min,Lu Cheng,Ge Liu,Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TSRouter 是一个基于图神经网络的动态路由框架，用于在时间序列推理任务中为每个查询智能选择最合适的模态（文本/图像）和模型（LLM/VLM），在4项任务上相对基线提升16%-46%，并支持零样本泛化和成本感知优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM和VLM在时间序列推理上能力互补——LLM以文本序列处理，保留精确数值但难以捕捉全局模式；VLM通过可视化高效捕获全局模式但可能丢失细粒度细节。同时，不同模型在特定任务上的专业能力和推理成本差异显著。因此，需要一种能够动态选择最优模态-模型组合的方法，但这需要建模任务、查询、模态和模型之间复杂的上下文交互。

Method: 提出TSRouter，一个基于图的动态路由框架。首先构建包含任务节点、查询节点、模态节点和模型节点的异构图，将查询特征、模态属性和模型能力之间的交互关系上下文化。然后将路由问题形式化为候选评分问题：对每个模态-模型对进行评估，基于用户定义的性能-成本偏好选择最优候选。

Result: 在4个不同的时间序列推理任务上全面评估，TSRouter相比多种基线方法取得16%至46%的相对提升；展现出鲁棒的零样本即插即用泛化能力，可推广到未见过的模型和新任务；通过成本感知优化在保持高性能的同时降低计算开销。

Conclusion: TSRouter有效解决了时间序列推理中模态与模型的动态选择问题，通过图结构建模复杂交互关系，实现了性能与成本的双重优化，并具备良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Time series reasoning is essential for real-world problem-solving. While both Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can reason about time-series data, their capabilities are complementary: LLMs process time series as text sequences and thus preserve exact numerical understanding, but struggle with global patterns, whereas VLMs efficiently capture these patterns by visualizing time series but may lose fine-grained details. Moreover, models vary significantly in task-specific expertise and inference costs. Dynamically selecting the most suitable modality and model for each query is therefore crucial, yet challenging because it requires modeling the complex interactions among tasks, queries, modalities, and models, which carry rich contextual signals. To this end, we introduce TSRouter, a graph-based dynamic routing framework. TSRouter constructs a heterogeneous graph of task, query, modality, and model nodes to contextualize the interactions among query characteristics, modality attributes, and model capabilities. TSRouter formulates routing as a candidate scoring problem, where each modality-model pair is evaluated based on user-defined performance-cost preferences to select the optimal candidate. Comprehensive evaluations on 4 distinct time series reasoning tasks reveal that TSRouter substantially outperforms diverse baselines with 16\% to 46\% relative improvements. Furthermore, TSRouter demonstrates robust zero-shot plug-and-play generalization to unseen models and novel tasks and preserves high performance while reducing computational overhead through cost-aware optimization. Our code is available at https://github.com/tianyi-lab/TSRouter.

</details>


### [120] [FairSelect: A Systematic Evaluation of Multi-Level and Intersectional Algorithmic Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08953)
*Nick Souligne,Isabella Mixton-Garcia,Vignesh Subbian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairSelect 是一个公平性策略评估工具包，支持在预处理、处理中、后处理阶段单独及组合应用公平性方法，通过合成数据和真实临床数据验证，发现公平性干预存在非加性和上下文依赖的交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性方法大多孤立评估，且仅沿单一人口统计维度（如种族或性别）衡量，忽略了交叉子群体和建模全生命周期中多阶段组合的实际需求，导致缺乏系统的公平性策略选择指南。

Method: 开发 FairSelect 工具包，支持多模型架构、交叉子群体评估，在预处理、处理中、后处理阶段单独及组合应用公平性方法，比较基线、单一方法和多层次配置下的公平性-效用权衡。验证采用合成临床数据集（模拟特定偏倚机制）和真实房颤患者两年卒中风险预测数据。

Result: 合成实验中，针对性公平性方法降低了目标子群体差异，组合策略带来更大的平均公平性提升且效用损失适中；临床预测任务中，缓解效果高度可变，部分组合同时改善公平性和预测性能，其他组合则无效甚至适得其反。

Conclusion: 公平性干预存在非加性和上下文依赖的交互效应；FairSelect 提供了一个实用框架，可系统识别在保持模型性能的同时改善子群体公平性的策略。

Abstract: Algorithmic fairness methods are increasingly used to identify and mitigate bias in machine learning models, yet most approaches are evaluated in isolation and along single demographic axes. This limits practical guidance for selecting fairness strategies, where disparities may arise across intersectional subgroups and across multiple stages of the modeling lifecycle. This work presents FairSelect, a toolkit for systematically evaluating fairness mitigation strategies applied individually and in combination across preprocessing, inprocessing, and postprocessing stages. FairSelect supports multiple model architectures, intersectional subgroup evaluation, and comparison of fairness utility tradeoffs across baseline, single method, and multi level configurations. The framework was validated using synthetic clinical datasets designed to represent specific bias mechanisms and a real-world replication of two-year stroke risk prediction among patients with atrial fibrillation. Synthetic experiments showed that targeted fairness methods generally reduced intended subgroup disparities, while combined strategies produced larger average fairness improvements with modest utility tradeoffs. In the clinical prediction task, mitigation effects were highly variable, with some combinations improving both fairness and predictive performance while others were ineffective or counterproductive. These findings demonstrate that fairness interventions interact in nonadditive and context dependent ways. FairSelect provides a practical framework for systematically identifying fairness strategies that improve subgroup equity while preserving model performance in clinical machine learning.

</details>


### [121] [Super-Tuning: From Activation-Aware Pruning to Sparse Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09287)
*Ivan Ilin,Philip Zmushko,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Super和Supra两种稀疏参数高效微调方法，利用剪枝启发的显著性信号（Wanda风格的激活加权幅值评分）来选定固定的稀疏可训练支持集。Supra进一步将该稀疏更新与LoRA混合，在匹配参数预算下实现性能提升。在Llama-3.2-1B和Llama-3-8B的算术任务上，该方法在同类适配器配置中取得最高平均准确率，表明剪枝启发的排序可为PEFT提供有效的固定稀疏支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM全参数微调内存、计算和存储开销巨大。现有PEFT方法（如LoRA）虽高效，但未充分利用剪枝领域中已成熟的显著性信号来选择哪些参数应被更新。作者探索能否将剪枝中的重要性评分重新用于指导稀疏微调中适配位置的选择。

Method: 1）Super：基于Wanda风格的激活加权幅值评分（通过一次校准前向传播计算），固定选出一个小规模的可训练稀疏支持集；2）Supra：混合适配器，将Super的稀疏更新与LoRA结合，通过简单的预算分割规则保持总可训练参数量不变；3）同时引入PaFi风格的纯幅值支持作为训练无关的稀疏基线；4）在Llama-3.2-1B和Meta-Llama-3-8B上使用单种子Math17K算术任务进行评测。

Result: 在Math17K算术实验上，最佳Super/Supra变体在各类调度选择的适配器配置中取得最高平均准确率。低分支持集（无论基于幅值还是Wanda排序）也能有效，这出乎意料。

Conclusion: 简单的剪枝启发排序可为PEFT提供有用的固定稀疏支持，尤其在与低秩适配器（LoRA）结合时效果更佳。该方法在保持参数预算的前提下提升了稀疏微调的性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain expensive to fine-tune because full-parameter updates require substantial memory, compute, and per-task storage. We study whether saliency signals originally developed for pruning can be reused to choose where a model should adapt. We propose Super, a sparse parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) method that fixes a small trainable support using a Wanda-style activation-weighted magnitude score [Sun et al., 2023] computed from a calibration pass. We then introduce Supra, a hybrid adapter that combines this sparse update with LoRA while preserving a matched trainable-parameter budget through a simple budget-splitting rule. In single-seed Math17K arithmetic experiments on Llama-3.2-1B and Meta-Llama-3-8B, the best Super/Supra variants achieve the highest average accuracy among the tested schedule-selected adapter configurations. We also include a PaFi-style magnitude-only support as a closest training-free sparse baseline and find that low-score supports under both magnitude and Wanda-style orderings can be effective. These results suggest that simple pruning-inspired orderings can provide useful fixed sparse supports for PEFT, especially when combined with low-rank adapters.

</details>


### [122] [Eluna: An Agentic LLM System for Automating Warehouse Operations with Reasoning and Task Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08960)
*Ning Liu,Kalle Kujanpää,Zhaoxuan Zhu,P Aditya Sreekar,Kaiwen Liu,Chuanneng Sun,Jorge Marchena Menendez,Matthew Bales,Tianyu Yang,Shahnawaz Alam,Rose Yu,Baoyuan Liu,Kristina Klinkner,Shervin Malmasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Eluna 是一个面向仓库 SOP 可靠执行的生产级图引导多智能体系统，通过有向无环图编码流程、并行子智能体分工，以及非对称情节蒸馏来训练小型模型，在 13 项任务基准和两个生产应用中达到 94% 的专家一致率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库操作依赖 SOP，这些 SOP 编码了复杂的多系统决策逻辑，需在严格时间约束下可靠执行。然而现有 LLM 智能体缺乏强制程序合规的机制，且完整 SOP 规范会导致上下文过载、性能退化。

Method: 提出 Eluna：图引导的多智能体框架，将 SOP 编码为具有渐进披露机制的有向无环图，独立任务委托给并行子智能体（各自具备持久代码执行和实时数据访问能力）。采用非对称情节蒸馏：强教师模型通过情节错误记忆进行改进，然后在剥离记忆的修正轨迹上微调小规模学生模型，内化修正而无需推理时额外开销。

Result: 在 13 项任务基准和两个生产应用中，微调后的模型达到或超越教师模型表现，击败所有更大规模的现成基线模型，在工单处理应用上达到 94% 的专家一致率。

Conclusion: Eluna 通过图引导架构结合非对称情节蒸馏，成功实现了生产环境下 SOP 的可靠执行，小模型经蒸馏后可匹敌甚至超越大模型，同时满足延迟和准确性要求。

Abstract: Warehouse operations are governed by Standard Operating Procedures (SOPs) that encode complex, multi-system decision logic, which must be executed reliably under strict time constraints, yet LLM agents lack mechanisms to enforce procedural compliance and degrade under the context overload full SOP specifications introduce. We present Eluna, a production-deployed agentic system for reliable SOP execution. Eluna is a graph-guided, multi-agent framework that encodes SOPs as directed acyclic graphs with progressive disclosure and delegates independent tasks to parallel sub-agents, each with persistent code execution and live data access. To meet production latency and accuracy needs, we use asymmetric episodic distillation where a strong teacher is improved through episodic error memories, then a smaller student is fine-tuned on the corrected trajectories with memory stripped, internalizing corrections without inference-time overhead. On a 13-task benchmark and two production applications, our fine-tuned models match or exceed their teacher, beat all larger off-the-shelf baselines, and reach 94% expert agreement on the ticket processing application.

</details>


### [123] [Mach-Mind-4-Flash Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09375)
*Foundation Model Team*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mach-Mind-4-Flash 是一个仅通过后训练优化（不增加预训练计算量）达到百亿参数级别性能的35B总参/3B激活的MoE智能体模型，提出MOPD多教师蒸馏和HMPO混合长度策略优化等关键技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决两个核心问题：(1) 如何在不扩展预训练的情况下，仅通过后训练使小激活参数量的MoE模型达到大模型水平；(2) 混合奖励RL中常见的"跷跷板退化"问题，以及推理链过长导致的推理成本过高问题。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1) 统一RL/OPD训练基础设施，配合动态多教师调度和算子级加速，实现17%端到端训练提速；(2) 多领域特定RL专家（推理、通用、智能体三条轨道）并行训练，通过多教师在策略蒸馏（MOPD）——一种路由反向KL目标——融合为单一通才模型，消除混合奖励RL的跷跷板退化；(3) 混合中长度策略优化（HMPO），一种单阶段token效率方法，压缩推理链19-46%，精度损失≤0.7个百分点。

Result: 在多个基准上取得领先或匹敌10-30倍激活参数量模型的成绩：AIME'26 92.70、IFBench 82.82、Behavioral-SafetyBench 80.74、BFCL-v4 75.80、BrowseComp-zh 72.31、ClawBench 84.20。

Conclusion: 通过MOPD和HMPO等创新后训练方法，小激活参数MoE模型可以在推理、安全、智能体等多样化任务上以极低的推理成本实现大模型级别的性能，证明了后训练优化的巨大潜力。

Abstract: We present Mach-Mind-4-Flash, a 35B-parameter Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) agentic model with 3B activated parameters. Through post-training optimization alone without scaling pre-training compute, the model achieves performance on par with or surpassing that of 100B-parameter-class models. By introducing scalable agentic interaction environments for large-scale reinforcement learning, the model attains significant performance gains on real-world application tasks. Our pipeline comprises three stages: (1) a unified RL/OPD training infrastructure with dynamic multi-teacher scheduling and operator-level acceleration, delivering 17\% end-to-end training speedup; (2) multiple domain-specific RL experts trained in parallel across Reasoning, General, and Agent tracks, then fused into a single generalist via Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD) -- a routed reverse-KL objective that eliminates the see-saw degradation of mixed-reward RL; (3) Hybrid Median-length Policy Optimization (HMPO), a single-stage token-efficiency method that compresses reasoning chains by 19--46\% with $\le$0.7 percentage-point accuracy loss. Mach-Mind-4-Flash scores 92.70 on AIME'26, 82.82 on IFBench, 80.74 on Behavioral-SafetyBench, 75.80 on BFCL-v4, 72.31 on BrowseComp-zh, and 84.20 on ClawBench -- leading or matching models with 10--30$\times$ its activated size at a fraction of the inference cost.

</details>


### [124] [NL-PAC: Specification Ambiguity and Certified Minimax Risk Floors in LLM-Mediated Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08961)
*Berkay Anahtarci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NL-PAC框架，利用固定模型的阈值化解码律定义可容许标签，在目标盲监督下给出了最坏风险下界（至少为逐点可容许目标类直径的一半），并证明数据无关策略可达精确随机化极小极大风险。冻结Qwen 2.5-3B审计展示了模型相对证书的获得条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型广泛用于标注与反馈，但自然语言规范常存在多重解读，而监督信道无法揭示哪一解读在起作用。额外标注仅减少采样误差，无法解决由此产生的识别问题（identification problem）。需要一种框架来形式化这一困境并给出可认证的保证。

Method: 提出Natural Language PAC (NL-PAC)框架：(1)利用固定模型的阈值化解码律定义可容许标签和候选目标；(2)多点可容许标签的概率等于逐点可容许目标类的直径；(3)在目标盲监督下证明最坏风险下界为直径的一半，且数据无关策略达到精确极小极大风险；(4)利用有限样本置信界从留存的未标注输入中对上述量进行认证；(5)在冻结的Qwen 2.5-3B上进行审计实验。

Result: 在冻结Qwen 2.5-3B审计中，一个预指定的提示获得了正的模型相对证书；而释义版本和精确规则对照组获得的证书为零。留存桥接审计发现，所供候选解读子句未能满足将证书迁移至一致解读所需的可容许性条件。该保证仅针对被审计的模型、提示、阈值和输入分布。

Conclusion: NL-PAC框架为模型标注行为提供了可认证的统计保证。正的模型相对证书可以在特定条件下获得，但该证书依赖于模型、提示和分布的特定组合，向人类解读的推广需要外部验证。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly provide labels, evaluations, and feedback for tasks specified in natural language. When a specification admits multiple readings but the supervision channel does not reveal which is operative, additional labels reduce sampling error without resolving the resulting identification problem. We introduce Natural Language PAC (NL-PAC), a framework that uses a fixed model's thresholded decoding law to define admissible labels and candidate targets. The probability that multiple labels are admissible equals the diameter of the pointwise-admissible target class, and under target-blind supervision every learner incurs worst-case risk of at least half this diameter, at every sample size; the exact randomized minimax risk over this class is attained by a data-independent strategy. Finite-sample confidence bounds make these quantities certifiable from held-out unlabeled inputs. In a frozen Qwen~2.5--3B audit, one prespecified prompt yields a positive model-relative certificate, whereas a paraphrase and exact-rule controls yield zero. A held-out bridge audit finds that supplied candidate reading clauses fail the admissibility condition needed to transfer the certificate to coherent readings. The guarantee is specific to the audited model, prompt, threshold, and input distribution; extending it to human interpretations requires external validation.

</details>


### [125] [Federated Low-Rank Koopman Learning for Multivariate Time-Series Anomaly Detection in IoT Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08978)
*Tung-Anh Nguyen,Van-Phuc Bui,Anh Tuyen Le,Kim Hue Ta,Minh Thuy Le,J. Andrew Zhang,Xiaojing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedKAD 是一种面向分布式 IoT 多元时间序列的资源高效联邦 Koopman 异常检测框架，将联邦训练建模为低秩一致性问题，通过 Stiefel-ADMM 算法在边缘设备上轻量地学习正常时序动态，实现高达 2100 倍训练加速、80 倍通信降低和 79 倍推理延迟降低，同时保持或超越联邦深度学习基线的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式 IoT 异常检测面临三大实际挑战：(1) 数据分散且非独立同分布（non-IID）；(2) 边缘设备带宽有限；(3) 边缘设备计算和内存资源受限。现有深度学习异常检测器需要训练和通信大规模神经网络模型，难以在上述约束下高效部署。

Method: 提出 FedKAD 框架：(1) 使用轻量级滑动窗口 Koopman 表示学习正常时序动态，替代深度神经网络；(2) 将联邦训练形式化为低秩一致性问题，仅交换紧凑子空间变量，原始数据和局部降阶动态保留在设备端；(3) 提出联邦 Stiefel-ADMM 算法，在正交约束下优化共享表示，并给出部分客户端参与下的收敛性与平稳性分析；(4) 推理时通过观测轨迹与学习的 Koopman 动态之间的预测残差进行局部异常检测。

Result: 在四个多元时间序列异常检测基准数据集上，FedKAD 与联邦深度学习基线相比，保持或提升了检测性能；训练速度提升高达 2.1×10³ 倍，通信量降低 80 倍，推理延迟降低 79 倍。

Conclusion: FedKAD 在检测性能不降的前提下，大幅降低了计算、通信和推理开销，充分验证了其在资源受限边缘设备上的实际部署可行性，为分布式 IoT 异常检测提供了高效的联邦学习解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed IoT systems generate multivariate time-series streams for monitoring physical assets, servers, and embedded sensing platforms. Detecting abnormal temporal behavior is critical for fault diagnosis, predictive maintenance, and security. However, practical IoT anomaly detection is hindered by decentralized and non-IID data, limited bandwidth, and the constrained computation and memory of edge devices. This paper proposes FedKAD, a resource-efficient federated Koopman anomaly detection framework for distributed IoT multivariate time series. Unlike deep-learning-based anomaly detectors that require training and communicating large neural models, FedKAD learns normal temporal dynamics through lightweight sliding-window Koopman representations. Federated training is formulated as a low-rank consensus problem, where raw sensor streams and local reduced dynamics remain on device while only compact subspace variables are exchanged with the server. To optimize the shared representation under orthonormality constraints, we develop a federated Stiefel-ADMM algorithm and provide convergence and stationarity analysis under partial client participation. During inference, each client detects anomalies locally by measuring the prediction residual between observed future trajectories and the learned Koopman dynamics. Experiments on four widely used multivariate time-series anomaly detection benchmarks show that FedKAD maintains or improves detection performance compared with federated deep-learning baselines. More importantly for IoT deployment, FedKAD provides up to $2.1\times10^3$ faster training, $80\times$ lower communication, and $79\times$ lower inference latency than neural baselines, confirming its suitability for resource-constrained edge devices.

</details>


### [126] [AlphaZero in Sparsely Rewarded Games: Limits and Auxiliary Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08984)
*Brent Kong,Tejas Ram,Tony Yue Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究AlphaZero在Connect Four和Chomp两个可评估预言机的域中的表现差距。尽管AlphaZero能达到强大水平，但无法维持最优博弈所需的精确轨迹。通过引入预言机监督的辅助损失（AZAL），能显著提升预言机一致性，在Chomp上达到完美，但在Connect Four上仍未实现完美博弈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AlphaZero虽然展示了超人类表现，但强博弈并不等于完美博弈。本文旨在系统研究神经引导MCTS在已知最优解的域中，能否真正恢复精确的最优策略，并揭示其差距所在。

Method: 在统一的自我博弈+MCTS框架下，对比三种变体：原始AlphaZero、多帧输入变体（仅用于Chomp）、以及AlphaZero辅助损失（AZAL）。AZAL在策略头中加入来自预言机（已知最优解）的监督信号。评估在两个预言机可评估域上进行：Connect Four（已解决的分 partisan 游戏，具有精确博弈论值）和Chomp（无偏博弈，受Grundy数结构支配）。

Result: 原始AlphaZero在两个域上表现强劲，但无法保持最优轨迹：Connect Four中无法维持最优走法线，Chomp中无法持续恢复g=0不变量。多帧输入对Chomp无效。AZAL显著提升预言机一致性：Chomp的10x11达到完美全盘一致性，9x10接近完美；Connect Four中提升预言机匹配率并延迟首次错误，但未达完美。

Conclusion: AlphaZero的强表现与完美博弈之间存在显著差距，仅靠多帧输入不足以弥合。加入预言机监督的AZAL能大幅提升一致性，但在更复杂的域中仍有局限。这表明恢复精确最优策略对当前方法仍具挑战性。

Abstract: AlphaZero has demonstrated that a neural-guided Monte Carlo Tree Search can achieve superhuman performance, but strong play does not necessarily imply perfect play. We study this gap in two oracle-evaluable domains with contrasting structure: Connect Four, a solved partisan game with exact game-theoretic values, and Chomp, an impartial game whose optimal play is governed by Grundy-number structure. Under a unified self-play $+$ MCTS pipeline, we compare vanilla AlphaZero, a multi-frame variant (limited to Chomp), and an AlphaZero Auxiliary Loss (AZAL) that adds oracle-derived policy supervision. We find that vanilla AlphaZero achieves strong play across both domains but cannot preserve the exact trajectories required for optimal play: in Connect Four, it fails to maintain the optimal line of play, while in Chomp, it fails to consistently restore the $g=0$ invariant. On rectangular Chomp boards, multi-frame inputs alone do not remove this gap. Nevertheless, AZAL substantially improves oracle consistency across multi-seeded full-game traces and sampled-state evaluations. On Chomp, AZAL reaches perfect full-game oracle consistency on 10x11 and high but not complete consistency on 9x10; on Connect Four, AZAL improves oracle-match rate and delays the first oracle mistake, but does not reach perfect play.

</details>


### [127] [Group Invariant Spectral Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08987)
*Yeari Vigder,Paulina Hoyos,David Thong,Joakim andén,Joe Kileel,Amit Moscovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将对称性直接融入谱嵌入的亲和核中，分析了在黎曼数据流形具有紧李群对称性时，三种不变核构造的图拉普拉斯算子逐点收敛到商空间上的二阶微分算子，从而提升收敛速率并恢复数据的内蕴几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际数据集具有旋转等对称性下的不变性，但标准谱嵌入方法将对称性相关的数据点视为无关，无法捕捉数据的内蕴低维结构。因此需要在谱嵌入框架中显式建模对称性。

Method: 将紧李群 G 的对称性直接编码到谱嵌入的亲和核中，构造三种 G-不变核；在黎曼流形 M 上分析对应图拉普拉斯算子的极限行为，证明其在商空间 M/G 上逐点收敛到显式的二阶微分算子。

Result: 理论上证明了收敛速率因有效维度降低（按群维数减少）而提升；实验上在 SO(2) 和 SO(3) 对称数据集上验证，G-不变谱嵌入能恢复数据内蕴几何，而标准谱嵌入即使在无限数据极限下也失效。

Conclusion: 通过将对称性融入亲和核，G-不变谱嵌入在理论上具有更优收敛性，在实践上能有效恢复商空间的内蕴几何，显著优于忽略对称性的标准方法。

Abstract: Spectral embedding methods are widely used for dimensionality reduction and clustering of high-dimensional datasets with intrinsic low-dimensional structures. Although many datasets of practical interest exhibit invariance under symmetries such as rotations, standard spectral embedding methods do not account for this, treating symmetry-related data points as unrelated. Our approach to this problem is to incorporate the symmetries directly into the affinity kernels used for spectral embedding. We analyze the case of a Riemannian data manifold $M$ with symmetries given by a compact Lie group~$G$ and prove that, under suitable conditions, graph Laplacians constructed from three types of invariant kernels converge pointwise to explicit second-order differential operators on the quotient space $M/G$. Our analysis implies improved convergence rates, as the effective dimension drops according to the dimension of the group. We validate our approach on datasets with $\mathrm{SO}(2)$ or $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ symmetry, and show that $G$-invariant spectral embedding recovers the intrinsic geometry of the data, in contrast to standard spectral embedding, which fails to do so even in the limit of infinite data.

</details>


### [128] [Model Agnostic Graph Prompt Learning for Crystal Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08996)
*Shrimon Mukherjee,Kishalay Das,Partha Basuchowdhuri,Pawan Goyal,Niloy Ganguly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多层次图软提示学习框架，通过节点级和图级软提示捕获晶体中未被显式编码的潜在特征，以即插即用的方式提升现有GNN在晶体性质预测上的性能（3%-15%），并支持跨性质知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有晶体图神经网络往往将领域知识硬编码到图编码模块中，导致参数膨胀且高度依赖专家经验；同时，将所有可能影响晶体性质的化学与结构特征显式纳入GNN编码器极为困难。因此，需要一种轻量、灵活的方法来捕获那些未被显式提供的潜在关键特征。

Method: 提出多层次图提示学习框架：节点级软提示用于捕获不同原子类型的局部化学语义，图级软提示用于编码晶体图的全局结构对称性。这些软提示作为可学习的连续向量，轻量且可无缝集成到任意现有GNN编码器中，无需修改原模型架构。

Result: 在多个主流基准数据集上，该方法使当前最先进GNN模型的晶体性质预测性能提升了3%至15%。此外，学习到的软提示能够实现跨性质知识迁移，显著改善训练数据稀缺情况下的预测性能。

Conclusion: 软提示学习是一种有效且通用的晶体性质预测增强策略，能够以极小的参数开销捕获隐式领域知识，提升预测精度并赋予模型跨性质迁移能力。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks have emerged as a powerful tool for the fast and accurate prediction of various crystal properties. These models often encode domain-specific knowledge into their graph encoding modules, which increases their parameter size and makes their performance heavily dependent on domain expertise. Added to this, explicitly incorporating all chemical and structural features, that might influence a specific crystal property into the GNN encoder, is a challenging task. In this work, we propose a soft prompt learning framework that captures latent features essential for property prediction, which are not explicitly provided to the GNN. We introduce a novel multilevel graph prompt learning framework comprising both node-level and graph-level soft prompts. At the node level, we capture the local chemical semantics of different atom types, while at the graph level, we encode the global structural symmetry of the crystal graph. Our proposed prompt learning framework is lightweight and seamlessly integrates with any existing GNN encoder. Extensive experiments on popular benchmark datasets show that incorporating prompt learning significantly improves (3\% - 15\%) the performance of state-of-the-art GNN models in crystal property prediction tasks. Furthermore, the learned soft prompts enable cross-property knowledge transfer, enhancing prediction performance for properties with limited training data. Code is available at https://github.com/shrimonmuke0202/Prompt.git

</details>


### [129] [Correlation-Aware Contextual Bandits with Surrogate Rewards for LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09015)
*Ajay Narayanan Sridhar,Ronak Singh,Mehrdad Mahdavi,Vijaykrishnan Narayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有相关臂和替代奖励信号的上下文赌博机问题，提出耦合奖励混合与解耦预测混合两种算法，在LLM路由等应用中显著提升样本效率与准确率-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上下文赌博机假设臂之间条件独立且仅依赖赌博机反馈，但在LLM路由等实际应用中，不同臂（如不同LLM）之间存在上下文相关的相关性，且可获得来自机器学习模型的替代奖励信号（如质量预估分数）。作者旨在利用这些可能带有噪声或错误指定的辅助信息来加速学习。

Method: 提出两种互补设计：(1) 耦合奖励混合方法，将真实奖励与替代奖励直接混合以加速学习，适用于替代信号可靠时；(2) 解耦预测混合方法，分别维护赌博机反馈和替代奖励的独立估计器，并自适应地组合其预测结果。解耦设计在替代奖励错误指定时具有鲁棒性，最坏情况下可恢复纯奖励赌博机方法的遗憾界，当替代预测足够信息量时则获得更优遗憾。

Result: 在LLM路由基准测试中，所提方法在不同准确率-成本权衡下均展现出优于标准上下文赌博机基线和强静态路由方法的样本效率与准确率-成本权衡。理论分析为两种方法均提供了遗憾界保证。

Conclusion: 通过有效利用替代奖励信号并引入解耦设计的鲁棒性机制，所提算法在保持最坏情况理论保证的同时，能在替代信号可靠时显著加速学习，为LLM路由等实际部署提供了有效的决策方案。

Abstract: We study contextual bandit problems with correlated arms and access to surrogate reward signals produced by a machine learning model, motivated by applications such as large language model (LLM) routing. Unlike classical contextual bandits that rely solely on bandit feedback and assume conditional independence across arms, our setting allows context-dependent inter-arm correlations and auxiliary reward information that may be noisy or misspecified. We propose algorithms that leverage such surrogate rewards through two complementary designs. A coupled reward-mixing approach pools true and surrogate rewards to accelerate learning when surrogate signals are reliable, while a decoupled prediction-mixing approach maintains separate estimators for bandit feedback and surrogate rewards and adaptively combines their predictions. This decoupling yields robustness to surrogate misspecification, recovering regret guarantees comparable to reward-only bandit methods in the worst case, while achieving improved regret when surrogate predictions are sufficiently informative. We provide theoretical regret analyses for both approaches and evaluate them on LLM routing benchmarks under varying accuracy versus cost trade-offs. The results demonstrate improved sample efficiency and consistently better accuracy-cost trade-offs compared to standard contextual bandit baselines and strong static routing methods.

</details>


### [130] [Variable-Length Generative Protein Design via Generalized Poisson Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09039)
*Chaoran Cheng,Zhanghan Ni,Yanru Qu,Yuxin Chen,Ruihan Guo,Jiajun Fan,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GPFlow 是一种可变长度蛋白质生成框架，通过建模非齐次广义泊松过程的速率函数，无需预先指定长度即可生成蛋白质，在结构设计、序列设计和肽协同设计等任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散和流基生成模型在蛋白质设计中需要预先指定序列长度，而最优长度往往未知且与可设计性紧密耦合，这限制了设计空间的灵活探索。

Method: 提出 Generalized Poisson Flow (GPFlow)，将蛋白质生成建模为非齐次广义泊松过程，通过最小化负对数似然学习速率函数，实现可变长度生成。论文还给出了恢复联合多模态分布的群体级保证，并推导了数据分布与生成分布间 KL 散度的上界。

Result: 在无条件设计中，GPFlow 提升了结构可设计性，序列设计达到最佳分布适应度，并能完美恢复长度分布；在条件 motif scaffolding 的 16 项结构设计任务中 10 项排名第一，序列设计中也通过更多任务；在肽协同设计中即使不使用真实长度预言机也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: GPFlow 成功解决了可变长度蛋白质生成问题，在结构、序列及肽协同设计等多模态任务中全面验证了其生成质量，展现出优于固定长度基线方法的性能。

Abstract: The ability to generate variable-length proteins is crucial in protein design, where the optimal length is often unknown and tightly coupled to designability. Current diffusion- and flow-based generative models typically require the protein length to be specified before sampling, limiting their flexibility in exploring the feasible design space. To address this limitation, we introduce Generalized Poisson Flow (GPFlow), a variable-length generative framework that learns the rate function of an inhomogeneous generalized Poisson process by minimizing its negative log-likelihood. We establish population-level guarantees for recovering the joint multimodal distribution and derive an upper bound on the KL divergence between the data and generated distributions. We comprehensively evaluate GPFlow across structure and sequence design, motif scaffolding, and peptide co-design, spanning Euclidean, categorical, and Riemannian modalities to fully validate its variable-length generation quality. In unconditional design, GPFlow improves structural designability and achieves the best distributional fitness for sequence design compared to their corresponding fixed-length baselines, while perfectly recovering the length distribution. In conditional motif scaffolding, GPFlow ranks first on 10 of 16 structure-based design tasks with significantly more unique successes and also achieves more passed tasks in sequence-based design. In peptide co-design, GPFlow remains competitive even without access to a native-length oracle.

</details>


### [131] [Learning More from Less: Reinforcement Learning from Hindsight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09042)
*Iris Xu,Sunshine Jiang,John Marangola,Nitish Dashora,Richard Li,Thomas Liu,Zexue He,Yuheng Zhi,Alex Pentland,Pulkit Agrawal,Zhang-Wei Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Learning from Hindsight (LfH) 方法，通过视觉-语言模型对失败 rollout 进行事后指令与奖励重标注，将失败转化为不同任务的成功样本，在 VLA 模型的 RL 后训练中将样本效率提升 5 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型 RL 后训练中，机器人 rollout 收集成本高昂，且操作任务通常仅有稀疏奖励，训练初期弱策略几乎全部失败，难以从中学习。但这些失败 rollout 实际上在某些未指定的任务上是成功的，现有方法未能利用这一信息。

Method: 提出 LfH 框架：利用单一 VLM 对失败 rollout 同时进行指令和奖励的 hindsight relabeling——为一批失败 rollout 生成事后指令，并评分每条轨迹对该指令的满足程度；将重标注数据与原始数据联合训练策略。

Result: 在 out-of-distribution LIBERO-PRO 任务上，LfH 相比标准 RL 实现 5 倍样本效率提升，且优于密集进度奖励基线；该增益在多种 VLA 骨干网络和实体 Franka 机器人上均得到验证。

Conclusion: 通过语言空间中的 hindsight relabeling，VLA 模型的泛化能力使策略能从相同轨迹中提取更多学习信号，显著缓解稀疏奖励下 RL 后训练的样本效率瓶颈。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly used to post-train vision-language-action (VLA) models, but every update consumes robot rollouts that are slow and costly to collect, making sample efficiency a central concern. Manipulation tasks typically provide only sparse rewards, so a weak policy fails almost every rollout early in training and has little to learn from, even when those failures execute coherent behavior. Such a failure, however, is a success at a different task. We present Learning from Hindsight (LfH), which brings hindsight relabeling to RL post-training of VLAs by scoring failed rollouts against the tasks they actually achieved. A single vision-language model relabels both the instruction and the reward, proposing a hindsight instruction for a group of failed rollouts and scoring how well each satisfies it, and the policy trains on the relabeled and original rollouts jointly. Because VLAs generalize across language, relabeling in language lets the policy learn more from the same trajectories. On out-of-distribution LIBERO-PRO tasks, where standard RL improves only slowly, LfH achieves $5\times$ improvement in sample efficiency, and outperforms a dense progress-reward baseline. The gains hold across VLA backbones and on a physical Franka robot.

</details>


### [132] [COBS: Cumulant Order Block Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09052)
*Alexander Tian,Aditya Ghai,Sanjit Neelam,Zaal Vasania,Akshay Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COBS 提出一种基于累积量展开的二阶近似块稀疏注意力方法，通过对每个块存储压缩的二阶统计量来更准确地估计注意力质量（attention mass），在几乎不增加 KV 缓存读取开销的情况下，将 NSA 的长上下文检索性能大幅提升至接近稠密注意力的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块稀疏注意力是一种硬件友好的 KV 缓存瓶颈缓解方案，但在领先的开源 LLM 中并不流行。作者以 DeepSeek NSA 为代表性方法进行分析，发现其核心挑战在于块选择阶段——需要从紧凑摘要中近似每个块的注意力质量（attention mass），而现有方法受限于一阶近似，导致选择不够准确。

Method: 作者首先将块选择问题形式化为按注意力质量（块内注意力分数之和）对块进行排序；随后通过累积量展开（cumulant expansion）揭示现有方法仅使用一阶近似估计注意力质量的局限性；在此基础上提出 COBS，在 NSA 架构上引入新型选择器，为每个块存储压缩的二阶统计量，从而更精确地近似注意力质量以指导块选择。

Result: 在 32k RULER 长上下文检索基准上，COBS 将 NSA 基线的平均分从 0.2999 提升至 0.8195（稠密注意力为 0.9040），弥补了约 86% 的差距；KV 缓存读取开销仅为 NSA 基线的 1.21 倍、稠密注意力的 1/15.15；同时保持短上下文性能，且位置级负对数似然（NLL）优于稠密注意力。

Conclusion: 通过引入二阶统计量来近似注意力质量，COBS 证明了块稀疏注意力可以在极低的 KV 缓存读取开销下逼近稠密注意力的质量，为块稀疏注意力在开源 LLM 中的广泛应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Block sparse attention is a hardware friendly way to alleviate the key-value (KV) cache read bottleneck in large language models (LLMs). However, it is not prevalent among leading open-weight LLMs, which rely instead on dense attention or fine-grained selection, thereby motivating our analysis. We study DeepSeek's Native Sparse Attention (NSA) as a representative method, whose three-branch design lets us isolate block selection, the most challenging and consequential stage. We formalize selection and reduce it to ranking blocks by a single quantity, the attention mass: the sum of a block's attention scores. We show that if selection retrieves the blocks with the largest attention mass, block sparse attention can match the quality of dense attention. However, computing the exact attention mass requires reading every key, so the problem of block selection ultimately reduces to approximating this mass from a compact summary instead of the full keys. Via a cumulant expansion, we show why existing methods falter: their selection strategies attempt to estimate the attention mass, but are confined to a first-order approximation. Therefore, we propose COBS (Cumulant Order Block Sparse Attention), an attention method that builds on NSA, incorporating a novel selector that stores a compressed second-order statistic per block. On the 32k RULER long-context retrieval benchmark, COBS raises the NSA baseline's mean score from 0.2999 to 0.8195, approaching dense attention at 0.9040 and closing about 86% of the gap, while using only 1.21x the KV cache read traffic of the NSA baseline and 15.15x less read traffic than dense. The same model preserves short-context behavior and attains lower position-wise negative log-likelihood (NLL) than dense attention in our comparison.

</details>


### [133] [EvoLP: Self-Evolving Latency Predictor for Model Compression in Real-Time Edge Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09063)
*Shuo Huai,Hao Kong,Shiqing Li,Xiangzhong Luo,Ravi Subramaniam,Christian Makaya,Qian Lin,Weichen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvoLP 是一个新型高效框架，用于准确预测边缘设备上深度学习模型的推理延迟，并能在网络压缩过程中持续进化以提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备资源有限且对实时性要求高，需要面向延迟目标的神经网络压缩，但直接在真实设备上测量延迟成本高、难度大，因此需要一个高精度的延迟预测器来指导压缩过程。

Method: 提出 EvoLP 框架，构建一个可进化的推理延迟预测器。该预测器能够在网络压缩过程中不断更新、进化，从而逐步提高延迟预测的精确度，并将预测结果反馈至压缩流程中以指导模型压缩。

Result: 在三种边缘设备和四种模型变体上的实验评估表明，EvoLP 在延迟预测精度上优于现有最先进方法；将其集成到模型压缩框架中后，能在满足严格延迟约束的前提下，引导压缩过程获得更高的模型精度。

Conclusion: EvoLP 是一种有效且实用的边缘设备推理延迟预测方案，兼顾高精度与可进化性，能显著提升延迟约束下模型压缩的效果。代码已开源。

Abstract: Edge devices are increasingly utilized for deploying deep learning applications on embedded systems. The real-time nature of many applications and the limited resources of edge devices necessitate latency-targeted neural network compression. However, measuring latency on real devices is challenging and expensive. Therefore, this letter presents a novel and efficient framework, named EvoLP, to accurately predict the inference latency of models on edge devices. This predictor can evolve to achieve higher latency prediction precision during the network compression process. Experimental results demonstrate that EvoLP outperforms previous state-of-the-art approaches by being evaluated on three edge devices and four model variants. Moreover, when incorporated into a model compression framework, it effectively guides the compression process for higher model accuracy while satisfying strict latency constraints. We open source EvoLP at https://github.com/ntuliuteam/EvoLP.

</details>


### [134] [Pitfalls and Remedies for Multi-Task Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09073)
*Carl Hvarfner,Sam Daulton,Max Balandat,Eytan Bakshy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 多任务高斯过程在贝叶斯优化迁移学习中存在相关矩阵估计失效问题，源于逐任务标准化和对数边际似然的有限样本偏差。作者提出三种保守补救措施：将均值和尺度提升为模型参数、约束任务协方差为非负、以及共置部分源与目标设计点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化中，多任务高斯过程(MTGP)是热启动目标实验的标准替代模型，但作者发现即使在最简单的仿射相关任务场景下，MTGP也会错误估计跨任务相关性，导致迁移学习失效。这揭示了该默认方法的根本缺陷，需要深入分析失效机制并提出改进方案。

Method: 在受控合成环境中系统分析MTGP的两类失效机制：(1)逐任务标准化（应对仿射切片歧义的标准方案）将有限样本对齐误差传播至相关性估计；(2)对数边际似然本身仅以逐样本速率识别相关性，且非重叠设计进一步稀释该信号。基于此提出三种补救措施：将逐任务均值和方差提升为显式模型参数、将任务协方差矩阵约束为非负相关、在源和目标设计中引入共置点。

Result: 在合成多任务问题和基于代理的超参数调优迁移实验中，三种补救措施在简单实例上能够恢复至仅用目标数据的基线水平，但在更困难的实例以及大多数基于排序和隐式上下文变体上，根本性失效依然存在。

Conclusion: 多任务高斯过程在仿射相关任务的迁移学习中存在结构性缺陷，所提出的保守补救措施仅能缓解简单场景下的问题，更广泛的失效表明需要超越传统MTGP框架的新方法。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization routinely warm-starts a target experiment with data from related source tasks, and the multi-task Gaussian process is the textbook surrogate for the job. We revisit this default in a controlled setting and find that it misestimates the cross-task correlation even in the simplest non-trivial case, affinely related source and target tasks, where a working transfer learning method should obviously succeed. We trace the failure to two independent structural mechanisms. Per-task standardization, the textbook fix for the affine slice ambiguity, propagates a finite-sample alignment error into the recovered correlation. The marginal likelihood itself identifies the correlation only at a per-sample rate that a Gaussian process at non-overlapping designs further dilutes. We propose three conservative remedies that follow from the analysis: promoting per-task means and scales to model parameters, restricting the task covariance to non-negative correlations, and co-locating part of the source and target designs. Across synthetic multi-task problems and surrogate-based hyperparameter tuning transfer, these remedies recover the target-only baseline on the simple instances, while the broader failure persists on harder instances and across most rank-based and latent-context variants.

</details>


### [135] [A Survey on the Green Development of Large Models: From Resource-Efficient Architectures to Hardware-Software Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09084)
*Linhui Xiao,Guiping Cao,Mingyue Guo,Xianchao Guan,Fan Yang,Ming Tao,Xin Li,Yuxin Peng,Yaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了大模型绿色发展的全栈式策略，涵盖高效模型构建（注意力优化、线性复杂度架构、稀疏化与合并）、训练部署（数据高效学习、参数高效微调、计算压缩）、能效硬件（AI芯片、内存优化、跨平台部署）以及在可持续发展关键领域的应用，最后指出持续学习、内存中心化硬件和标准化评估等未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模AI模型虽取得突破性性能，但其巨大的计算成本、能源消耗和环境影响已成为不可忽视的问题。本文旨在为构建可持续、可扩展且对社会负责的大模型发展路径提供全面的路线图。

Method: 这是一篇系统综述，采用全栈式硬件-软件协同设计的视角。在算法层面梳理了注意力算子优化、线性复杂度架构、模型稀疏化与合并、数据高效学习、参数高效微调及计算压缩等技术；在硬件层面分析了主流AI芯片、内存优化、跨平台部署与可持续基础设施；并考察了大模型在遥感解译、国家级基础设施等可持续关键领域的应用案例。

Result: 综述揭示了当前绿色大模型发展的技术全景：算法侧已涌现出多种降低计算复杂度的方案，硬件侧在专用AI芯片和内存优化方面取得进展，软硬件协同设计成为提升能效的核心范式。同时，大模型正被应用于可持续性关键领域，形成双向赋能关系。

Conclusion: 大模型的绿色发展需要在算法、硬件和系统层面协同推进。未来应重点关注持续学习范式、以内存为中心的硬件架构，以及建立标准化的评估协议，以此确保大模型向可持续、可扩展和负社会责任的方向演进。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of large-scale AI models has led to significant performance breakthroughs across diverse domains, yet it has also raised critical concerns regarding computational costs, energy consumption, and environmental sustainability. This survey provides a comprehensive overview of the green development of large models, emphasizing resource-efficient architectures and full-stack hardware-software co-design. We systematically review recent advances in efficient model construction, including attention operator optimization, linear-complexity architectures, and model sparsification and merging, as well as training and deployment strategies such as data-efficient learning, parameter-efficient fine-tuning, and computational compression. Beyond algorithmic improvements, we explore energy-efficient AI hardware, including mainstream AI chips, memory optimization, cross-platform deployment, and sustainable infrastructure. Furthermore, we examine how large models are being applied to sustainability-critical domains such as DeepSeek, remote sensing interpretation, national-scale infrastructure, and global initiatives. Finally, we discuss key challenges and future directions, highlighting the need for continual learning paradigms, memory-centric hardware, and standardized evaluation protocols. This survey aims to offer a holistic roadmap toward sustainable, scalable, and socially responsible development of large models. Paper homepage: https://cje.ejournal.org.cn/article/doi/10.23919/cje.2025.00.438

</details>


### [136] [EXHOLD: Experience-Aware Real-Time Hold Control for Large-Scale Ride-Hailing Matching at DiDi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09090)
*Xu Liu,Kai Wan,Zihao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EXHOLD是一个两阶段框架，将体验感知的订单配对评估与hold-time执行解耦：第一阶段学习决策模型将司机-订单对分配到离散体验层级，第二阶段通过约束优化求解单调hold-time调度表。该框架已在滴滴巴西生产环境中部署，A/B实验显示其在行程完成率、司机收入、乘客取消率等方面均有显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业界hold策略通常依赖多个预测模型上的启发式阈值，在非平稳交通条件下表现脆弱，且难以针对多目标体验信号进行优化。需要一个更鲁棒、可优化的hold控制框架。

Method: 提出EXHOLD两阶段框架：(1) Stage I：学习决策模型，通过优化聚合匹配漏斗中各满意度信号的统一目标，将每个司机-订单对分配到离散、可解释的体验层级；(2) Stage II：通过对经验分位数的约束优化求解单调hold-time调度表，显式施加服务约束以限制对高潜力匹配的不必要延迟，同时最大化整体体验提升。

Result: 在滴滴巴西生产系统中通过随机A/B实验验证：EXHOLD提升了行程完成率和司机收入，显著降低了乘客取消率，改善了漏斗效率。消融实验和行为分析证实两个阶段均不可或缺，且策略在时空异质性条件下能做出校准的决策。

Conclusion: EXHOLD是一个可部署的hold控制框架，通过解耦评估与执行，在保持服务约束的同时实现了多目标体验优化。该系统已在巴西生产流量中实际部署运行。

Abstract: In large-scale ride-hailing, hold control is a critical mechanism for improving passenger-driver experience. By selectively delaying certain driver-order pairs, the system waits for better opportunities, reduces cancellations, and mitigates wasted driver effort. However, existing industrial hold strategies often rely on heuristic thresholding over multiple predictive models, which can be brittle under non-stationary traffic and hard to optimize for multi-objective experience signals.
  We propose EXHOLD, a deployable two-stage framework decoupling experience-aware pair assessment from hold-time execution. In Stage I, we learn a decision model assigning each driver-order pair to discrete, interpretable experience tiers by optimizing a unified objective that aggregates satisfaction signals across the matching funnel. In Stage II, we solve for a monotone hold-time schedule via constrained optimization over empirical quantiles. This explicitly enforces service guardrails bounding the unnecessary holding of promising matches while maximizing overall experience improvement.
  We evaluate EXHOLD through randomized A/B experiments in DiDi's production system in Brazil. Results show consistent gains in marketplace efficiency and experience: EXHOLD increases trip completion and driver income, significantly reduces passenger cancellations, and improves funnel efficiency. Ablations and behavioral analyses confirm both stages are essential and that the policy makes calibrated decisions under spatiotemporal heterogeneity. EXHOLD is currently deployed, serving production traffic in Brazil.

</details>


### [137] [Quantum Circuits in Diffusion Models: A Fair-Comparison Study and a Mechanistic Analysis of Angle-Embedding Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09108)
*Jaeuk Kim,Sanghoon Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究将变分量子电路（VQC）通过挤压-激励通道调制支架集成到扩散模型中。通过角色匹配的经典对照实验和多样本显著性检验，发现量子核心在DDPM和潜在扩散模型上达到与经典对照相当的FID，但未展现出参数效率优势。此外，识别出在基于分数的NCSN中，无界分数目标导致角度嵌入超出旋转门周期，引发相位混叠和量子调制器崩溃，并提出有界变换方案修复此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索变分量子电路能否以参数高效的方式增强扩散生成模型，并通过公平比较协议确定量子组件在生成建模中的实际贡献，同时揭示量子-经典混合架构中角度嵌入的结构性失效模式。

Method: 采用挤压-激励（SE）通道调制支架将VQC隔离为可插拔模块；使用角色匹配的经典控制（保持功能角色、移除量子操作）；在DDPM和潜在扩散模型（MNIST、CIFAR-10）以及基于分数的NCSN（MNIST）上进行多样本显著性检验和配对采样种子测试；针对NCSN中的无界分数目标提出有界变换 θ←π·tanh(·) 将输入映射到非混叠域。

Result: 1) 量子核心在DDPM和潜在扩散上达到与角色匹配经典控制相当的FID均值，EfficientSU2的配对检验未检测到统计显著差异；2) 量子核心参数量比角色匹配控制少4.5-9倍，但参数匹配的经典控制也能达到相当FID，未能建立量子参数效率优势；3) NCSN中无界分数目标导致角度嵌入超出2π周期引发相位混叠和调制器崩溃，有界变换显著改善量子核心性能。所有实验均在经典模拟的小量子比特规模上进行，未声明量子优势。

Conclusion: 量子核心在扩散模型中可与经典控制性能持平但未展现参数效率优势；基于分数的扩散模型中角度嵌入存在根本性的相位混叠失效问题但可通过有界变换修复；该研究提供了一个量子增强生成模型的公平比较协议和角度嵌入失效的机制性解释，而非量子优势声明。

Abstract: We study the integration of variational quantum circuits (VQCs) into diffusion models through a squeeze-and-excitation (SE) channel-modulation scaffold that isolates the quantum contribution. Using a role-matched classical control and multi-seed significance testing across DDPM and latent diffusion on MNIST and CIFAR-10, with a score-based NCSN study on MNIST, we find that quantum cores achieve comparable mean FID to the classical control across DDPM and latent diffusion, while paired sampling-seed tests for EfficientSU2 detect no statistically significant difference. Although the quantum cores use $4.5$--$9\times$ fewer core parameters than the role-matched control, parameter-matched classical controls attain comparable mean FID, so the experiments do not establish a quantum parameter-efficiency advantage. We further identify a structural failure in score-based NCSN: the unbounded score target, proportional to $1/σ$, drives angle-embedding inputs far beyond the $2π$ period of rotation gates, causing phase aliasing and collapse of the quantum modulator. A bounding transformation, $θ\leftarrow π\tanh(\cdot)$, maps inputs to the non-aliasing domain and substantially improves both quantum cores. Since all circuits are classically simulated at a few-qubit scale, we do not claim quantum advantage. Instead, the study provides a fair-comparison protocol for quantum-enhanced generative models and a mechanistic account of when and why angle embeddings fail.

</details>


### [138] [Power Flow Feasibility Assessment Using Variational Graph Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09122)
*Ferran Bohigas-Daranas,Hamid Latif-Martinez,Eduardo Prieto-Araujo,Pere Barlet-Ros,Oriol Gomis-Bellmunt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种变分图自编码器（VGAE），用于检测电力潮流计算的解可行性，以评估AI驱动求解器所提供解的有效性，并在IEEE 118节点系统上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来基于图神经网络等数据驱动方法虽被广泛研究用于加速潮流计算，但几乎无人关注解的可行性问题——即解是否存在（潮流是否收敛），而传统求解器天然能判断这一点。AI驱动求解器缺乏对解可行性的检测能力，这在实际应用中存在风险。

Method: 采用变分图自编码器（Variational Graph Autoencoder, VGAE），以IEEE 118节点系统为例，对电力潮流解的可行性进行二分类检测。VGAE利用图结构编码电网拓扑，通过编码器-解码器架构学习节点特征的低维潜在表示，进而判断给定运行条件下潮流解是否存在。

Result: 在IEEE 118节点标准测试系统上进行了验证，表明VGAE能够有效检测潮流解的可行性，为评估AI驱动求解器输出结果的可靠性提供了依据。

Conclusion: VGAE为AI驱动的潮流计算提供了一种关键的可行性校验机制，弥补了现有数据驱动方法缺乏解存在性判断的不足，有助于提升AI求解器在实际电力系统应用中的可信度。

Abstract: Data-driven methods, including graph neural networks, have been studied for accelerating power flow calculations in recent years, but very little attention has been paid to the solution feasibility, which can be obtained by traditional solvers. This paper presents a Variational Graph Autoencoder (VGAE) that detects the power flow solution feasibility, using the IEEE 118-bus case, to assess the validity of the solutions provided by AI-driven solvers.

</details>


### [139] [Present but Rescaled: Chat-to-Agent Transfer of Additive Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09156)
*Lucas Pinto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究了加性激活引导从单轮对话到ReAct智能体的迁移，发现引导的表示强度基本保持，但行为耦合在不同模型间差异巨大——有的放大、有的衰减，且该效应归因于ReAct格式脚手架而非工具观察边界，对AI安全部署有直接影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加性激活引导（additive activation steering）目前几乎完全在单轮对话场景中校准，但其目标模型正越来越多地被部署为使用工具的ReAct智能体。论文旨在系统性地研究从chat到agent场景的迁移效应，以揭示部署环境变化对引导行为效果和安全性的影响。

Method: 采用配对信息设计（matched-information design）：将相同内容分别呈现为普通对话或ReAct工具使用片段；每轮重新编码对话记录以排除KV缓存污染；使用匹配范数的随机方向作为对照；对三个模型家族（Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma-2-9B、Yi-1.5-9B）进行系统性测试，结合行为测量和表示读出（representation read-out）；采用两项预注册工具定位效应来源。

Result: 注入方向到达后期层的强度在各设置和模型间几乎不变（agent/chat比率0.83-1.16），但行为耦合因模型和上下文而异：Qwen2.5-7B上拒绝绕过向量在agent中放大（T=1.45），Gemma-2-9B放大（T=2.00），Yi-1.5-9B衰减（T=0.43）；方向消融不产生放大而加性注入产生放大，增益差20.1个百分点；效应定位于ReAct格式脚手架而非工具观察边界。

Conclusion: 加性引导的安全含义即时且不可预测：agent部署在某些模型上可将基于引导的拒绝绕过放大至2倍，而在其他模型上衰减，因此不能假设某模型在加性引导下是安全的。效应来自加性注入特有机制，与ReAct格式本身相关。
