Abstract: Additive activation steering (injecting a scaled residual-stream direction during generation) is calibrated almost entirely in single-turn chat, yet the models it targets are increasingly deployed as tool-using ReAct agents. We present the first systematic chat-to-agent transfer study of additive steering, coupling behavioral measurement with a representation read-out in a matched-information design: the same items rendered as plain chat or as a ReAct tool-use episode, with matched-norm random-direction controls and the transcript re-encoded every turn to exclude KV-cache contamination. Transfer is real but rescaled, and the right description is a dissociation: the injected direction reaches the late layers at near-full strength in every setting and model tested (install-site agent-over-chat ratios 0.83-1.16 across three families), while the behavioral coupling is reset per model and context. On Qwen2.5-7B a refusal bypass vector amplifies in the agent (T = 1.45, CI [1.20, 1.78], N = 300); across a powered uniform-protocol distribution the coupling spans amplification (Gemma-2-9B T = 2.00) to attenuation (Yi-1.5-9B T = 0.43, CI [0.29, 0.60]), with no universal constant and a single clean attenuator against a universal sign. Directional ablation of the same axis does not amplify (T = 0.93, CI including 1) while additive injection amplifies (T = 1.50), a 20.1-point gain difference (CI [13.4, 26.8]) that identifies an additive-specific mechanism. Two pre-registered instruments converge to localize the rescaling to the ReAct format scaffold, before any tool observation, rather than to the observation boundary where a dilution account would predict it. The safety implication is immediate and unpredictable: agentic deployment amplifies steering-based refusal bypass by up to 2.00x on some models while others attenuate, so a deployment cannot assume a given model is safe under additive steering.

</details>


### [140] [A Personalized Computational Framework for Assessing the Sufficiency of Partially Observed Data in Healthcare AI models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09165)
*Qingchu Jin,Felistas Mazhude,Jamie B. Rabb,Robert S. Kramer,Douglas B. Sawyer,Raimond L. Winslow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出特征充分性分析(FSA)方法,用于判断不完整的临床特征子集是否足以达到使用全部特征时的预测性能(即全特征容量,FFC),从而在临床数据缺失的情况下指导是否需要额外采集数据,促进医疗AI系统的前瞻性部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实临床场景中,机器学习模型所需的所有特征往往无法同时获得,数据缺失普遍存在。当前缺乏系统性的方法来评估现有不完整特征集是否已足够支撑可信的AI预测,导致要么盲目预测,要么不必要的额外检测。

Method: 定义全特征容量(FFC)概念作为基准;提出FSA方法,通过估计缺失变量在已观测特征条件下的分布,对每位患者进行个体化评估,判断现有特征子集是否达到FFC。FSA还可衍生出基于预测充分性的可解释特征排序、识别难以预测的患者亚群,以及实现数据采集的成本感知优化。

Result: 在两个临床案例中验证:心脏术后延长通气预测和门诊患者10年死亡率预测。FSA能有效判断特征充分性,提供可解释的特征重要性排序,并识别出内在难以预测的患者群体。

Conclusion: FSA为评估不完整临床信息是否足以支撑可信的AI辅助决策提供了通用计算框架,有助于医疗AI系统在多样化临床环境中的前瞻性部署。

Abstract: Achieving early and timely diagnosis and treatment for disease is a major challenge. Recent applications of machine learning (ML) algorithms trained on patient data have shown promise in many different settings for predicting the patient health state. A challenge often faced when applying these ML algorithms is that at any given time, not all clinical variables (features) needed as input to perform prediction tasks are available. We define the concept of full-feature-capacity (FFC) to refer to prediction performance when such algorithms make use of all features on which they were trained. We then introduce Feature Sufficiency Analysis (FSA) - an analysis for determining whether a subset of all clinical features needed by an AI model is sufficient to achieve FFC. FSA estimates the underlying distributions of missing variables conditioned on features that are available. FSA provides a patient-specific assessment of whether the existing set of measured features achieves FFC. If yes, then there is no need to acquire further inputs and a ML-based prediction. We provide two case studies: prediction of need for postoperative prolonged ventilation in patients recovering from heart surgery; 10-year mortality prediction in an outpatient cohort. We also demonstrate that FSA also provides a clinically interpretable feature-ranking methodology based on prediction sufficiency, identifies intrinsically hard-to-predict patient populations, and has the potential to perform cost-aware optimization for clinical data acquisition. FSA provides a generic computational approach for determining whether incomplete clinical information is sufficient to support trustworthy AI-assisted clinical decision-making, thereby facilitating the prospective deployment of healthcare AI systems across diverse clinical settings.

</details>


### [141] [COAST: Context-Aware Differential Learning for Gene Expression Prediction in Spatial Transcriptomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09166)
*Keunho Byeon,Sunhong Park,Jeewoo Lim,Jin Tae Kwak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COAST 提出了一种上下文感知的差分学习框架，通过联合优化绝对表达回归与目标-上下文斑点间的符号差分回归，从 H&E 图像预测空间基因表达，在多个数据集上取得了一致性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间转录组学预测方法主要监督绝对表达水平，忽略了空间斑点之间相对表达关系这一重要信息。高成本、低通量的空间转录组学技术亟需从廉价的 H&E 组织病理图像中更准确地预测基因表达。

Method: COAST 框架包含三个核心设计：(1) 利用类型特异性调制（type-specific modulation）对局部和全局上下文特征进行条件化处理；(2) 使用 Transformer 编码器聚合目标斑点与上下文斑点的 token，同时捕捉细粒度局部模式和切片级全局结构；(3) 设计联合损失函数，将绝对表达回归与目标-上下文斑点间的符号差分回归相结合进行训练。

Result: 在多个空间转录组学数据集上的实验表明，COAST 在基于相关性和基于分布的多种评估指标上均取得了一致性的性能改进。

Conclusion: 上下文感知的差分学习是组织病理学图像预测空间基因表达的有效策略，显式建模斑点间相对表达关系能够显著提升预测性能。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics enables profiling of spatial gene expression but is limited by high cost and low throughput, motivating prediction from H&E histopathology images. Existing context-aware methods mainly supervise absolute expression, while relative expression relationships between spots are rarely used explicitly. We propose COAST, a context-aware differential learning framework for spatial gene expression prediction. COAST conditions the local and global context features with type-specific modulation and aggregates the target and context spot tokens using a Transformer encoder to capture both fine-grained local patterns and slide-level structure. It is trained with a joint objective that combines absolute expression regression with signed differential regression between the target and context spots. Experiments on multiple spatial transcriptomics datasets show consistent improvements in correlation- and distribution-based metrics, demonstrating the effectiveness of context-aware differential learning for histology-based spatial gene expression prediction.

</details>


### [142] [Understanding Schedule-Free Methods in Nonconvex Optimization: Rate Guarantees and Escaping Saddles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09167)
*Jiseok Chae,Donghwan Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对Schedule-Free方法在非凸光滑优化中的收敛性进行了理论分析，证明Schedule-Free梯度下降及其随机版本均达到了最优的最坏情况收敛速率，并进一步证明了该方法在任意小的一次性扰动下可避免严格鞍点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schedule-Free方法在实证中表现出色，能够媲美甚至超越经过调参的学习率调度器，但其在非凸优化（现代机器学习目标的典型场景）中的收敛理论一直未被探索。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 基于与Schedule-Free方法相关联的连续时间极限常微分方程，构建Lyapunov分析框架；将Schedule-Free梯度下降形式化为非自治动力系统，并在此基础上进行严格鞍点回避分析。

Result: （1）Schedule-Free梯度下降和Schedule-Free随机梯度下降在光滑非凸目标下均达到一阶方法中最优的最坏情况收敛速率；（2）Schedule-Free梯度下降在任意小的一次性扰动下具有严格鞍点回避性质。

Conclusion: 本文的理论结果为Schedule-Free方法在实践中表现出的强大性能提供了更深入的理论理解，揭示了其收敛性和鞍点回避能力。

Abstract: Schedule-Free methods have attracted growing interest for alleviating the burden of designing and tuning a learning rate scheduler, while matching and sometimes even outperforming optimizers with tuned schedulers. Despite their strong empirical results, their convergence theory in nonconvex optimization, where modern machine learning objectives typically arise, has remained largely unexplored. In this paper, we provide worst-case analyses of Schedule-Free gradient descent and Schedule-Free stochastic gradient descent, in their standard form and without auxiliary modifications or restrictive conditions, for smooth but possibly nonconvex objectives. Based on a Lyapunov analysis derived from the continuous-time limiting ordinary differential equation associated with these methods, we show that Schedule-Free gradient descent and Schedule-Free stochastic gradient descent achieve the optimal worst-case convergence rates attainable among first-order methods. We further formulate Schedule-Free gradient descent as a nonautonomous dynamical system and prove strict-saddle avoidance under an arbitrarily small one-time perturbation. These theoretical results provide a better understanding of the strong performance that Schedule-Free methods demonstrate.

</details>


### [143] [Application of machine learning to monster level prediction in tabletop RPG game design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09196)
*Jolanta Śliwa,Jakub Adamczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将桌面角色扮演游戏中怪物等级预测任务形式化为表格型有序回归问题，构建首个Pathfinder第二版怪物数据集，系统比较多种回归与有序分类方法，发现基于树的集成模型表现最优，且模型解释与游戏规则和人类直觉高度一致，证明机器学习可有效辅助游戏平衡设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在TTRPG（如Pathfinder）开发中，设计平衡的怪物是一项核心但耗时费力的工作。每个怪物由大量数值属性描述，这些属性共同决定其强度（以有序等级表示）。研究者希望探索机器学习是否能根据怪物属性自动预测等级，从而辅助设计师决策，减轻人工负担。

Method: 将任务建模为表格型有序回归。构建首个专门针对TTRPG怪物等级预测的数据集（源自Pathfinder第二版公开数据）。系统比较三类方法：带舍入方案的经典回归模型、专用表格有序回归算法、以及使用有序感知损失的神经网络。采用时间顺序和扩展窗口两种评估协议，配合多种互补指标模拟真实设计工作流。并利用可解释AI（特征重要性、误差分布）分析模型行为。

Result: 基于树的集成模型（如梯度提升树）显著优于线性模型和神经网络方法，在有排序指标上接近完美，预测准确率高。可解释性分析表明模型与人类直觉一致，其判断模式遵循游戏内在规则。

Conclusion: 机器学习能够可靠地逼近设计师的评判标准，可作为有效的计算机辅助工具用于怪物平衡及更广泛的TTRPG系统设计。

Abstract: Designing balanced adversaries is a central but labor-intensive task in tabletop role-playing game (TTRPG) development. In systems such as Pathfinder, each monster is described by many numerical attributes that jointly determine its power, summarized as an ordinal level. We investigate whether machine learning can support designers by predicting this level from a monster's attributes, framing the task as tabular ordinal regression. We introduce what is, to our knowledge, the first dataset built specifically for TTRPG monster-level prediction, derived from publicly available Pathfinder Second Edition data. Using it, we compare classical regression models with rounding schemes, dedicated tabular ordinal regression algorithms, and neural networks with ordinal-aware losses. To mirror real design workflows, we evaluate all models under chronological and expanding-window protocols with several complementary metrics. Results show that tree-based ensembles outperform linear models and neural approaches, achieving near-perfect ordinal ranking and high predictive accuracy. Explainable AI analyses, such as feature importance and error distributions, show that the model is aligned with human intuition and follows patterns grounded in game rules. Together, these results show that machine learning can reliably approximate designer judgments and serve as an effective computer-aided tool for monster balancing and broader TTRPG system design.

</details>


### [144] [Interference and Retention in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09202)
*Julius Störk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将遗忘直接建模为任务间干扰，通过路径平均曲率量化深层网络中的干扰能量，进而推导出IGFA方法——一种无重放、无Fisher的持续学习方法，在任务可分离时实现无损保留，在任务冲突时将不可逆遗忘转化为可恢复的可塑性损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法依赖重放、弹性正则化或蒸馏等事后机制，缺乏对遗忘本质的直接建模。作者认为遗忘应被理解为任务间干扰，通过直接建模干扰来从根本上解决灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 首先在冻结特征机制下，将遗忘量化为新任务对旧任务的干扰能量；在深层网络中通过路径平均曲率以最少额外前向传播恢复该量。当任务支撑集不相交时可结构性地消除遗忘；当任务在冲突方向上重叠时存在不可消除的失真下界。基于此几何分析，提出干扰门控功能分配（IGFA）方法：任务对齐时共享方向，冲突时保护方向，无需重放也无需Fisher信息。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，IGFA在任务结构可分离时实现无损保留；在任务不可分离时，将不可避免的代价从不可逆遗忘转化为可推迟但可恢复的可塑性损失。在异质任务流上匹配最强重放无关的结构化基线，在相似性使迁移值得保留时优于无条件投影方法。

Conclusion: 将遗忘建模为任务间干扰为持续学习提供了新的几何视角。IGFA通过任务感知正交化实现方向共享与保护，将遗忘重塑为可恢复的可塑性损失，为无重放持续学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Continual learning commonly relies on post-hoc mechanisms such as replay, elastic regularization, or distillation. This work argues that forgetting should instead be modeled directly as interference between tasks. In the frozen-feature regime, forgetting from learning a new task is exactly the interference energy induced on the old task. In deep networks, the same quantity is recovered through path-averaged curvature with minimal additional forward passes.
  When task supports are disjoint, forgetting can be eliminated structurally and when task supports overlap in conflicting directions, a non-zero distortion floor is unavoidable. The same geometry optimally merges models through task-aware orthogonalization. From this analysis we derive Interference-Gated Functional Allocation (IGFA), a replay-free, Fisher-free method that shares directions when tasks align and protects them when they conflict. Across benchmarks, IGFA achieves lossless retention when tasks are structurally separable and moves unavoidable cost from irreversible forgetting into deferred but recoverable plasticity when they are not. It matches the strongest replay-free structural baselines on dissimilar-task streams and improves on unconditional projection when similarity makes transfer worth preserving.

</details>


### [145] [Temporal Knowledge Graph Forecasting under Distribution Shifts: A Synthetic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09232)
*Konrad Özdemir,Julia Gastinger,Lukas Kirchdorfer,Heiner Stuckenschmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过合成TKG生成器研究时序知识图谱预测模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性，发现模型鲁棒性高度依赖信号类型，其中潜在实体社区结构偏移构成最强挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TKG预测模型仅在经验基准数据集上评估，无法揭示模型对时序分布偏移的鲁棒性。作者旨在通过受控偏移环境系统性地评估不同架构的适应能力与局限。

Method: 设计一个合成TKG生成器，编码三种数据生成机制：递归性（recurrence）、同质性（homophily）和周期性（periodicity）。在此生成器上构建平稳与偏移两种体制，对七种预测架构（包括简单记忆基线及复杂模型）进行受控评估。

Result: (1) 在平稳条件下，递归性和周期性规律基本可恢复，且当递归主导时简单记忆基线具有竞争力；(2) 结构性断裂暴露模型自适应能力不足，其中潜在实体社区结构的偏移构成最强挑战；(3) 鲁棒性高度依赖信号类型。

Conclusion: 当前TKG预测模型面对时序分布偏移时存在明显局限，尤其在结构偏移下自适应能力不足。该研究加深了对模型能力边界的理解，为未来鲁棒性TKG预测研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Temporal knowledge graphs (TKGs) represent evolving relational systems, whose underlying data-generating processes often change over time. Yet, TKG forecasting models are commonly evaluated only on empirical benchmark datasets that provide limited insight into the models' robustness to such distribution shifts. Recognising this issue, we study TKG forecasting under controlled shift environments using a synthetic TKG generator that encodes three temporal and structural properties -- recurrence, homophily, and periodicity -- as data-generating mechanisms. This allows us to evaluate seven forecasting architectures under stationary and shifting regimes. Our experiments suggest that robustness in TKG forecasting is highly signal-dependent. Recurrence-based and periodic regularities are largely recoverable under stationary conditions, and simple memory-based baselines can be competitive when recurrence dominates the data. However, structural breaks reveal limitations in model adaptivity, with shifts in latent entity-community structure posing the strongest challenge in our study. Overall, our findings improve the understanding of the capabilities and limitations of current TKG models confronted with temporal distribution shifts.

</details>


### [146] [All you need is SAMPAT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09235)
*Jayadeva,Madhur Aswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAMPAT 是一种三层神经网络架构，能够以封闭、紧凑的代数解析形式逼近任意光滑函数，从而提供完全的可解释性，同时保持有竞争力的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度神经网络普遍缺乏可解释性，这对需要从实验数据中获取科学洞见的场景构成了障碍。作者旨在设计一种既能保持强大函数逼近能力、又能输出可解释的闭式解析表达式的神经网络架构。

Method: 提出 SAMPAT（基于多元多项式与解析变换的光滑逼近），这是一种三层架构，可证明地学习连续且处处可微的函数。其核心思想是：通过限制神经元间的连接模式，可以导出不同类型的逼近器（如正则多项式、三角多项式、有理式、高斯函数、高斯混合及其任意组合）；不施加限制时，架构可自主学习合适结构。引入跳跃连接后，4至6层的 SAMPAT 可覆盖当前 AI/ML 中广泛使用的大量模型族，使得模型族的选择本身也可作为学习过程的一部分被优化。

Result: 在合成数据和基准数据集上的实验表明，SAMPAT 以更简洁的表示形式取得了有竞争力的性能。许多任务仅需两层 SAMPAT 即可胜任。此外，SAMPAT 还可用于多项式因式分解和非线性系统建模。

Conclusion: SAMPAT 成功实现了可解释性与预测性能的统一：其近似结果可表示为闭式代数解析式，同时通过结构约束和跳跃连接，能够灵活覆盖从简单多项式到复杂模型族的广泛函数类，使模型族与参数可被联合优化。

Abstract: The current state of the art in AI/ML rests on deep neural architectures, which, in general, suffer from a lack of interpretability. Interpretability is crucial to gleaning insights while analyzing experimental data, where quantitative predictions may not be adequate for a scientist. We present a three layer neural architecture, SAMPAT (Smooth Approximation via Multivariate Polynomials and Analytic Transformations), that can provably learn a continuous, everywhere differentiable function, that can approximate any smooth function arbitrarily closely. SAMPAT's approximant can be expressed as a closed and compact algebraic, analytic expression, providing complete interpretability. Experiments on synthetic and benchmark datasets indicate that SAMPAT yields competitive performance with simpler representations. For many tasks, a two layer SAMPAT suffices. By imposing restrictions on the connectivity between neurons, SAMPAT may be used to provide a range of approximants, including regular and trigonometric polynomials, rational expressions, Gaussians, mixtures of Gaussians, as well as arbitrary combinations of the same; without restrictions, it learns a suitable structure. SAMPAT may be used to factorize polynomials and model nonlinear systems. With the addition of skip connections, a 4 to 6 layer SAMPAT is adequate to represent a substantive range of methods widely used in AI/ML, allowing the choice of a model's family, not just its parameters, to also be optimized as part of the learning process.

</details>


### [147] [Risk-Aware General-Utility Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09298)
*Pedro P. Santos,Fábio Vital,Alberto Sardinha,Francisco S. Melo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有风险感知目标的通用效用马尔可夫决策过程（GUMDPs），提出使用熵风险度量（ERM）来权衡期望性能与风险厌恶，并设计了基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的在线规划算法，可在多种任务中实现可证明精度的风险感知策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的通用效用MDP框架允许灵活定义目标（如状态访问频率的函数），但缺乏对风险感知的考量。实际决策中，决策者往往需要在期望回报与风险之间进行权衡，因此需要将风险度量引入GUMDP框架，使智能体能够在追求丰富目标的同时具备风险厌恶能力。

Method: 首先形式化定义了风险感知GUMDP，将熵风险度量（ERM）作为目标函数引入。然后，提出一种基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的在线规划方法，通过构建搜索树并对状态-动作价值进行统计估计，可证明地求解风险感知GUMDP，达到任意所需精度。

Result: 在四类不同任务上进行了实验验证：标准MDP、最大状态熵探索、模仿学习以及多目标MDP。实验结果表明，该方法能够成功优化从风险中性到风险厌恶的多种风险感知行为谱系。

Conclusion: 风险感知GUMDP框架有效扩展了传统GUMDP的适用范围，结合ERM和MCTS的求解方法在理论和实践上均表现出色，为需要在丰富目标设定下进行风险权衡的决策问题提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We study general-utility Markov decision processes (GUMDPs) with risk-aware objectives. In this framework, an agent aims to optimize a risk measure of the distribution of objective values, where the objective function depends on the frequency of visitation of states induced by the agent's policy. First, we motivate, propose, and formalize risk-aware GUMDPs, which enable agents and decision makers to trade off expected performance by risk aversion while benefiting from the rich set of objectives that can be cast under the framework of GUMDPs. We focus our attention on the entropic risk measure (ERM). Second, we show how we can solve risk-aware GUMDPs with ERM objectives by resorting to online planning techniques. In particular, we propose an approach based on Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to provably solve risk-aware GUMDPs up to any desired accuracy. Third, we provide a set of experimental results showcasing that our approach is successful when optimizing for a spectrum of risk-aware behaviors in the context of GUMDPs under diverse tasks (standard MDPs, maximum state entropy exploration, imitation learning, and multi-objective MDPs).

</details>


### [148] [Forget Narrowly, Retain Broadly: Unlearning as an Asymmetric Generalization Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09236)
*Amit Peleg,Naman Deep Singh,Naama Pearl,Bibhabasu Mohapatra,Matthias Hein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有LLM机器遗忘评估存在"欠遗忘"（知识在转述查询下重现）和"过遗忘"（无关知识被误删）两大问题，根源在于评估未覆盖遗忘-保留边界。本文提出SUITE评估协议与训练语料，精确刻画该边界，并基于此开发JensUn++算法，在多个LLM上取得最优的遗忘-保留效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM机器遗忘基准不可靠：一是忽略知识在改写/间接查询下的重现（欠遗忘），二是缺乏对无关知识保留的细粒度语义、句法、词汇探测（过遗忘）。二者反映非对称泛化问题——遗忘评估需覆盖多样化查询表述，保留评估需探测隐式定义的庞大"所有不相交事实"集合。当前数据集缺乏对遗忘-保留边界的细粒度标注。

Method: 提出SUITE——一个评估协议与训练语料，在真实世界事实领域中捕获遗忘-保留结构。SUITE提供遗忘集与保留集之间的精确边界标注。基于SUITE训练的洞察，进一步提出JensUn++遗忘算法，支持顺序遗忘与联合遗忘两种设置。

Result: 使用SUITE训练的方法性能大幅提升，表明训练数据与算法设计同等重要。JensUn++在三个LLM上、顺序与联合遗忘两种设置下均取得最优的遗忘-保留效用权衡。

Conclusion: 可靠的遗忘评估必须覆盖非对称泛化：遗忘侧验证跨表述鲁棒性，保留侧验证大规模隐式定义集合的完整性。SUITE提供了这一评估基础设施，JensUn++验证了良好数据设计加算法创新可显著推进LLM机器遗忘水平。

Abstract: Machine unlearning in LLMs is the targeted removal of specific knowledge while preserving all other capabilities, critical for privacy and safety. Yet existing benchmarks measure it unreliably. They miss knowledge that resurfaces under paraphrased or indirect queries, a failure we call under-forgetting, and lack the semantic, syntactic, and lexical probes needed to verify that unrelated knowledge is preserved, a failure we call over-forgetting. Both failures reflect an asymmetric generalization problem. Forget evaluation must cover diverse query formulations of the same target facts, testing whether forgetting holds beyond exact training prompts. Retain evaluation must probe a far larger and implicitly defined set, namely every fact disjoint from the forget target. The retain set thus defines the effective forget set, yet current datasets provide no fine-grained annotation of this forget-retain boundary. We address this with SUITE, an evaluation protocol and training corpus that captures forget-retain structure for real-world factual domains. Methods trained on SUITE improve substantially, showing that training data is as important as algorithmic design. Building on the obtained insights, we introduce JensUn++, an unlearning algorithm that achieves the best forget-retain utility trade-off across three LLMs, in both sequential and joint unlearning settings. Code and datasets are available at https://amitpeleg.github.io/forget-narrowly-retain-broadly

</details>


### [149] [LionVote: Per-Layer Learning Rate Adaptation for Lion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09266)
*Kris Atallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Lion优化器存在跨层学习率不匹配问题：注意力/MLP层有效尺度偏高2.6-2.8倍，归一化层偏高约2倍，跨层差异达32%。作者提出LionVote——一种逐层学习率机制，通过梯度方向稳定性和动量健康两个诊断指标投票调整每层学习率，在ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100上达到69.7%准确率，优于Lion(69.0%)和AdamW(68.8%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现Lion优化器在ViT架构上存在显著的跨层学习率不匹配问题：注意力层和MLP层的有效学习率尺度比归一化层高出约32%，这一差异无法通过单一全局学习率修正。需要一种逐层自适应学习率机制来解决这种架构异质性带来的优化困难。

Method: 提出LionVote：一种逐层学习率机制，每个参数张量维护一个复合层级（compound level），即一个持久化整数。每c个epoch，通过两个诊断指标——梯度方向稳定性（gradient direction stability）和动量健康（momentum health）——进行投票，若平局则由验证损失作为仲裁。投票阈值由几何恒等式、EMA时间常数和噪声底限估计推导得出；更新节奏有结构上界约束并通过消融实验选定。

Result: 在ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100上，LionVote达到69.7%的top-1准确率，显著优于Lion的69.0%（Welch's t检验，p<0.02）和AdamW的68.8%。但在均匀CNN架构上，配合余弦退火的SGD仍占主导；在ViT架构上，逐层自适应的收益依赖于具体任务。

Conclusion: 逐层学习率自适应对架构异质性强的模型（如ViT）有显著价值，但其收益具有架构依赖性和任务依赖性——均匀CNN上SGD仍是最优选择，ViT上的增益则与具体任务相关。

Abstract: Per-layer diagnostics reveal that, at the prescribed learning rate, Lion's effective scale is 2.6-2.8x too high for attention and MLP parameters and ~2x too high for normalization layers on ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100; this 32% cross-layer-type disparity cannot be reproduced by a single global rate. The measurement comes from LionVote, a per-layer learning rate mechanism in which each parameter tensor maintains a compound level, a persistent integer updated every c epochs by two diagnostics (gradient direction stability and momentum health) resolved by a validation loss tiebreaker. Voting thresholds derive from geometric identities, the EMA time constant, and a noise-floor estimate; cadence is bounded structurally and selected by ablation. On ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100, LionVote achieves 69.7% top-1 accuracy vs. Lion's 69.0% (p < 0.02, Welch's t-test) and AdamW's 68.8%. Per-layer adaptation value depends on both architectural heterogeneity and task; on uniform CNN architectures tuned SGD with cosine annealing remains dominant, and on ViT architectures gains are task-dependent.

</details>


### [150] [Autoregressive latent diffusion for 3D molecule generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09277)
*Federico Ottomano,Gaopeng Ren,Yingzhen Li,Kim E. Jelfs,Alex M. Ganose*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KRONOS 提出了一种潜在自回归扩散框架，在预训练自编码器的潜在空间中联合建模分子图拓扑与几何结构，并通过 Fill-in-the-Middle 混合训练策略，在单一从左到右的自回归模型中同时支持无条件生成和片段条件生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的3D分子生成领域被扩散模型主导，但扩散模型需预先指定分子大小；自回归方法虽支持可变长度生成和部分分子上下文条件控制，但在无条件生成与条件生成之间难以取得平衡。KRONOS 旨在弥合这一差距，在单一架构中统一两种生成范式。

Method: KRONOS 采用潜在自回归扩散框架：(1) 利用预训练自编码器将分子压缩到潜在空间，在该空间中同时建模分子图的拓扑和3D几何结构；(2) 引入受 Fill-in-the-Middle (FIM) 范式启发的混合训练策略，使单一从左到右的自回归模型能同时处理无条件生成和片段条件生成。

Result: 在 QM9 和 GEOM-Drugs 数据集上的实验表明：KRONOS 在自回归方法中取得了领先的无条件生成性能，且与扩散模型保持竞争力；片段条件生成的引入对无条件生成性能影响可忽略不计，验证了单一架构可同时支持两种生成范式。

Conclusion: KRONOS 成功证明了潜在自回归扩散框架结合 FIM 风格混合训练，能够在单一模型中有效统一无条件分子生成与片段条件分子生成，在保持自回归方法灵活性优势的同时，实现了与扩散模型相竞争的性能。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) molecule generation has been dominated by diffusion models, which achieve strong generation quality but typically require the molecular size to be specified a priori. Recent autoregressive approaches have substantially narrowed the performance gap while naturally supporting variable-length generation and conditioning on partial molecular context. However, balancing unconditional and context-conditioned generation remains challenging. We introduce KRONOS, a latent autoregressive diffusion framework that generates molecules in the latent space of a pre-trained autoencoder, jointly modeling molecular graph topology and geometry, while retaining the flexibility of autoregressive generation. We further introduce a mixed training strategy inspired by Fill-in-the Middle (FIM) paradigm, enabling both unconditional and fragment-conditioned molecular generation within a single left-to-right autoregressive model. Experiments on QM9 and GEOM-Drugs demonstrate that KRONOS achieves leading unconditional generation performance among autoregressive methods, while remaining competitive with diffusion models. Moreover, fragment-conditioned generation is achieved with negligible impact on unconditional generation performance, demonstrating that both generation paradigms can be supported within a single architecture.

</details>


### [151] [Shortcut Trajectory Planning for Efficient Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09336)
*Guanquan Wang,Yoshimasa Tsuruoka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STP 提出了一种单阶段训练的捷径轨迹规划框架，通过步长条件实现可调节的一步或几步推理，结合可行性校正的critic选择候选计划，在简化训练流程的同时取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的轨迹规划器推理成本高，而基于一致性的规划器虽然减少了采样步数，但依赖两阶段教师-学生蒸馏，增加了训练成本且可能引入不稳定性。需要一种训练简单、推理快速且性能强的轨迹规划方法。

Method: 提出STP框架：(1) 在单阶段训练条件捷径轨迹模型，通过步长条件（step-size conditioning）支持可调节的一步和几步推理；(2) 使用带有可行性感知校正的critic（feasibility-aware correction）对候选规划进行选择，确保生成轨迹的可行性。

Result: 在D4RL标准基准上，涵盖 locomotion、navigation、manipulation 和 dexterous control 等任务，STP 取得了强性能，同时简化了快速生成式规划的训练流程。

Conclusion: STP 证明了单阶段捷径模型可以替代两阶段蒸馏范式，在离线强化学习的轨迹规划中实现高效推理（一步/几步）与强性能的统一，同时避免了教师-学生蒸馏带来的训练复杂性和不稳定性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based trajectory planners have shown strong performance in offline reinforcement learning, but their iterative denoising process often incurs high inference cost. Consistency-based planners reduce the number of sampling steps, yet they typically rely on a two-stage teacher--student distillation pipeline that increases training cost and may introduce instability. We propose Shortcut Trajectory Planning (STP), an offline model-based reinforcement learning framework that incorporates shortcut models as efficient trajectory generators. STP trains a conditional shortcut trajectory model in a single stage, supports adjustable one-step and few-step inference through step-size conditioning, and selects candidate plans using a critic augmented with feasibility-aware correction. Across standard D4RL benchmarks, including locomotion, navigation, manipulation, and dexterous control tasks, STP achieves strong performance while simplifying the training pipeline for fast generative planning.

</details>


### [152] [Graph Neural Networks for Scalable and Transferable Node Centrality Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09372)
*Samra Sana,Giorgio Mantica,Saul Imbrici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究使用消息传递GNN近似介数中心性和紧密度中心性，将其建模为节点排序问题。核心发现是：混合分布训练可提升GNN在不同图拓扑间的结构迁移能力，介数中心性迁移效果良好（τ最高0.938），但紧密度中心性对社区结构图敏感，向真实世界拓扑迁移仍具挑战。GNN推理相较精确计算实现最高97.7倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算介数中心性和紧密度中心性在大规模图上计算代价极高。现有GNN近似方法通常仅在与训练分布相同的图上表现良好，跨拓扑泛化能力不明。本文旨在探索消息传递GNN是否能学习可迁移的结构表征，而非仅拟合训练分布。

Method: 将中心性近似形式化为节点排序问题，以精确中心性值作为监督信号，使用Kendall's τ排序相关系数评估排序质量。在Erdős–Rényi、Barabási–Albert和Gaussian Random Partition三类合成图上进行训练与跨族测试，提出混合分布训练策略。模型规模扩展至N=5000节点的大图，并在推理速度上与精确算法对比。

Result: 在未见Erdős–Rényi图上，介数τ=0.851、紧密度τ=0.894；5000节点大图介数模型τ=0.938；混合分布训练改善介数跨图族迁移；紧密度对社区结构图敏感，向真实世界拓扑迁移较差；GNN推理相比精确计算加速达97.7倍。

Conclusion: 混合分布训练能有效提升GNN中心性近似的结构迁移能力，尤其对介数中心性效果显著；紧密度中心性的拓扑敏感性仍是开放挑战。GNN方案在保持较高质量的同时实现大幅加速，具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) provide a learning-based framework for approximating graph quantities that are expensive to compute exactly. This paper investigates GNNs for scalable approximation of betweenness and closeness centrality, formulated as a node-ranking problem. Exact centrality values are used as supervision, and ranking quality is evaluated using Kendall's tau rank correlation. We study whether message-passing GNNs can learn transferable structural representations across different graph topologies rather than only fitting the distribution used during training. On unseen Erdos renyi graphs, the proposed models achieve tau = 0.851 for betweenness and tau = 0.894 for closeness. A large-scale betweenness model trained on graphs with N = 5,000 nodes achieves tau = 0.938, demonstrating scalability. Mixed-distribution training on Erdos renyi, Barabasi-Albert, and Gaussian Random Partition graphs improves betweenness transfer across graph families. In contrast, closeness centrality remains more sensitive to community-structured graphs and shows reduced transfer to real-world topologies. Finally, GNN inference achieves up to a 97.7x speedup over exact computation. These results show that mixed-distribution training can improve structural transfer in GNN-based centrality approximation, while identifying closeness centrality's sensitivity to topology as an open challenge.

</details>


### [153] [Fully Trainable Deep Differentiable Logic Gate Networks and Lookup Table Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09399)
*Wout Mommen,Lars Keuninckx,Matthias Hartmann,Werner Van Leekwijck,Piet Wambacq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种针对可微逻辑门网络（LGN）和查找表网络（LUTN）的连接优化训练方法，通过学习每个输入引脚的连接概率分布来优化连接，同时并行学习最优门类型或LUT条目。该方法在多个基准上大幅超越固定连接方案，且所需门数减少近50倍，同时保证了多达10层的训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可微逻辑门网络和查找表网络通常采用固定连接结构，这限制了网络的表达能力和效率。作者希望在不依赖固定拓扑的前提下，同时优化连接和门类型/LUT条目，从而在更少的逻辑资源下获得更高的分类精度。

Method: 提出一种基于概率分布的连接训练算法：为每个门/LUT的输入引脚维护一个可选连接的集合及其概率分布，训练中选择概率最高的连接；同时并行学习最优的门类型或LUT条目。为实现深层训练稳定性，采用了高学习率、直通估计器（straight-through estimator）以及修剪恒定输出的门类型。还引入了一种LUT神经元描述，支持通过反向传播进行稳定训练。

Result: 在Yin-Yang、MNIST和Fashion-MNIST三个基准上验证：MNIST上2层8000个门达到98.92%，仅1层8000个门达到98.45%，所需门数比固定连接LGN减少近50倍；LUTN上2层2000个6输入LUT达到98.88%，参数量减少4倍且精度更高；训练稳定性验证至10层（LGN）和6层（LUTN）。

Conclusion: 所提出的连接优化方法能同时学习最优连接拓扑和门功能，在显著减少逻辑资源的同时提升性能，为可微逻辑网络的高效部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: We introduce a novel method for both partial and full optimization of the connections in deep differentiable logic gate networks (LGNs) and lookup table networks (LUTNs). Our training method utilizes a probability distribution over a set of connections per gate/lookup table (LUT) input pin, selecting the connection with highest merit, all whilst the optimal gate types or LUT-entries are learned in parallel. We show that the connection-optimized LGNs outperform standard fixed-connection LGNs on the Yin-Yang, MNIST Handwritten Digits and Fashion-MNIST benchmarks, while requiring only a fraction of the number of logic gates. We achieve 98.92% on the MNIST dataset with two layers of 8000 gates. With only one layer of 8000 gates, we obtain 98.45%, showing that our method requires almost 50 times fewer gates compared to fixed-connection LGNs. Training stability up to ten layers has been ensured by employing a high learning rate, straight-through estimators and trimming constant-output gate types. Additionally, we present a LUT neuron description that enables stable training with backpropagation, tested up to 6-layer deep networks. The model requires four times fewer trainable parameters and still achieves a higher accuracy compared to the fixed-connection LGN training algorithm. Our connection-training algorithm also works well for the LUTNs, achieving an accuracy of 98.88% for two layers of 2000 6-input LUTs.

</details>


### [154] [Learning Physics-Informed Surrogate Model of Linear Elastic Displacement Fields from Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09382)
*Rodolphe Barlogis,Ferhat Tamssaouet,Quentin Falcoz,Stéphane Grieu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理信息DeepONet代理模型，用于含裂缝弹性体的实时结构健康监测。该模型以边界条件和裂缝几何作为输入，无需有限元生成训练数据，通过局部惩罚项弱施加裂缝面的无牵引条件，实现位移场的快速预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限元方法在含裂缝结构的实时监测中计算成本过高，而现有数据驱动代理模型依赖大量有限元生成的训练数据，缺乏物理一致性。因此需要开发一种快速、物理一致、且无需依赖有限元训练数据的代理模型。

Method: 采用物理信息DeepONet（Physics-Informed DeepONet）框架。模型输入为边界条件与裂缝几何，裂缝几何通过专用编码策略处理。在裂缝边界上，通过局部惩罚项弱施加无牵引（traction-free）边界条件。训练完全基于物理控制方程（弹性力学方程）的残差，不依赖有限元生成的标签数据。

Result: 在一个代表性裂缝几何上验证了该方法的可行性，表明模型能够有效预测位移场，同时满足裂缝面无牵引的物理约束。

Conclusion: 所提框架展示了物理信息DeepONet在含裂缝弹性域代理建模中的可行性，为后续推广到多样化裂缝几何的代理模型奠定了基础。

Abstract: This work aims to develop a fast and physically consistent surrogate model for real-time structural health monitoring of fractured elastic domains. We propose a physics-informed DeepONet framework that predicts displacement fields from both boundary conditions and fracture geometry, using a dedicated encoding strategy for the latter and without relying on finite-element-generated training data. The traction-free condition on the fracture boundary is imposed weakly through a localized penalty term. The presented numerical example focuses on one representative fracture geometry, demonstrating the feasibility of the formulation and laying the groundwork for extensions to surrogate modeling across diverse fracture geometries.

</details>


### [155] [On-Device Adaptive Battery Power Prediction for Electric Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09400)
*Avik Bhatnagar,Anton Paule,Tobias Schuermann,Sebastian Reiter,Oliver Bringmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种在资源受限的电动汽车系统中实现设备端自适应学习的方法，通过将预训练电池功率预测模型转化为可适应版本，利用在线和离线自适应策略持续适应新数据分布，分别实现了最高7.49%和14.88%的平均绝对误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车中的深度学习时序预测模型在遇到与训练数据分布不同的新数据时，性能会显著退化。然而，在资源受限的车载嵌入式系统上实现模型持续适应面临算力和存储等约束，因此需要一种高效的设备端自适应学习方法来解决数据分布偏移问题。

Method: 将已有的预训练电池预测模型转化为可适应版本，保留其初始训练中的关键超参数知识；系统性地研究了在线自适应和离线自适应两种策略，使模型能够在资源受限的EV嵌入式系统上持续适应新的、未见过的数据分布。

Result: 在多种模型和时间跨度上的预测性能均有显著提升：在线自适应技术使平均绝对误差降低最高达7.49%，离线自适应技术降低最高达14.88%，均优于未经过自适应的模型部署方案。

Conclusion: 在真实电动汽车场景中，设备端自适应学习能够显著提升电池功率预测精度，证明了相比直接部署未自适应模型，该方法具有实质性优势，为资源受限环境下的持续学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Adaptive power management in Electric Vehicles (EVs) requires accurate power prediction. Although deep learning models have emerged as highly effective for time-series forecasting in this domain, their performance is prone to degradation when exposed to data with distributions different from the training data. We introduce a novel approach that enables on-device learning in resource-constrained EV systems to continuously adapt pretrained battery prediction models to new, unseen data. We leverage existing pretrained models by transforming them into adaptable versions that retain critical hyperparameter knowledge from their initial training. We comprehensively investigate both online and offline model adaptation strategies. Our results demonstrate significant improvements in forecasting performance across various models and time horizons, achieving mean absolute error reductions of up to 7.49\% and 14.88\% with online and offline adaptation techniques, respectively. This study highlights the substantial benefit of on-device adaptation, resulting in enhanced battery power predictions than unadapted model deployments in real-world EV scenarios.

</details>


### [156] [Data-Efficient Deep Learning: Empirical Guidelines for Training Set Size Estimation in Inertial Sensor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09402)
*Ofir Kruzel,Itzik Klien*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了惯性传感器分类任务中学习曲线的收敛规律，发现准确率随数据集大小呈一致的对数增长模式，并提出了"稳定性点"指标来量化所需的最小样本量，从而为惯性感知领域的数据采集活动提供了从"追求数据量最大化"转向"优化数据效率"的实证指导框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在惯性传感器分类任务（如人类活动识别、手机位置识别）中依赖大规模数据集，但数据采集成本高昂、难以规模化，且目前缺乏基于数据驱动的方法来确定达到目标精度所需的最小样本量。

Method: 提出统一框架，在二分类和多分类场景下分析分类性能；基于六个真实数据集（总计102.7小时惯性测量数据）系统评估学习曲线收敛速率；推导经验公式估计性能与数据集大小的关系；定义"稳定性点"指标，即学习曲线在预设的渐近最大值平均绝对百分比偏差内稳定所需样本量。

Result: 准确率遵循一致的对数增长模式，与任务复杂度无关；模型通常比传统经验法则所建议的样本量少得多即可达到实用稳定性；所提框架可从大规模试点研究外推总数据需求。

Conclusion: 将惯性感知领域的范式从最大化数据量转向优化数据效率，为惯性传感器应用中录制活动的规划提供了具体、数据驱动的指导方针。

Abstract: Deep learning models dependency on large-scale inertial datasets presents a significant bottleneck in inertial sensor-based classification tasks, such as human activity recognition and smartphone location recognition. In these domains, data collection requires massive recording campaigns that are complex, time-consuming, and difficult to scale. Currently, data-driven guidelines for determining the minimum sample size required to reach a desired accuracy level do not exist. To address this gap, this study presents a systematic empirical evaluation of learning curve convergence rates in inertial classification. We introduce a unified framework that analyzes classification performance under both binary and multi-class scenarios, and derive an empirical formula to estimate performance relative to dataset size. Testing across six diverse, real-world datasets totaling 102.7 hours of inertial measurements demonstrates that accuracy follows a consistent logarithmic growth pattern, regardless of task complexity. Leveraging this finding, we propose a quantitative stability point metric, defined as the sample size required for the learning curve to stabilize within a predefined mean absolute percentage deviation of its asymptotic maximum. Our analysis reveals that models often reach practical stability with substantially fewer samples than traditional heuristics suggest. Ultimately, we offer a generalizable framework to extrapolate total data requirements from small-scale pilot studies, optimizing the tradeoff between recording effort and model reliability. These findings shift the prevailing paradigm from maximizing data volume toward optimizing data efficiency, offering concrete, data-backed guidelines for planning recording campaigns in inertial sensing applications.

</details>


### [157] [SYNRARE: Synthetic Rare Disease EHR Generation for ML Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09404)
*Nicolai Dinh Khang Truong,Richard Röttger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SYNRARE 是一个基于 Synthea 框架的图形用户界面工具，用于生成罕见病患者的合成电子健康记录，这些记录与常见病患者仅有可定义程度的差异，从而支持在受控条件下对机器学习算法进行基准测试和开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病诊断常因症状与常见病相似而延迟。机器学习应用于电子健康记录虽有望加速诊断，但法律与隐私问题构成重大障碍。合成数据生成是替代方案，但现有算法缺乏对罕见病患者亚群（与多数患者存在可定义差异）的生成支持。

Method: 基于 Synthea 框架开发 SYNRARE 图形用户界面，允许用户以可定义程度修改并生成罕见病患者的合成电子健康记录，使其与常见病患者仅在指定特征上存在差异，便于在受控技术条件下进行算法测试。

Result: SYNRARE 使研究人员能够在任意场景下快速对其机器学习算法进行基准测试，填补了现有合成数据生成工具在罕见病模拟方面的空白。

Conclusion: SYNRARE 为罕见病诊断算法的开发与评估提供了一个隐私安全、可控、可定制的合成数据生成平台，已在 GitLab 上开源发布。

Abstract: Motivation: Rare disease (RD) diagnosis is frequently delayed due to the similarities in symptoms to common disease variants. Machine Learning Algorithms applied to Electronic Health Records show promise for accelerating the diagnosis; however, legal and privacy concerns pose significant barriers. To address these issues, Synthetic Data Generation is an alternative method for obtaining Electronic Health Records and can be applied with any Machine Learning algorithm for benchmarking and development purposes. Despite the availability of Synthetic Data Generation algorithms, support for generating a subset of patients that differ in a definable degree from the majority to simulate patients with RD is often lacking.
  Results: We present SYNRARE, a graphical user interface based on the Synthea framework that enables easier modification and generation of synthetic Electronic Health Records of RD patients, which differ only to a definable degree from patients with common diseases, thereby enabling the benchmarking and testing of algorithms under controlled technical conditions. SYNRARE enables researchers to rapidly benchmark their Machine Learning algorithms across any scenario.
  Availability and implementation: SYNRARE, including detailed instructions for installing, is available at https://gitlab.sdu.dk/screen4care/synrare.

</details>


### [158] [Action-Factored Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Scalable Quantum Device Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09422)
*Edwin De Nicolo,Rahul Marchand,Cornelius Carlsson,Pranav Vaidhyanathan,Natalia Ares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QADAPT框架，通过在线学习动作空间的因子化表示来解耦多智能体，解决量子点阵列调谐中参数串扰导致的非平稳性问题，实现了对未见量子器件尺寸的零样本泛化和近似恒定的收敛步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合作式多智能体强化学习适合量子点阵列调谐等大参数空间问题，但当参数串扰严重时，各智能体面临非平稳环境，导致学习不稳定——这与人工调谐该类系统时遇到的困难相同。因此需要一种方法解耦智能体、减少相互干扰。

Method: 提出QADAPT框架，核心是在线学习动作空间的因子化表示（factored representation），将智能体解耦以最小化相互干扰。利用该因子化结构，各智能体基于局部测量和局部奖励高效学习共享策略，实现模块化控制。

Result: 该方法实现了对未见量子器件尺寸的零样本泛化（zero-shot generalization），且达到目标状态所需的收敛步数保持近似恒定，不随系统规模增长而显著增加。

Conclusion: 该工作为大规模量子处理器的快速校准提供了一条可扩展的技术路径，展示了因子化多智能体强化学习在量子器件自动化调谐中的潜力。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning is well suited to problems with large parameter spaces and exploitable local structure, such as the tuning of electrostatically-defined quantum-dot arrays. However, if parameter cross-talk is strong, a non-stationary environment from the perspective of any individual agent can destabilize learning - the same effect that plagues manual tuning of such systems. We propose using a factored representation of the action space, learned online, to decouple agents and minimize their interference. Our framework, QADAPT, uses this factorization to efficiently learn shared policies based on local measurements and rewards. With this modular strategy, we achieve zero-shot generalization to unseen quantum device sizes and maintain an approximately constant number of convergence steps to reach target regimes. This work provides a scalable route toward the rapid calibration of large-scale quantum processors.

</details>


### [159] [Active rejection enables reliable generalization of universal machine-learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09456)
*Mingxiang Luo,Xinnan Mao,Lu Wang,Lei Bai,Feng Ding,Yuqiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ATR通过结构级不确定性决策，利用少量真实r²SCAN标签校准多个预训练uMLIP教师模型，选择高置信度预测生成伪标签，仅用0.2%真实标签蒸馏出289万条r²SCAN级数据，显著提升CHGNet在基准测试和分子动力学模拟中的性能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度计算（如r²SCAN）成本高昂，导致uMLIP训练数据远小于开放材料空间；且强平均基准性能不保证对每个结构的能量-力预测都可靠。需要一种可扩展的高保真数据构建方法。

Method: 提出Adaptive Multi-Teacher Routing (ATR)，将高保真数据构建形式化为结构级不确定性决策问题。利用少量真实r²SCAN标签校准多个预训练uMLIP教师，结合结构描述符、教师身份和教师间分歧度，估计每个结构-教师对的可靠性；选择高置信度预测生成伪标签，拒绝无足够可靠教师的结构。

Result: 仅用0.2%候选结构的真实r²SCAN标签，蒸馏出289万条可追溯的r²SCAN级伪标签。在留出r²SCAN结构和MP-r²SCAN基准上，ATR训练的轻量CHGNet一致优于基线及非路由对照。有限温度分子动力学显示ATR在多个材料体系中提升动力学鲁棒性，基线模拟发生灾难性结构坍塌时ATR保持稳定轨迹。

Conclusion: 主动拒绝（active rejection）是将多个预训练uMLIP转化为可扩展、可靠的高保真数据构建系统的有效机制，为高保真uMLIP训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: Universal machine learning interatomic potentials (uMLIPs) bridge quantum-mechanical accuracy and large-scale molecular dynamics, but the cost of high-accuracy calculations such as r$^2$SCAN limits training to datasets that remain small relative to the open materials space. Strong average benchmark performance also does not guarantee reliable energy--force predictions for every structure. We propose Adaptive Multi-Teacher Routing (ATR), which reformulates high-fidelity data construction as a structure-wise decision problem under uncertainty. Using a small set of real r$^2$SCAN labels, ATR calibrates multiple pretrained uMLIP teachers and combines structural descriptors, teacher identity, and inter-teacher disagreement to estimate the reliability of each structure--teacher pair. It selects high-confidence predictions for pseudo-label generation and rejects structures for which no teacher is sufficiently reliable. With real r$^2$SCAN labels for only 0.2\% of candidate structures, ATR distils 2.89 million traceable r$^2$SCAN-level pseudo-labels for pretraining. On held-out r$^2$SCAN structures and the MP-r$^2$SCAN benchmark, a lightweight CHGNet trained on the ATR-generated dataset consistently outperforms the baseline and non-routed controls. Finite-temperature molecular dynamics further shows that ATR improves dynamical robustness across multiple material systems, maintaining stable trajectories where baseline simulations undergo catastrophic structural collapse. These results establish active rejection as an effective mechanism for converting multiple pretrained uMLIPs into a scalable and reliable data-construction system for high-fidelity uMLIPs.

</details>


### [160] [All Explanations are Wrong, But Many Are Useful: Exploring the Rashomon Explanation Set with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09502)
*Pan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rashomon Explanation范式，认为准确性与可解释性并非根本对立，通过构建一组忠实且引导预测的解释（而非单一解释），可使二者互补。基于此提出RashomonLLM——一种解释-预测-反思的智能体工作流，在三个业务场景中同时提升了预测精度和解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前XAI方法普遍存在准确性-可解释性权衡困境，但作者认为这一权衡并非本质性的，而是将解释与预测视为独立目标所导致的人为假象。当二者适当耦合时，赋予模型自我解释能力反而能提升其准确性。

Method: 提出Rashomon Explanation范式：构建一组忠实且能引导预测的解释集合，证明该集合一般非空且解释保真度可界定其引导模型的性能下界。设计RashomonLLM——基于Explanation-Prediction-Reflection的智能体工作流，通过迭代对齐解释与预测来生成自然语言解释，并证明其收敛且能恢复完整解释集。

Result: 在客户流失分类、临床生存回归和大规模直播日志点击率预测三个场景中，RashomonLLM在预测精度和解释质量上均显著优于现有最优预测模型和XAI基线，性能增益由解释保真度驱动，且对分布偏移、时间切分和随机种子具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架打破了准确性-可解释性二分的传统认知，证明二者可以协同提升，从而在推动业务性能的同时为消费者信任奠定基础。

Abstract: Explaining machine-learning models is increasingly important for decision-making and consumer trust, yet it is widely believed to come at a cost: existing Explainable AI (XAI) methods suffer from a persistent accuracy-explainability trade-off. We argue that this trade-off is not fundamental, but an artifact of treating explanation and prediction as separate objectives; when properly coupled, they become complementary, so that equipping a model to explain itself improves, rather than degrades, its accuracy. We introduce the Rashomon Explanation paradigm, which builds a set of faithful, prediction-guiding explanations rather than a single one, and prove that this set is generally non-empty and that explanation fidelity bounds the performance of the models it guides. To explore this set, we propose RashomonLLM, an Explanation-Prediction-Reflection agentic workflow that generates explanations in natural language by iteratively aligning them with predictions, and we prove it converges and recovers the full set. Across customer-churn classification, clinical survival regression, and industrial click-through prediction on large-scale live-streaming logs, RashomonLLM significantly outperforms state-of-the-art prediction and XAI baselines on both accuracy and explanation quality, with gains driven by explanation fidelity and robust to distribution shifts, temporal splits, and seeds. Our framework thus advances business performance while laying the groundwork for consumer trust.

</details>


### [161] [TSAI-MetaFraud: A Benchmark Dataset for Financial Fraud Transaction and Behavioral Risk Detection in Metaverse Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09528)
*Refat Ishrak Hemel,Ehsan Hallaji,Roozbeh Razavi-Far*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TSAI-MetaFraud，一个面向虚拟经济欺诈分析的多模态、多任务基准数据集，整合行为、交易和图结构信息，并定义了多项基准任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有元宇宙欺诈检测数据集通常孤立地关注用户行为、身份认证或金融交易，缺乏多模态融合的基准，限制了多模态欺诈检测方法的可复现评估。

Method: 构建TSAI-MetaFraud数据集，融合行为数据、交易数据和图结构信息，并注入逼真的欺诈与自动化机器人场景；同时定义交易欺诈检测、跨模态节点分类、时序链路预测和弱监督欺诈检测等基准任务，使用机器学习模型和图神经网络进行基线评估。

Result: 提供了统一的虚拟经济数据资源，涵盖行为活动、金融交互和关系结构，并给出了各基准任务上的基线实验结果。

Conclusion: TSAI-MetaFraud为推进元宇宙生态中的多模态学习、图挖掘、欺诈分析和可信AI研究提供了一个标准化基准平台。

Abstract: The emergence of metaverse platforms has created virtual economies that introduce new challenges related to fraud, bot activity, and illicit financial behavior. Despite growing interest in trustworthy metaverse analytics, existing datasets typically focus on user behavior, authentication, or financial transactions in isolation, limiting the development and reproducible evaluation of multimodal fraud detection methods. To address this gap, we present TSAI-MetaFraud, a multimodal, multi-task benchmark dataset for fraud analytics in virtual economies. TSAI-MetaFraud integrates behavioral, transactional, and graph-structured information while incorporating realistic fraud and automated bot scenarios. We define benchmark tasks including transaction fraud detection, cross-modal node classification, temporal link prediction, and weakly supervised fraud detection, and provide baseline evaluations using machine learning models and graph neural networks. By jointly capturing behavioral activity, financial interactions, and relational structure within a unified virtual economy, TSAI-MetaFraud provides a benchmark for advancing multimodal learning, graph mining, fraud analytics, and trustworthy AI in emerging metaverse ecosystems.

</details>


### [162] [GatedLinear: Adaptive Routing of Complementary Linear Bases for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09537)
*Qitai Tan,Ruiwen Gu,Yilin Su,Mo Li,Xu Lin,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GatedLinear 是一种轻量级时间序列预测框架，通过三种互补线性基的自适应路由来解决时间序列中的异质性动态模式（平滑趋势、非平稳漂移、相位对齐循环），并引入三因子融合门实现通道、时间步和相位的精细路由，在保持小参数量的同时达到与复杂基础模型相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度时序模型通常采用单一计算机制（如自注意力或频谱滤波）处理所有时序动态，但真实世界时间序列高度异质——不同变量、不同预测视野需要本质上不同的预测策略，单一机制难以兼顾平滑趋势延续、非平稳漂移和严格相位对齐循环等多种相互排斥的模式。

Method: 提出 GatedLinear 框架，将预测建模为对三种互补线性基的自适应路由：(1) 全局趋势-季节基用于平滑投影；(2) 基于差分的增量基用于非平稳漂移；(3) 相位对齐循环基用于显式周期性复用。核心创新是 Tri-Factorized Fusion Gate（三因子融合门），将路由决策分解为通道特定偏好、视野感知偏移和基于已知未来时间标记的相位索引偏置三个因子，实现逐点的细粒度软路由，无需堆叠重型计算模块。

Result: 在标准基准数据集上，GatedLinear 相较于最近的复杂基础模型达到了最先进或高度竞争力的预测精度，同时路由模式具有显式可解释性，参数量显著更少。

Conclusion: 通过自适应路由多种互补线性基并结合三因子融合门，GatedLinear 以轻量设计有效应对时序预测中的异质性挑战，在精度-效率权衡和可解释性方面均表现优异。

Abstract: Time series forecasting requires models to capture diverse, often mutually exclusive, temporal dynamics, from smooth trend continuation to nonstationary drift and strict phase-aligned recurrence. While recent deep learning models have improved accuracy, they typically force these diverse patterns through a single computational backbone governed by fixed algorithmic inductive biases (e.g., self-attention or spectral filtering). This single-mechanism approach often struggles with the profound heterogeneity of real-world series, where different variables and forecast horizons necessitate fundamentally different predictive treatments. To address this, we propose GatedLinear: a lightweight framework that frames forecasting as the adaptive routing of complementary linear bases. GatedLinear leverages a pool of three specialized mechanisms: a global trend-seasonal basis for smooth projection, a difference-based incremental basis for nonstationary drift, and a phase-aligned recurrence basis for explicit cyclic reuse. To dynamically orchestrate these distinct behaviors, we introduce a Tri-Factorized Fusion Gate that disentangles routing decisions into channel-specific preferences, horizon-aware offsets, and phase-indexed biases derived from known future time marks. This design allows the model to perform highly granular, point-wise soft routing across different predictive regimes without stacking computationally heavy neural modules. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art or highly competitive accuracy against recent complex foundational models, while offering explicitly interpretable routing patterns and operating with a substantially smaller parameter footprint.

</details>


### [163] [CoCoT-EEG: Contrastive-Pretrained Multiscale Convolutional Transformer for EEG Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09543)
*Gabriel Mahuas,Victoria Shevchenko,Ugo Tanielian,Yassir Bendou,Richard Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoCoT提出了一种基于对比学习的脑电图基础模型，采用多尺度时序卷积和Transformer编码器，在多项解码任务中匹配或超越当前主流的掩码重建预训练方法，揭示了对比学习在EEG基础模型构建中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的EEG基础模型多采用掩码重建预训练策略，但该方法对于高噪声、信息集中于窄频带等有限维度的EEG数据而言是次优的，亟需探索替代性的预训练范式。

Method: 提出CoCoT模型，核心设计包括：(1) 多尺度时序卷积输入层，捕获不同时间分辨率的EEG特征；(2) Transformer编码器模块进行序列建模；(3) 采用对比学习（contrastive learning）作为预训练目标，替代传统的掩码重建策略。

Result: CoCoT在大量异质电极配置的基准解码任务上，匹配或超越了最先进的基于重建预训练的EEG模型。从零训练时，CoCoT亦优于先前单任务解码模型，甚至媲美预训练模型，展现了出色的架构灵活性和数据效率。消融实验系统验证了对比学习预训练目标及架构设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 对比学习是构建EEG基础模型的可行方案，多尺度卷积与Transformer的组合架构具有优秀的数据效率和泛化能力。该工作为未来大规模EEG预训练策略的探索指明了新方向。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretrained foundation models (FM) have shown early promise for non-invasive electroencephalogram (EEG) decoding applications. Many recent large-scale models converged on the approach of tokenizing raw EEG followed by masked reconstruction pretraining. However, this recipe has been shown to be suboptimal for data, like EEG, with high noise amplitude and information confined to limited dimensions such as narrow frequency bands. Building on this insight, we develop a novel contrastive-pretrained EEG model with multiscale temporal convolution input layers and Transformer encoder blocks (CoCoT). CoCoT matches or beats state-of-the-art reconstruction-pretrained EEG models on extensive benchmark decoding tasks with heterogeneous electrode configurations. Furthermore, CoCoT trained from scratch outperforms previous single-task decoding models and even rivals pretrained models, showcasing the architecture's flexibility and data efficiency. Through systematic ablations, including model architecture and pretraining objective, we demonstrate the viability of contrastive learning for building EEG FMs while suggesting key architectural design considerations, prompting further investigations in alternative large-scale pretraining strategies.

</details>


### [164] [Graph-Regularized Low-Rank Matrix Completion by Variable Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09546)
*Benoît Loucheur,P. -A. Absil,Michel Journée*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GR-RTRMC，在RTRMC框架中引入图正则化，利用矩阵行列间内在关系提升低秩矩阵补全的精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RTRMC方法仅利用低秩约束的几何结构，忽略了矩阵行与行、列与列之间的内在关联性。当数据在行或列上呈现强相关性时，这种关联信息对补全精度至关重要。

Method: 在RTRMC的Grassmann流形无约束优化框架中，引入图正则化项来编码行与行、列与列之间的相似性关系，从而在优化过程中同时利用低秩先验和图结构先验。

Result: 通过融合图正则化，该方法在存在强行列相关性的场景下，相比原始RTRMC提升了补全精度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GR-RTRMC成功将图正则化融入黎曼优化框架，为利用数据内在关系改进矩阵补全提供了有效途径，尤其在行列相关显著的场景中表现优异。

Abstract: We address the low-rank matrix completion problem by incorporating graph regularization into the existing Riemannian Trust-Region Matrix Completion (RTRMC) framework. The latter uses the geometry of the low-rank constraint to remodel the problem as an unconstrained optimization problem on a single Grassmann manifold. Our approach, named Graph-Regularized RTRMC (GR-RTRMC), exploits the inherent relationships between rows and columns of the matrix. By using these relationships, we aim to improve the accuracy and robustness of matrix completion, particularly in scenarios where the underlying data exhibits strong correlations between rows or columns.

</details>


### [165] [Semantic Pareto-DQN: A Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning Framework for Financial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09641)
*Cláudio Lúcio do Val Lopes,Lucca Machado da Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对金融异常检测中极端类别不平衡导致的“欺诈崩溃”问题，本文提出语义帕累托-DQN，一种多目标强化学习框架，通过LLM编码的语义状态表示与向量化奖励解耦，在无需数据重采样的情况下实现异常召回与运营摩擦的帕累托最优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单目标算法在极端类别不平衡下会陷入“欺诈崩溃”（fraud collapse），即模型默认预测多数类，无法在拦截异常与控制客户摩擦之间取得平衡。现有数据重采样方法会引入失真，因此需要一种无需扭曲数据分布即可实现多目标优化的新范式。

Method: 提出Semantic Pareto-DQN框架：(1)将异构交易特征合成为连贯的自然语言叙述，利用大语言模型（LLM）编码生成鲁棒且尺度不变的语义状态表示；(2)设计向量化奖励函数，显式解耦金融效能、运营摩擦和语义发现三个目标；(3)通过映射连续帕累托前沿，动态导航漏检异常与误报之间的非对称成本。

Result: 在E-Commerce欺诈检测和UCI Credit两个数据集上的实证评估表明，Semantic Pareto-DQN成功打破了零召回陷阱，相比标量化基线方法实现了显著更优的少数类召回率，能够在有限运营摩擦下换取更高的异常发现能力。

Conclusion: 语义帕累托-DQN为极端类别不平衡下的金融异常检测提供了一种有效替代方案，通过多目标强化学习与语义状态表示的结合，在不依赖数据重采样的情况下实现了异常检测与客户体验之间的可控权衡。

Abstract: Financial anomaly detection suffers from extreme class imbalance, causing traditional single-objective algorithms to exhibit ``fraud collapse'', defaulting to the majority class and failing to balance anomaly interdiction with customer friction. To overcome this without distortive data resampling, we propose the Semantic Pareto-DQN, a multi-objective reinforcement learning framework. Our approach synthesizes heterogeneous transaction features into cohesive natural-language narratives, encoded by large language models, thereby producing a robust, scale-invariant state representation. The agent optimizes a vectorial reward that explicitly decouples financial efficacy, operational friction, and semantic discovery. By mapping the continuous Pareto frontier, the system dynamically navigates the asymmetric costs of missed anomalies versus false positives. Empirical evaluations across E-Commerce fraud and UCI Credit datasets show that semantic Pareto-DQN successfully shatters the zero-recall trap. It achieves superior minority-class recall compared to scalarized baselines, providing an alternative to trade bounded operational friction for financial anomaly discovery.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Extrapolation of extreme covariates in generalized additive regression using extreme-value theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08921)
*Viviana Carcaiso,Sebastian Engelke,Juliette Legrand,Thomas Opitz*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该论文提出将广义加性模型(GAMs)与多元极值理论的渐近模型相结合，以提升协变量外推场景下的预测性能，特别针对气候变化等极端条件应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际预测中，训练数据与预测数据之间的协变量分布可能发生偏移，尤其是在气候变化情景下，未来气候模拟的协变量往往包含更极端的值。传统GAM在协变量分布尾部的外推能力不足，导致极端条件下的预测不准确。

Method: 提出一种混合方法：对协变量分布的主体部分使用GAM建模，而在高协变量值区域采用多元极值理论的渐近模型。针对二分类响应（基于潜变量假设）和连续响应，在极值理论驱动的特定边际尺度下，假设潜变量或连续响应通过适当的连接函数与协变量呈线性关系并带有加性误差项。

Result: 在欧洲野火预测的应用中，结合环境与气象协变量，新方法展现出比传统GAM更优的预测性能，尤其是在极端条件下的外推预测得到显著改善。

Conclusion: 将GAM与多元极值渐近模型融合的策略，能有效解决协变量外推中的预测退化问题，为气候变化等涉及极端条件的应用提供了稳健的统计建模框架。

Abstract: We propose methods to enhance the predictive performance of generalized additive models (GAMs) in the context of covariate extrapolation, where predictions rely on covariates beyond their observed range. When using predictive models such as GAMs, shifts in the covariate distribution between training and prediction datasets can occur. Ignoring this issue may lead to inaccurate predictions in the tail of the covariate distributions. For example, this problem is particularly critical in climate-change scenarios, where covariates simulated from future climate scenarios are likely to contain more extreme conditions. Our approach integrates GAMs for the bulk of covariate distributions with asymptotic models from multivariate extreme-value theory at high covariate values. We consider binary responses based on a latent variable assumption, and also continuous responses. For large values of the covariates, on a specific marginal scale motivated by extreme-value theory the latent variable or continuous response is assumed to depend linearly on the covariates with an additive error term, when using an appropriate link function. In an application to wildfires in Europe, we explore how the new method can improve predictions, using environmental and meteorological covariates.

</details>


### [167] [A Statistical Test for the Benefits of Personalizing Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08951)
*Zhaoqi Li,Emma Brunskill*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统计假设检验方法，用于评估个性化干预策略是否显著优于单一最佳干预策略，并在多个领域数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化干预在医疗、营销、社会科学等领域前景广阔，但实际部署需权衡其潜在收益与增加的成本及脆弱性。决策者缺乏一种严谨的统计工具来判断：基于历史数据，个性化策略是否确实值得投入。

Method: 提出一种统计假设检验框架，在给定历史数据的条件下，检验个性化干预策略的绩效是否优于部署单一最佳干预。该方法在特定条件下保持严格的第I类错误控制，同时实现最小可能方差的渐近正态性。

Result: 在职业培训、抑郁症治疗、教育及推荐系统等多个多样化数据集上，该检验方法展现出良好的通用性，且性能优于现有替代方法。

Conclusion: 该检验方法为干预科学领域的决策者提供了一种简洁而有力的工具，可用于量化个性化的潜在收益，从而支持是否采用个性化策略的决策。

Abstract: From medicine to marketing to social sciences, the promise of tailoring interventions to individuals is undeniable. However, practical applications force weighing personalization's potential benefits with its possible increased cost and fragility. We introduce a statistical hypothesis test that evaluates, given historical data, evidence that a personalized intervention policy's performance will surpass deploying the best single intervention. The test maintains strict type-I error control while achieving asymptotic normality with the minimal possible variance under specified conditions. Results on diverse datasets from job training, depression treatment, education and recommendation systems demonstrate the test's versatility and its superior performance over alternatives. This test can support decision-makers throughout the intervention sciences by providing a simple and powerful quantification of the potential benefits of personalization.

</details>


### [168] [Orthogonalized Design Matrices Speed-ups of Bayesian Semiparametric Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09013)
*Nurul Fitriyani,Matt P. Wand*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该论文提出通过在贝叶斯半参数回归中使用正交化设计矩阵，可将吉布斯采样和坐标上升变分推断等循环型算法的计算复杂度降低约两个数量级（相对于列维度），实验显示加速约5到60倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯半参数回归通常需要采用吉布斯采样或坐标上升变分推断等循环型方法，这些方法在每次迭代中需要对设计矩阵进行大量矩阵运算。随着基函数数量的增加，计算负担急剧上升，因此亟需在不牺牲推断精度的前提下大幅降低计算开销的技术手段。

Method: 作者提出在进入循环迭代（如MCMC或变分推断）之前，对设计矩阵进行正交化预处理。通过将原有的设计矩阵（包含预测变量观测值及其基函数展开）重新参数化为正交基的形式，使得循环内部原本复杂的矩阵求逆和乘法运算退化为逐元素操作，从而将计算复杂度相对于设计矩阵列维度降低约两个数量级。

Result: 计算机实验表明，该正交化预处理策略在不同模型和数据集上均能实现显著的加速效果，提速幅度大约在5倍到60倍之间，验证了理论上的复杂度降低预期。

Conclusion: 在贝叶斯半参数回归中，简单地对设计矩阵进行正交化预处理即可带来高达两个数量级的计算量削减，是一种通用、易实现且效果显著的加速范式。

Abstract: We explain how important classes of Bayesian semiparametric regression fitting and inference procedures can be sped up, significantly, via the use of orthogonalized design matrices. Typically, design matrices in semiparametric regression contain predictor observations and basis functions of such data. In Bayesian semiparametric regression, loop-type approaches such as Gibbs sampling and coordinate ascent variational inference typically are required. We show that pre-loop reformulation of Bayesian semiparametric regression models involving orthogonalized design matrices lead to two orders of magnitude, with respect to column dimension, computational reduction. Our computer experiments reveal that this simple paradigm results in approximately 5- to 60-fold speed-ups.

</details>


### [169] [Comprehensive identifiability analysis and reliable parameter estimation for an SEIR model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09137)
*Eduard Campillo-Funollet,James Van Yperen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文对SEIR流行病学模型进行了全面的结构可辨识性分析，揭示了标准模型参数的全局不可辨识性问题，并提出了一种通过观测系统方法导出的全局可辨识且计算稳定的重参数化方案，显著改善了参数估计的收敛性和数值稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SEIR模型的参数（传播率、潜伏率、感染率的倒数）难以直接测量，通常需通过优化方法估计。然而，标准SEIR系统参数并非全局可辨识，导致优化算法频繁收敛到错误的局部最优解，并遭受数值刚性问题困扰。因此，亟需一种能够消除参数估计非唯一性、提升计算稳定性的解决方案。

Method: 作者采用结构可辨识性分析方法对SEIR框架进行全面分析，完整刻画了多个局部可辨识参数的特征；基于观测系统方法，将系统转化为全局可辨识结构，导出一种重参数化方案；在数值实验中，将一阶灵敏度方程融入优化器以增强估计过程的鲁棒性和执行速度。

Result: 所提出的重参数化方案显著提高了优化收敛频率，避免了数值溢出导致的运行时错误，并能一致性地恢复正确参数。融入一阶灵敏度方程进一步提升了估计过程的鲁棒性和执行速度。

Conclusion: 数值条件良好的参数辨识方法，结合对参数可辨识性的全面理解，能够确保SEIR模型为传染病预测和理论流行病学提供可靠且严谨的洞察。

Abstract: The Susceptible-Exposed-Infectious-Removed (SEIR) model is a fundamental model in epidemiology. Model parameters such as the reciprocal transmission, incubation, and infectious rates are often difficult to measure directly, and they are estimated by solving an optimisation problem aiming to minimise the difference between the observed data and the model solution. However, the parameters of the standard SEIR system are not globally identifiable, causing optimisation algorithms to frequently converge to incorrect local optima and suffer from numerical stiffness. Here we show a comprehensive structural identifiability analysis of the SEIR framework, and present a globally identifiable and computationally stable reparameterisation of the model derived via an observational system approach. We fully characterise the multiple locally identifiable parameters, and by transforming the system into a globally identifiable structure, we eliminate the non-uniqueness issues in the parameter estimation approaches. Our numerical experiments demonstrate that this reformulation significantly improves convergence frequency, avoids runtime errors caused by numerical overflow, and consistently recovers the correct parameters. Furthermore, incorporating first-order sensitivity equations into the optimiser enhances the robustness and execution speed of the estimation process. Numerically well-conditioned methods for parameter identification, together with a comprehensive understanding of the identifiability of the parameters, ensure that the model yields reliable, rigorous insights for infectious disease forecasting and theoretical epidemiology.

</details>


### [170] [Causal Perspectives on Network Meta-Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09200)
*Ahmed Boughdiri,Francisco Andrade,Clément Berenfeld,Julie Josse*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于聚合数据的成对与网络荟萃分析的统一因果框架。作者主张因果估计量应先于聚合方法，需明确定义目标人群和异质性来源，推导出臂级（arm-level）聚合的自然识别方法。该框架在网络荟萃分析中与传统的对比范式有本质区别，为"对比法 vs 臂法"的长期争论提供了新的概念性论据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前荟萃分析通常先汇总研究层面的治疗效果再得出总体估计，但缺乏对因果估计量的明确定义。作者认为这种做法在因果解释上存在模糊性：目标人群和异质性来源未被事先明确，导致估计量的因果含义不清，尤其在网络荟萃分析中，传统对比范式对治疗网络的依赖可能带来识别上的问题。

Method: 研究者构建了一个统一的因果框架，用于基于聚合数据的成对和网络荟萃分析：(1) 相对于临床上有意义的目标人群（如试验所代表的平均人群）定义治疗效果；(2) 通过考虑治疗效果修饰因子和中心效应来解释异质性；(3) 从因果识别出发，自然推导出臂级聚合（arm-level aggregation），而非将其作为单纯的建模选择；(4) 在网络设置中，治疗效果的识别不依赖于治疗网络本身。

Result: 数值研究表明，所提出的估计量能够瞄准明确定义的因果效应，而传统方法的因果解释仍不清晰。尽管两种方法在许多情况下产生相似估计，但在特定设置中存在分歧，这对荟萃分析证据的解释可能产生重要影响。

Conclusion: 因果估计量应优先于聚合方法：先定义目标人群和异质性来源，再推导所需的聚合方式。这一视角转变从本质上改变了荟萃分析的估计量和方法论，为"对比法 vs 臂法"争论提供了支持臂法的额外概念论据，并揭示了传统方法在因果解释上的潜在局限。

Abstract: Pairwise and network meta-analyses occupy the highest tier of evidence-based medicine and routinely inform clinical guidelines and healthcare decision-making. Current approaches typically aggregate study-level treatment effects to obtain an overall estimate. We argue that the causal estimand should come first, with the aggregation derived only afterwards: the target population and the relevant sources of between-study heterogeneity should be explicitly defined before deriving the aggregation required for identification. This shift in perspective fundamentally changes both the estimands and the methodology.
  We develop a unified causal framework for pairwise and network meta-analysis based on aggregate data. By defining treatment effects with respect to a clinically meaningful target population, for example, the average population represented by the contributing trials, and accounting for heterogeneity induced by treatment-effect modifiers and center effects, we show that identification naturally leads to arm-level aggregation. In the network setting, this causal formulation departs fundamentally from the conventional contrast-based paradigm: arm-level aggregation emerges from the causal formulation rather than from a modeling choice, and treatment effects are identified without relying on the treatment network itself. This perspective provides an additional conceptual argument in the long-standing contrast-based versus arm-based debate.
  Numerical studies show that the proposed estimators target well-defined causal effects, whereas the causal interpretation of conventional approaches remains unclear. Although both approaches often produce similar estimates, we identify settings in which they diverge, with potentially important implications for the interpretation of meta-analytic evidence.

</details>


### [171] [Rethinking the Scientific Method: An Introduction to Bayesian Epistemology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09281)
*Jordan D. A. Hart,Daniel W. Franks*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 科学家之间的分歧常源于隐性的认识论框架差异（确认主义与证伪主义）。本文论证贝叶斯认识论可整合二者优势并克服其局限：确认与证伪皆为贝叶斯更新的特例；先验主观性并非致命弱点；该框架可将可重复性危机重新理解为认识论问题，并为研究设计、证据综合与发表规范提供实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学争论往往不是数据层面的分歧，而是研究者无意识地运用不同的认识论框架（确认主义 vs. 证伪主义）来解释证据。这两种传统各有合理之处，但也各自面临不可回避的困境：确认主义受困于归纳问题，证伪主义则受制于迪昂-奎因问题且无法解释理论接受与行动决策的合理性。需要一种能够统一二者、克服其弱点的认识论框架。

Method: 本文采用哲学分析与论证的方法，将贝叶斯认识论作为统一框架进行辩护。具体而言：(1) 将确认（evidence support）与证伪（deductive refutation）分别重构为贝叶斯概率更新的特殊情形；(2) 回应先验概率的主观性批评，论证其在实际科学实践中的弱化；(3) 推导该框架对研究设计、证据综合与发表规范的实际操作含义，例如用"假设是否产生能区分竞争假设的预测"取代传统的可证伪性标准。

Result: (1) 确认主义与证伪主义均可作为贝叶斯更新的极限或特例被推导出来；(2) 对先验主观性的常见反驳在实践中被高估，通过稳健性检验和证据积累可有效收敛；(3) 贝叶斯框架将可重复性危机重新定位为认识论问题而非纯粹统计问题；(4) 提出以"预测的区分能力"替代"可证伪性"作为更有操作性的科学评价标准。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯认识论为科学推理提供了实用且统一的认识论基础。即使仅作为非正式的思维模型加以采纳，也能减少研究者之间的摩擦、提升研究效率，并有助于恢复科学进步的累积性特征。

Abstract: Scientists routinely disagree not about data but about how to interpret evidence, because they implicitly operate from different epistemological frameworks without recognising it. The two dominant traditions, confirmationism and falsificationism, each capture genuine insights about scientific reasoning but face well-documented limitations. Confirmationism provides a natural account of how evidence supports hypotheses but cannot escape the problem of induction. Falsificationism provides logical rigour through deductive refutation but is undermined by the Duhem-Quine problem and offers no account of how scientists rationally accept theories and act on them. Here we argue that Bayesian epistemology provides a practical resolution to this impasse. By treating evidence probabilistically and operating over a finite, revisable set of hypotheses, the framework recovers the valid contributions of both traditions while addressing their core weaknesses. We show that confirmation and falsification emerge as special cases of Bayesian updating, that the subjectivity objection to priors is weaker than commonly supposed, and that the framework has direct practical consequences for study design, evidence synthesis, and publishing norms. Specifically, it replaces the falsifiability criterion with the more useful question of whether a hypothesis makes predictions that discriminate between competitors, and reframes the reproducibility crisis as an epistemological rather than a purely statistical problem. Adopting Bayesian epistemology, even informally as a mental model, can reduce friction between researchers, improve research efficiency, and help restore the cumulative character of scientific progress.

</details>


### [172] [Inference of large scale relational state processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09363)
*Rūta Juozaitienė,Ernst C. Wit*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一个连续时间框架，用于建模和推断多状态关系网络（如友谊的不同层级），将传统的二元关系分析推广到多状态情境。模型通过状态依赖协变量驱动转换强度，可分解为锚定（当前状态）和牵引（目标状态）机制，并支持线性和平滑非线性效应。针对完整事件历史和面板数据两种采样方式分别开发了高效推断方法，仿真和实证均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关系网络分析方法大多局限于二元状态（有关系/无关系），无法刻画现实中关系通过多个状态（如相识、朋友、密友）演化的过程。研究者需要分析哪些因素促成关系状态的创建、升级、降级或解除，但缺乏能够处理多状态关系网络且兼具可解释性、计算效率和灵活推断的统一框架。

Method: 提出连续时间多状态关系网络模型：每条边在多个状态间以马尔可夫过程转换，转换强度由状态依赖协变量驱动，协变量效应可分解为锚定机制（基于当前状态）和牵引机制（基于目标状态），支持线性和平滑非线性两种形式。针对完整事件历史数据，采用Cox型偏似然结合嵌套病例-对照抽样进行高效估计；针对面板数据，推导似然的一般ODE形式，二元状态下可获得特别高效的推断过程。

Result: 模拟研究验证了模型参数能被准确恢复。在青少年友谊数据的实证应用中，该框架不仅复现了已有成熟建模技术的实质性结论，还提供了显著的计算效率提升。框架保留了经典网络效应的可解释性，并将其推广到多状态关系。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将经典网络效应从二元关系推广到多状态关系，在完整事件历史和面板数据两种采样设计下均适用，兼具可解释性、计算效率和灵活性，能够扩展到更大规模、更复杂的研究设计中。

Abstract: Relational states refer to concepts such as friendship or collaboration, in which a relationship persists over a certain amount of time. Study of relational states often involves figuring out what factors contribute to the creation or dissolution of these relationships. However, most methods available now restrict their attention to binary states, i.e., ties that are either present or absent, even though many real-world systems evolve through multiple relational states (e.g., acquaintance, friendship, close friendship). We propose a continuous-time framework for modelling and inferring relational state networks in which each edge evolves by transitioning between two or more states.
  In our model, transition intensities are driven by state-dependent covariates that might be decomposed into anchoring (current-state) and pulling (target-state) mechanisms, with both linear and smooth non-linear effects. We address two common sampling regimes. With full event histories, a Cox-type partial likelihood with nested case-control sampling enables efficient estimation of both parametric and smooth effects. Instead, for panel data we derive a general ODE formulation for the likelihood, which leads to a particularly efficient inference procedure for binary state model. Simulation studies confirm accurate recovery of model parameters, and an empirical application to adolescent friendship data reproduces the substantive conclusions of established modelling techniques while offering substantial computational gains. The framework preserves the interpretability of classical network effects, generalizes them to multi-state ties, and scales to larger, more complex designs under both full-history and panel sampling designs.

</details>


### [173] [Decision-analytical models as causal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09397)
*Maurice Korf,Myriam Hunink,Richard Post,Jeremy Labrecque*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文将决策分析模型明确框架化为因果推断任务，定义并分解了决策分析模型偏倚为结构偏倚（模型偏倚）和输入参数偏倚（目标偏倚），并特别聚焦于因使用缺乏直接可观测类比的非传统目标参数而导致的目标偏倚问题，为医学决策分析建模与因果推断提供了统一的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫生经济学评价旨在通过比较不同干预措施下的成本与健康后果来回答因果问题，但潜在结局的联合分布很少能从单一数据源获得。当前实践中依赖决策分析模型综合多源因果参数来近似这些分布，然而该方法有效性的前提——即模型假设的可信度——尚未被系统性地审视。本文旨在弥补这一缺口，将决策分析建模正式纳入因果推断框架，以明确偏倚来源及其假设依赖。

Method: 本文采用概念性分析方法，将决策分析建模过程形式化为因果推断任务。核心工作包括：(1) 将决策分析模型偏倚分解为模型偏倚（源于模型结构）和目标偏倚（源于输入参数）；(2) 重点分析目标偏倚的产生机制，指出决策分析模型常需使用缺乏可直接观测类比的非传统目标参数，即使简单情境下这些参数中的偏倚也可能通过模型传播并放大；(3) 在因果推断的统一框架下，阐明因果假设在其中的角色。

Result: 研究揭示了决策分析模型偏倚的两个核心组分：模型结构偏倚和输入参数偏倚（目标偏倚）。特别指出，即使在没有复杂混杂的简单设定中，由于模型依赖非传统目标参数，目标偏倚亦可能产生并通过模型结构传播，威胁估计的有效性。最终，临床决策的可信度完全取决于其背后假设的可信度。

Conclusion: 本文为医学决策分析建模与因果推断建立了统一的理论基础，使决策分析模型潜在的偏倚及其因果假设来源得以显式化。核心结论是：决策分析模型的效度高度依赖其因果假设的可信度，临床决策不能被赋予超过其假设可信度的信任，提示研究者和决策者需审慎评估模型假设的合理性。

Abstract: Health economic evaluations are fundamentally concerned with answering causal questions by targeting estimands that contrast the costs and health consequences that would be observed under at least two different interventions. This requires the joint distribution of potential outcomes under each level of intervention, which, with appropriate causal assumptions, can in principle be identified from the joint distribution of observed health outcomes. Such data, however, are rarely available from a single source. This limitation has motivated the use of decision-analytical models to approximate the joint distribution of outcomes under each intervention directly, informed by causal parameters drawn and synthesized from multiple sources, so that the potential outcomes of interest can be approximated as an expectation over the model-implied outcome trajectories. The validity of this approach, however, depends on the credibility of the underlying assumptions. In this work, we formalize this procedure explicitly as a task of causal inference, thereby defining and decomposing decision-analytical model bias into components arising from model structure (model bias) and input parameters (target bias). Because decision-analytical models often rely on unconventional target parameters lacking straightforward observable analogues, and because bias in these parameters can propagate through the model, target bias may arise even in simple settings, a point of central focus in this work. More broadly, this work provides a unifying foundation for medical decision-analytical modelling and causal inference, making explicit the potential for decision-analytical model bias and the role of causal assumptions contributing to it. Ultimately, the resulting clinical decision is only as credible as the assumptions underlying it.

</details>


### [174] [Global testing of SNP-methylation interactions on binary phenotypes via a logistic functional regression model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09535)
*Yvelin Gansou,Karim Oualkacha,Marzia Angela Cremona,Lajmi Lakhal-Chaieb*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全局检验方法，用于检测DNA甲基化与一组SNP之间的交互作用对二分类表型的影响。该方法采用逻辑函数型回归模型，将CpG位点的甲基化数据转化为光滑函数型预测变量，并通过局部核函数与离散SNP基因型交互，实现区域层面的稳定推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多局限于SNP-CpG逐对分析，难以捕捉甲基化与遗传变异在区域层面的联合交互效应，且忽略了CpG位点之间的空间相关性，导致检验功效不足。需要一种能整合区域甲基化空间结构、同时评估多位点基因-表观遗传交互作用的全局检验框架。

Method: 提出逻辑函数型回归模型：首先将CpG位点的甲基化测量值通过基函数展开转化为光滑函数型预测变量；再引入局部核函数，将连续的函数型甲基化变量与离散SNP基因型之间的交互作用局部化建模；最终构建一个全局检验统计量，用于检验所有交互项的整体效应是否为零。

Result: 模拟研究表明，所提检验具有良好校准的一类错误率，且在检验功效上优于经典的SNP-CpG逐对分析方法。在肥胖病例-对照研究的公开甲基化与基因分型数据上进一步验证了方法的实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 该方法为基因-表观遗传交互作用分析提供了一种有效的区域层面统计推断工具，能够充分利用甲基化空间结构信息，在控制假阳性的同时提升检验灵敏度。

Abstract: Understanding how genetic and epigenetic factors jointly influence binary health outcomes remains a major challenge in biomedical research. We propose a global test for the overall effect of interactions between DNA methylation and a set of single nucleotide polymorphisms (SNPs) on a binary phenotype. We propose a logistic functional regression model in which methylation measurements at CpG sites are transformed into smooth functional predictors interacting with discrete SNP genotypes through a localized kernel. This framework enables stable inference on region-level interactions while accounting for the spatial structure of methylation around SNPs. Extensive simulations show that the proposed test provides well-calibrated type I error and improved power over classical SNP-CpG pairwise analyses. The practical relevance of the method is illustrated using publicly available methylation and genotyping data from an obesity case-control study.

</details>


### [175] [A censoring-aware target interface for tabular foundation models in survival prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09577)
*Yue Lyu,Steven H. Lin,Xuelin Huang,Ziyi Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出 SurvFM-RMST，一种将右删失生存数据转换为限制性平均生存时间（RMST）的 Jackknife 伪观测目标框架，使表格基础模型无需生存分析专用微调即可进行时间点特异的 RMST 回归，并在模拟和真实数据上验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型为异质小样本表格数据提供了可复用的预测能力，但这些模型通常假设结局完全观测；而生存分析中广泛存在的右删失使得无法直接使用普通回归标签，需要一种删失感知的接口，将删失生存数据与表格基础模型桥接起来。

Method: SurvFM-RMST 框架：将生存结局转换为限制性平均生存时间（RMST）的 Jackknife 伪观测目标，从而将生存预测问题转化为标准的回归任务，允许任意表格基础模型骨干网络在特定时间窗口下进行 RMST 回归，无需进行生存分析专用微调。

Result: 在已知条件 RMST 的受控模拟中，SurvFM-RMST 能准确恢复受限无事件时间，且伪 RMST 目标优于朴素的受限观测时间目标和仅事件目标。在 36 个 SurvSet 静态数据集上，基于表格基础模型的 SurvFM-RMST 与现有生存分析及 RMST 回归方法表现相当（相对性能因终点、时间窗口及实际约束而异），预测的 RMST 还能将留存患者分层为具有有序观测无事件时间和事件富集的组。

Conclusion: 伪 RMST 目标构建可作为一种可移植接口，有效连接删失生存数据与表格基础模型预测。

Abstract: Time-to-event prediction from tabular patient data is central to prognosis and biomedical decision support, but right-censored follow-up prevents direct use of ordinary regression labels. Tabular foundation models offer reusable prediction machinery for modest heterogeneous datasets, yet they generally assume fully observed outcomes. We introduce SurvFM-RMST, a censoring-aware target-interface framework that converts survival outcomes into jackknife pseudo-observation targets for restricted mean survival time, enabling multiple tabular backbones to perform horizon-specific RMST regression without survival-specific fine-tuning. In controlled simulations with known conditional RMST, SurvFM-RMST recovered restricted event-free time accurately, and pseudo-RMST targets outperformed naive restricted observed-time and event-only targets.
