Across 36 eligible static SurvSet datasets, SurvFM backbones were competitive with established survival and RMST-regression comparators, though relative performance varied by endpoint, horizon and practical constraints. Predicted RMST further stratified held-out patients into groups with ordered observed event-free time and event enrichment. Overall, the results support pseudo-RMST target construction as a portable interface between censored survival data and tabular foundation-model prediction.

</details>


### [176] [An Explicit Link between Extreme Value Theory and Compositional Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09567)
*Manuel Hentschel,Sebastian Engelke*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文建立了多元极值理论（Hüsler-Reiss模型）与成分数据分析之间的显式代数联系，通过斜投影、Hüsler-Reiss逆和变差图映射等变换，统一了两者中的协方差/依赖结构表示，并展示了双向方法论迁移：将极值图模型引入成分数据分析，以及将成分数据的降维方法应用于多元极值分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元极值理论和成分数据分析都处理"相对信息"为核心的数据结构——前者关注阈值超出极限在同质性下的相对轮廓，后者关注正向量在乘法尺度下的比值。尽管两者发展了许多平行的协方差与依赖表示（如变差图、协方差、精度参数化等），但两个领域之间缺乏明确的联系，限制了方法的互鉴。

Method: 作者以Hüsler-Reiss极值模型和成分数据分析中的成对对数比、中心对数比、加性对数比表示为出发点，通过斜投影、Hüsler-Reiss逆算子以及变差图映射这一小组简单变换，严格建立起两套表示体系之间的显式代数对应关系。

Result: 建立了极值理论与成分数据分析之间参量化表示的代数桥梁。基于此，成功将Hüsler-Reiss图模型引入成分数据领域，提出内蕴逻辑-正态图模型；同时展示了成分数据分析中的降维方法可用于多元极值分析。

Conclusion: 两个领域之间的代数联系不仅具有理论美学价值，更在方法论层面实现了统计工具的跨领域迁移，为成分数据的图建模和极值分析的降维提供了新途径。

Abstract: Extreme value theory and compositional data analysis both study settings where relative information plays a central role. In multivariate extreme value theory, threshold exceedance limits satisfy homogeneity properties that separate the radial size of an extreme event from its relative profile. In compositional data analysis, positive vectors are analysed up to multiplicative scale, and inference is based on ratios or log-ratios between components. Consequently, both fields have developed several covariance and dependence representations of the underlying relative structure. In the Hüsler-Reiss model for extremes, these include variogram, covariance, and precision parametrizations. In compositional data analysis, analogous representations arise from pairwise log-ratios, centred log-ratios, and additive log-ratios. We establish an explicit link between the two fields that relates these different representations by a small set of simple transformations, including oblique projections, Hüsler-Reiss inverses, and the variogram map. From a methodological perspective, leveraging this algebraic connection enables the transfer of statistical approaches from one field to the other. For instance, we introduce intrinsic logistic-normal graphical models for compositional data, which are based on Hüsler-Reiss graphical models for extremes. Conversely, we explore how dimensionality reduction methods from compositional data analysis can be applied to the analysis of multivariate extremes.

</details>


### [177] [Dynamic Frechet Regression with Feature Selection for Distributional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09613)
*Kiran Adhikari,Amrutha Dinesh,Mathew Kuttolamadom,Ying Lin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对随时间或深度等有序索引演化的分布值响应，提出动态Fréchet回归框架（DFR）。通过索引感知加权机制，在每个索引处将预测定义为分布度量空间中的加权Fréchet均值，同时结合稀疏度量学习进行几何感知的特征选择，在仿真和增材制造数据中展现出优于现有方法的预测精度与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学与工程应用中，响应并非标量或向量，而是随时间、深度等有序索引演化的概率分布等统计对象。这类动态分布轨迹的回归面临双重挑战：既要捕捉分布内部变异性，又要建模跨索引的演化规律。现有方法难以在保留分布内在几何结构的同时实现索引特异性的预测。

Method: 提出动态Fréchet回归（DFR），核心包括：(1) 索引感知加权机制：预测定义为分布度量空间（如Wasserstein空间）中的加权Fréchet均值，权重由预测变量相似度和索引邻近度联合决定；(2) 几何感知特征选择：基于稀疏度量学习识别驱动分布动态的关键预测变量，无需依赖欧氏系数。

Result: 仿真研究表明，DFR在预测精度和特征恢复方面均优于现有方法。增材制造实际数据的应用验证了其生成可解释、索引特异性分布预测的能力。

Conclusion: DFR为分布值回归提供了一个原则性框架，有效保留了分布响应的度量几何结构。通过索引感知加权实现跨邻近索引的信息借用，同时结合稀疏度量学习增强高维场景下的可解释性。该方法有望广泛应用于动态分布数据建模。

Abstract: Many scientific and engineering applications generate responses that are not scalars or vectors, but statistical objects whose form evolves over an ordered index such as time, depth. Probability distributions are a prominent example, capturing variability and uncertainty that cannot be summarized by low-dimensional statistics. When such responses are observed sequentially, the resulting dynamic distributional trajectories pose significant challenges for regression, particularly in relating scalar predictors to both within-index variability and cross-index evolution. We propose Dynamic Fréchet Regression (DFR), a framework for modeling index-dependent trajectories of distribution-valued responses. DFR extends Global Fréchet Regression by introducing an index-aware weighting mechanism. At each index, predictions are defined as weighted Fréchet means in a metric space of distributions (e.g., Wasserstein space), preserving the intrinsic geometry of the response. The weights depend jointly on predictor similarity and index proximity, enabling index-specific prediction while borrowing strength across neighboring indices. To improve interpretability in high-dimensional settings, DFR incorporates a geometry-aware feature selection approach based on sparse metric learning, which identifies predictors driving distributional dynamics without relying on Euclidean coefficients. Simulation studies show improved predictive accuracy and feature recovery over existing methods. An application to additive manufacturing data demonstrates its ability to produce interpretable, index-specific distributional predictions.

</details>


### [178] [How useful is a wrong model? Information-sharing for inference under mean misspecification in linear models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09579)
*Emily C. Hector*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的模型有用性指数，用于在均值模型不完全正确的情况下，通过收缩个体均值向共享均值模型靠拢，从而获得比纯模型方法和纯非模型方法更高效的个体化均值估计。该方法与James-Stein估计量存在联系，并通过个人追踪器数据进行了实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准统计理论在均值模型拟合不完美时会失效，导致实践中"模型拟合"与"模型有用性"之间存在矛盾。现有框架将模型二分为"正确"或"错误"，忽视了模型即使不完全正确也可能有用的现实。本文旨在打破这种二分法，在不假设模型正确的前提下仍能利用均值模型的有用性。

Method: 以线性回归框架为基础，定义了一个新的"模型有用性指数"(model usefulness index)，用于量化均值模型在多大程度上有助于个体观测的推断。基于该指数，提出一种估计量：将每个观测结果的个体化均值向共享均值模型收缩，收缩程度由有用性指数控制。文章建立了该估计量与James-Stein估计量的联系，并给出了该估计量比纯模型方法和纯非模型方法更高效的条件。此外，提出了一种数据依赖的有用性指数估计方法，兼顾统计直觉与效率。

Result: 所提出的估计量在均值模型不完全正确的情况下，能够比基于模型的方法和非基于模型的方法产生更高效的个体化均值推断。有用性指数有效指导了收缩程度的选择，数据依赖的指数估计方法在统计直觉与效率之间取得了平衡。

Conclusion: 不应将模型的正确性与有用性混为一谈。通过有用性指数将个体均值向共享均值模型收缩，可以在不假设模型正确的前提下实现更高效推断，为实践中模型拟合不完美时的统计推断提供了新范式。

Abstract: As almost all models are wrong, a mean model's usefulness is often accepted as sufficient justification for its use. In practice, however, standard statistical theory breaks when the mean model fit is imperfect, limiting this usefulness. This tension between fit and usefulness arises from a dichotomization of model fit: the model is either right or it is wrong. Motivated by the linear regression framework, we propose an alternative viewpoint that leverages the mean model's usefulness without assuming it is right or wrong. We define a new model usefulness index and use it to share information across individual observations through the mean model. The result is an estimator of each outcome's mean that shrinks individualized means towards the shared mean model, with the degree of shrinkage governed by this usefulness index. We draw connections between our estimator and the James-Stein estimator and establish when and how our estimators of the individualized means yield more efficient inference than model-based and non-model-based alternatives. We also propose a data-dependent estimate of the usefulness index that balances statistical intuition with efficiency considerations. We illustrate our method's practical value in an analysis of personal tracker data.

</details>


### [179] [SPOT-IC: Improving prediction for interval-censored data via survival probability transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09640)
*Jingyi Hu,Yu Gu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对区间删失数据的迁移学习方法，通过精心设计的惩罚项将源研究的生存概率信息迁移至目标研究，无需共享个体级数据，并支持多源数据的自适应聚合以避免负迁移，理论上证明可加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在慢性病研究中，区间删失数据因删失区间宽、随访有限而难以准确预测。现有迁移学习方法要么对模型或参数相似性施加严格假设，要么需要获取源研究的个体级数据，存在隐私和可行性问题。

Method: 提出一种新颖的迁移学习方法：(1) 通过精心设计的惩罚项将源研究的生存概率信息迁移到目标研究，允许任意源模型且无需共享个体数据；(2) 基于简单的EM算法实现高效计算；(3) 当多个源研究可用且信息量未知时，开发数据自适应聚合程序，对负迁移具有鲁棒性。

Result: 理论分析表明，只要至少有一个源研究具有足够信息量，所提估计量就能达到比仅使用目标数据的估计量更快的收敛速度。在广泛的模拟研究和阿尔茨海默病神经影像学倡议（ADNI）数据的实际应用中，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法在保护数据隐私的前提下，有效利用多源辅助信息提升区间删失数据的预测精度，具有理论保证和实用价值，特别适用于慢性疾病研究等删失严重、随访有限的场景。

Abstract: Accurate prediction with interval-censored data is particularly challenging when censoring intervals are wide and follow-up is limited, as is common in studies of chronic diseases. Although auxiliary information from source studies may improve prediction in a target study, existing transfer learning methods typically impose restrictive assumptions on model or parameter similarity, or require access to individual-level source data. We propose a novel transfer learning method for interval-censored data that allows arbitrary source models and avoids sharing source data. Our approach transfers survival probability information from source studies through a carefully designed penalty and enables efficient computation via a simple EM algorithm. When multiple source studies are available and their informativeness is unknown, we further develop a data-adaptive aggregation procedure that is robust to negative transfer. Theoretical analysis shows that the proposed estimator attains a faster convergence rate than the target-only estimator whenever at least one source study is sufficiently informative. Extensive simulation studies and an application to data from the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [EHR-MPC: Inference-Time Control for Sepsis Treatment with Generative Patient Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08793)
*Joshua Pickard,Wei Qi,Na Li,Ann Woolley,Lisa Cosimi,Roy Kishony,Deborah Hung*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出 EHR-MPC 框架，通过生成式电子健康记录模型构建患者数字孪生，将脓毒症治疗优化解耦为学习患者动态与推理时模型预测控制（MPC），在 8 家医院的多中心 ICU 队列上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法学习固定的脓毒症治疗策略，在推理阶段无法灵活适应变化的临床目标，亟需一种能在推理时动态优化治疗决策的框架。

Method: EHR-MPC 框架将学习患者动态与治疗优化解耦：(1) 训练一个生成式电子健康记录模型作为患者数字孪生，预测干预措施下的临床轨迹；(2) 利用模型预测控制（MPC），在推理时通过仿真规划优化治疗策略。评估采用 Mass General Brigham 系统 8 家医院的多中心 ICU 脓毒症队列，同时使用 off-policy 重要性采样和 on-policy 仿真两种评估方式。

Result: 与强化学习基线相比，EHR-MPC 在 off-policy 评估中取得可比的性能，在仿真评估中表现更优。

Conclusion: 该工作将脓毒症治疗优化重新定义为对已学习患者动态的推理时控制，建立了基于生成式临床模型进行决策的通用框架，克服了传统 RL 固定策略的局限性。

Abstract: Sepsis is a leading cause of mortality, yet optimal treatment policies remain contested. Existing reinforcement learning (RL) approaches learn fixed strategies for sepsis treatment, limiting adaptability to changing clinical objectives during inference. We propose EHRMPC, a framework that decouples learning patient dynamics from optimizing treatment by training a patient digital twin in the form of a generative electronic health record (EHR) model. The digital twin predicts clinical trajectories under interventions and enables model predictive control (MPC) to optimize treatments via inference-time planning over simulations. We evaluate EHR-MPC on a multicenter ICU sepsis cohort spanning 8 hospitals in the Mass General Brigham health system using both off-policy importance sampling and on-policy simulation-based evaluation. Relative to RL baselines, EHR-MPC achieves comparable off-policy performance and improved simulation performance. Unlike RL, this work frames sepsis treatment optimization as inference-time control over learned patient dynamics, establishing a general framework for decision making with generative clinical models.

</details>


### [181] [Influence Diagnostics in High-dimensional M-estimation: Precise Asymptotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09250)
*Hugo Cui*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 在高维极限下（n与d同阶），本文严格刻画了凸M估计中留一影响（leave-one-out influence）的极限分布，并揭示了有影响力的样本倾向于靠近决策边界，与主动学习中的数据选择启发式方法建立联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典留一影响分析仅适用于低维大样本（n→∞, d固定）情形，但在高维设定（n与d同阶）中，单个训练样本的影响会与其他所有样本产生复杂依赖，其统计特性尚不清楚。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 在高斯设计下的凸M估计框架中，采用高维极限分析（n≍d→∞），推导留一影响在整个训练集上的分布收敛到某个极限测度，并对其进行精确刻画。

Result: 证明了留一影响的分布收敛于一个可精确刻画的极限测度；同时发现高影响力样本倾向于位于决策边界附近，为主动学习中的边界采样策略提供了理论支撑。

Conclusion: 本文在凸M估计的高维设定下，首次给出了留一影响分布的完整理论刻画，并建立了影响分析与主动学习中决策边界采样之间的理论联系。

Abstract: The impact of a given training point on a statistical model is classically measured through its leave-one-out influence, which quantifies the effect of its removal from the training set on the model accuracy. While the statistics of leave-one-out influences are well understood in the low-dimensional, large sample limit $n\to \infty, d=O(1)$, they become more intricate in high dimensions, as the influence of a given sample develops non-trivial dependencies on all other training samples. For convex M-estimation under Gaussian design, in the high-dimensional limit $n\asymp d$, we show that the distribution of the influences across the training set converges to a limiting measure which we sharply characterize. Building on these results, we provide evidence that influential samples tend to lie close to the decision boundary, thereby making contact with a standard data selection heuristic in active learning.

</details>


### [182] [Spectrally Deconfounded Gradient Boosting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09371)
*Andrea Nava,Peter Bühlmann,Fabio Sigrist*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向梯度提升的非线性谱去混杂框架，通过谱损失替代普通平方误差损失来收缩混杂对齐方向上的学习，并结合正则化与早停实现去混杂，同时提供了混合模型解释和经验贝叶斯调参方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵活的机器学习方法对隐藏混杂因素敏感，可能学到由未观测混杂因子诱导的虚假关联而非稳定的因果信号。现有谱去混杂方法主要局限于线性模型，缺乏适用于梯度提升等非线性方法的有效方案。

Method: 作者提出非线性谱去混杂框架：用谱损失（spectral loss）替代普通平方误差损失，通过对协变量矩阵的高方差方向施加收缩来减缓混杂对齐方向上的学习。关键创新在于揭示去混杂并非仅靠谱损失实现，而是谱收缩与正则化（尤其是早停）的交互作用。此外，通过混合模型解释将LAVA型收缩与随机效应调整联系起来，导出经验贝叶斯调参程序，并利用拉普拉斯近似和核随机效应将方法扩展至一般似然和非线性混杂。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界实验中，谱去混杂提升方法在隐藏混杂条件下改善了目标函数的估计，且相比现有非线性谱去混杂基线方法具有显著更高的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 谱去混杂可通过谱损失、正则化与早停的协同作用成功扩展到梯度提升框架中，形成可扩展的非线性去混杂方法，兼具理论解释性和实用调参方案。

Abstract: Flexible machine-learning methods can be sensitive to hidden confounding: they may learn associations induced by unobserved confounders rather than stable signals. Spectral deconfounding mitigates this problem by shrinking high-variance directions of the covariate matrix that, under dense confounding, carry latent confounder information. Existing work has largely focused on linear models. We develop a nonlinear spectral deconfounding framework for gradient boosting. Our approach replaces the ordinary squared-error loss by a spectral loss, which alters the boosting dynamics by slowing down learning in confounding-aligned directions. We show that deconfounding is not achieved by the spectral loss alone, but by the interaction between spectral shrinkage and regularization, especially in terms of early stopping. Moreover, we provide a mixed-model interpretation that connects LAVA-type shrinkage to random-effects adjustment and yields an empirical-Bayes procedure for tuning the spectral loss. We also extend the method to general likelihoods and nonlinear confounding using Laplace approximations and kernel random effects. Across synthetic and real-world experiments, spectrally deconfounded boosting improves estimation of the target function under hidden confounding and is substantially more scalable than existing nonlinear spectral deconfounding baselines.

</details>


### [183] [Characterization of the basin of convexity for multi-snapshot spike deconvolution via variable projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09593)
*Meghna Kalra,Maxime Ferreira Da Costa,Kiryung Lee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究多快照尖峰反卷积问题，采用变量投影方法将问题简化为仅关于尖峰位置的非凸最小二乘问题（VarProSD）。作者通过Beurling–Selberg极值逼近，显式刻画了目标函数的凸性盆地区域，建立了随机噪声下估计量的一致性及对抗噪声下的误差界，并证明了梯度下降在盆地内的局部收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多快照尖峰反卷积在超分辨率显微成像、雷达目标检测等领域具有重要应用。现有方法大多缺乏对优化景观的精确几何刻画，作者旨在填补这一空白：通过变量投影消除振幅变量，获得仅关于位置的简化目标函数，并利用解析工具（Beurling–Selberg逼近）给出与PSF无关的尖锐理论保证。

Method: （1）采用变量投影（Variable Projection）消除振幅的闭式解，将多快照尖峰反卷积转化为仅关于尖峰位置的非凸最小二乘问题（VarProSD）；（2）利用功率谱密度和光滑性等PSF性质，显式刻画VarProSD目标函数的凸性盆地，揭示采样带宽与尖峰分离度对局部几何的影响；（3）借助Beurling–Selberg极值逼近，获得结构化矩阵条件数的PSF无关尖锐界；（4）在凸性盆地内，证明随机噪声下估计量依快照数收敛的一致性，以及对抗噪声下基于逆映射局部Lipschitz性质的更紧误差界；（5）证明梯度下降在盆地内初始化的局部收敛性，并提出改进的ESPRIT初始化加梯度精炼的算法框架。

Result: （1）显式给出了VarProSD目标函数凸性盆地的解析条件，其依赖于PSF的功率谱密度和光滑性，以及采样带宽与尖峰最小分离度；（2）在凸性盆地内，建立了随机噪声下估计量随快照数增加的一致性；（3）在对抗噪声下给出了基于逆映射局部Lipschitz常数的更紧误差界；（4）证明了从盆地内任意点出发，梯度下降线性收敛到真实尖峰位置；（5）数值实验验证了改进ESPRIT初始化结合梯度精炼方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文通过变量投影和Beurling–Selberg极值逼近工具，为多快照尖峰反卷积提供了完整的优化景观分析和理论保证，揭示了PSF属性、采样带宽和尖峰分离度如何共同决定问题的局部几何性质，并为梯度下降方法提供了可验证的收敛条件。

Abstract: We study the problem of multi-snapshot spike deconvolution, where the goal is to recover the locations of sparse impulses from their noisy convolution with a known point spread function (PSF) across multiple snapshots. We adopt a variable-projection formulation that eliminates the amplitudes in closed form, reducing the task to a nonconvex least-squares problem over the spike locations alone, which we refer to as the variable-projection formulation of spike deconvolution (VarProSD). We provide an explicit characterization of the basin of convexity of the VarProSD objective in terms of key PSF properties, including its power spectral density and smoothness, revealing how sampling bandwidth and spike separation influence the local geometry. Within this basin, we establish that the estimator is consistent in the number of snapshots under stochastic noise, and provide a complementary, sharper error bound under adversarial noise via the local Lipschitz property of the inverse map. We further show local convergence guarantees for gradient descent when initialized within the basin. A central ingredient throughout is the use of Beurling--Selberg extremal approximations, which enable sharp, PSF-agnostic bounds on the conditioning of the structured matrices arising in the optimization landscape. Numerical experiments validate our theoretical findings and demonstrate the effectiveness of modified ESPRIT initialization followed by gradient-based refinement.

</details>


### [184] [Deep Gaussian Processes on Directed Acyclic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09645)
*Federico L. Perlino,Oliver Hamelijnck,Adam M. Johansen,Theodoros Damoulas*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出在DAG上构建深度高斯过程（Deep GPs on DAGs），理论研究其先验坍缩行为及图拓扑对信息保存的影响，推导出输入区分性保持的几乎必然下界，并设计结构化变分近似以保留图依赖和组合不确定性，最终在蛋白质信号网络、多保真度仿真等任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现实过程可表示为有向无环图（DAG）上的函数复合（因果机制、多保真度建模、基因调控网络等），但这些函数通常被部分观测、含噪声且采样异质，给重建、不确定性传播和推断带来巨大挑战。现有方法难以在保留图结构依赖的同时处理组合不确定性。

Method: （1）在DAG上对函数施加高斯过程先验，自然导出DAG上的深度高斯过程形式化框架；（2）理论分析先验坍缩行为，研究图拓扑与中间观测对信息保存的影响，推导出输入区分性在深度上保持的几乎必然渐近频率下界，并确定使该下界成立的一般核函数类，同时证明了Dunlop等人关于输入连接作用的观察；（3）提出结构化变分近似方法，能保留图依赖、组合不确定性，并捕捉碰撞节点的解释消除（explaining-away）行为。

Result: 理论结果：得到输入区分性保持的几乎必然下界，确定了广泛的核函数类。实验验证：在潜碰撞DAG、蛋白质信号传导网络和多保真度重离子碰撞仿真三个任务上，达到最先进性能，同时能恢复低保真度贡献并提供仿真器层级的可解释性。

Conclusion: DAG上的深度高斯过程为图结构函数复合建模提供了原则性框架。理论分析揭示了图拓扑和中间观测在防止先验坍缩中的关键作用，结构化变分近似有效保留了组合不确定性。该方法在多个真实任务上表现出色，兼具预测性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Many real-world processes can be represented as compositions of functions along a directed acyclic graph (DAG). In causal modelling, these correspond to the underlying mechanisms; in engineering, to multiple fidelity levels; and in gene-regulatory networks, to transcription factors. These functions are partially observed across the DAG, with noisy and heterogeneously sampled measurements, posing significant challenges for reconstruction, uncertainty propagation, and inference. To tackle these challenges, we place priors over functions and naturally arrive at Deep Gaussian Processes over DAGs. We theoretically study their prior-collapse behaviour, and the effect of graph topology and intermediate observations on the preservation of information. We obtain almost-sure lower bounds on the asymptotic frequency of depths at which the distinction between inputs is preserved, identify broad kernel classes for which these hold, and prove an observation by \cite{dunlop2018} on the role of input connections. We offer a structured variational approximation that retains graph dependencies, preserves compositional uncertainty, and captures the explaining-away behaviour of colliders. Finally, we empirically validate our theoretical results and our methodology, and model a latent-collider DAG, a protein signalling network, and a multi-fidelity heavy-ion collision emulation task, attaining state-of-the-art performance while recovering low-fidelity contributions and yielding interpretability of the simulator hierarchy.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Interval Certifications for Multilayered Perceptrons via Lattice Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08773)
*Merkouris Papamichail,Konstantinos Varsos,Giorgos Flouris,João Marques-Silva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将对抗鲁棒性问题归约为格遍历问题。每个格元素对应一个包围输入点的轴对齐超矩形（区间）。作者区分了两种认证：声音认证（区间内扰动不改变预测）和完备认证（超出区间必改变预测）。通过"精化-验证"迭代框架，利用形式化验证器可保证声音的最大性和完备的最小性。优化问题中存在不对称性：完备认证可在多项式次预言调用内求得最小解，而声音认证被证明是强难解的。对称区间（ℓ∞球）上的优化问题给出了对数算法，并在ParallelepipedoNN系统上进行了实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗鲁棒性是AI安全的基础问题。现有研究主要关注声音认证（sound certification），即寻找一个保证不改变预测的区间，但完备认证（complete certification）——即寻找一个保证一旦超出就改变预测的区间——在文献中尚未被研究。本文旨在建立统一的理论框架，同时处理这两种对偶的认证问题，并揭示它们之间的计算复杂性不对称性。

Method: 核心方法是将对抗鲁棒性归约为格遍历问题，其中格元素为轴对齐超矩形（区间）。作者提出"精化-验证"（refine & verify）迭代框架，使用格遍历算子结合形式化MLP验证器，保证声音认证的最大性和完备认证的最小性。对于优化问题，分别分析了完备认证（多项式预言调用可解）、声音认证（证明强难解性）、以及对称区间（ℓ∞球，给出对数算法）三类情形。实证部分基于新开发的ParallelepipedoNN系统。

Result: 1. 完备认证的最小化问题可在多项式次预言调用内求解；2. 声音认证的最大化问题被证明是强难解的（strong intractability）；3. 对称区间（ℓ∞球）上的优化问题存在对数时间算法；4. 揭示了声音认证与完备认证之间的计算不对称性；5. 通过ParallelepipedoNN系统进行了实证验证。

Conclusion: 本文建立了对抗鲁棒性的格遍历统一框架，首次形式化定义了完备认证问题。核心发现是声音认证与完备认证之间存在显著的计算不对称性——前者强难解，后者多项式可解。对称区间情形下存在高效对数算法。该框架为AI安全的形式化验证提供了新的理论视角和实用工具。

Abstract: In this work we present a rigorous theoretical framework to a foundational problem of AI safety, namely adversarial robustness. In particular, we show that the adversarial robustness problem can be reduced to a lattice traversal problem. Each element of this lattice corresponds to an interval, i.e., an axis-aligned hyper-rectangle, containing an input point $\mathbf{x}$. Consider a multilayered perceptron classifier (MLP). An interval $I$ constitutes a sound certification if $\mathbf{x} \in I$ and $\mathbf{x}$ can be freely perturbed in $I$ without changing the MLP's prediction. Complementarily, an interval $I$ constitutes a complete certification if $\mathbf{x} \in I$ and when $\mathbf{x}$ moves outside of $I$ the MLP's prediction is guaranteed to change. While the sound certification problem corresponds to the well-studied adversarial robustness, complete certifications have not been examined in the literature. We develop lattice traversal operators, which we apply in a refine & verify iterative scheme. Using formal MLP verifiers, sound maximality and complete minimality are guaranteed. Moreover, we examine objective optimization problems. There we discover some interesting asymmetries. For complete certifications, the minimum solution is obtained in polynomial oracle calls. This does not hold for sound certifications, where we prove strong intractability results. Additionally, we examine optimization problems in symmetric intervals (i.e., $\ell_\infty$-spheres), where we provide logarithmic algorithms. Finally, we present an empirical evaluation, using the novel ParallelepipedoNN system.

</details>


### [186] [CogniConsole: Externalizing Inference-Time Control as a Formal Abstraction for Reliable LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08774)
*Vanessa Figueiredo,Wilter Franceschi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"LLM可靠性仅取决于模型能力"的传统观点，证明推理时控制（inference-time control）对可靠性有显著影响。作者提出CogniConsole架构，通过将控制外部化为结构化接口，结合程序化协调与有界提示推理，系统性地降低输出方差和失败率，为将推理时控制视为一等抽象提供了实证基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM系统的可靠性通常被归结为模型能力问题（通过扩展模型规模来解决），但作者观察到许多常见故障模式（如上下文漂移、约束遵循不一致）可能并非源于能力不足，而是源于推理时控制层的规范不足。因此需要探究：在固定模型架构下，通过改进推理时控制能否系统性地提升可靠性。

Method: 提出CogniConsole架构，将推理时控制外部化为一个结构化接口，融合程序化协调（programmatic coordination）与有界提示推理（bounded prompt-based reasoning）。设计"面向可控性的探针"（controllability-oriented probes），在N=489的多步交互环境中进行实验，沿"非结构化→完全结构化支撑"的梯度增加结构支撑强度，对比不同条件下的输出方差和失败率。

Result: 在固定模型架构下，随着结构支撑从非结构化逐步增强到完全结构化，输出方差和失败率均系统性降低。这表明上下文漂移、约束遵循不一致等故障模式主要源于控制层规范不足，而非模型能力不足。

Conclusion: 推理时控制应被提升为一等抽象（first-class abstraction），而非仅作为模型能力的附属。这为超越单纯扩展模型规模的LLM系统设计与评估开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Reliability in large language model (LLM) systems is typically framed as a function of model capability. We challenge this by demonstrating that reliability is significantly influenced by \emph{inference-time control} -- the computational layer governing task framing and context selection. We introduce \emph{CogniConsole}, an architectural instantiation that externalizes this control into a structured interface combining programmatic coordination with bounded prompt-based reasoning. Through \emph{controllability-oriented probes} ($N=489$) in a multi-step interactive environment, we show that increasing structural scaffolding -- from unstructured to fully scaffolded -- \textbf{systematically reduces output variance and failure rates under a fixed model architecture}. Our results indicate that many observed failure modes, such as context drift and inconsistent constraint adherence, arise from under-specified control rather than insufficient capability. This work provides an empirical basis for treating inference-time control as a first-class abstraction, opening new directions for designing and evaluating LLM systems beyond scaling alone.

</details>


### [187] [GATS: Graph-Augmented Tree Search with Layered World Models for Efficient Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08894)
*Maureese Williams,Dymitr Nowicki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GATS 提出一种基于图增强树搜索的规划框架，通过三层世界模型替代 LLM 推理，在规划阶段实现零 LLM 调用、100% 成功率，且结果完全确定性，显著优于 LATS 和 ReAct。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM Agent 规划方法（如 LATS、ReAct）在规划阶段重度依赖 LLM 推理，导致计算成本高、行为随机且不稳定。需一种在推理时消除 LLM 调用同时保持或提升规划性能的方法。

Method: 提出 GATS（Graph-Augmented Tree Search）框架，核心为：(1) 基于 UCB1 的系统性树搜索策略；(2) 三层世界模型——L1 精确符号动作匹配、L2 从执行日志中学习的统计模型、L3 针对未知动作的 LLM 预测。三层模型逐步退化，在推理时完全替代 LLM 调用。

Result: 在含分支路径和死胡同的合成规划任务上，GATS 达 100% 成功率（LATS 92%，ReAct 64%）。在涵盖编程工作流、网页导航、长时域任务等 12 个压力测试场景中，GATS 保持 100% 成功率（LATS 降至 88.9%，ReAct 仅 23.9%）。GATS 每次任务规划阶段零 LLM 调用（LATS 每任务需 37 次），且跨运行方差为零。

Conclusion: 系统性搜索结合可学习世界模型可以大幅超越 LLM 引导的探索策略，为 Agent 规划提供了一种高效、确定性强且低成本的替代方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown promise in multi-step planning tasks, but existing approaches like LATS (Language Agent Tree Search) and ReAct rely heavily on LLM inference during planning, leading to high computational costs and stochastic behavior. We present \textbf{GATS} (Graph-Augmented Tree Search), a planning framework that combines systematic UCB1-based tree search with a layered world model to eliminate LLM calls during inference while achieving superior planning performance. Our three-layer world model integrates: (L1) exact symbolic action matching, (L2) statistics learned from execution logs, and (L3) LLM-based prediction for unknown actions. On synthetic planning tasks with branching paths and dead-ends, GATS achieves \textbf{100\% success rate} compared to 92 % for LATS and 64\% for ReAct. On a comprehensive stress test spanning 12 challenging scenarios -- including coding workflows, web navigation, and long-horizon tasks -- GATS maintains \textbf{100\% success} while LATS drops to 88.9 % and ReAct to 23.9%. GATS requires \textbf{zero LLM calls per task} during planning (vs. 37 per task for LATS) and produces deterministic plans with zero variance across runs. Our results demonstrate that systematic search with learned world models can substantially outperform LLM-guided exploration for agent planning.

</details>


### [188] [Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench: Testing the Limits of Agents on Long-Horizon Terminal Tasks with Dense Reward-Based Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08964)
*Zongxia Li,Zhongzhi Li,Yucheng Shi,Ruhan Wang,Junyao Yang,Zhichao Liu,Xiyang Wu,Anhao Li,Yue Yu,Ninghao Liu,Lichao Sun,Haotao Mi,LeoweiLiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了 Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench，一个包含 46 个长时程终端任务的基准，通过细粒度子任务分解提供密集中间奖励，评估前沿模型后发现最强模型完美通过率仅 10.9%，揭示了长时程终端智能体的巨大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终端基准测试存在两大缺陷：(1) 任务过于简单，数分钟内即可完成，无法考验长时程规划与迭代调试能力；(2) 仅以最终结果评估，缺乏中间过程奖励信号，无法刻画智能体的部分进展与渐进式问题解决能力。

Method: 构建 Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench 基准，涵盖 46 个长时程任务，横跨实验复现、软件工程、多模态分析、交互游戏、科学计算等 9 个类别。每个任务采用 Terminal-Bench 风格设定，配备参考解或仿真引擎，并进一步分解为细粒度分级子任务，实现密集中间奖励和部分得分机制。评估 15 个前沿模型，记录 token 消耗、episode 数、执行时间等指标，并分析失败模式与错误模式。

Result: 智能体平均每任务消耗 990 万 token，约 231 个 episode，运行 85.3 分钟。最强模型在 0.95 部分奖励阈值下 pass@1 仅 15.2%，在 1.0 完美奖励阈值下仅 10.9%；全部模型的平均通过率分别为 4.3% 和 1.7%。

Conclusion: Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench 显著比现有终端基准更具挑战性，当前最先进模型在长时程终端任务上仍有巨大提升空间。该基准的发布将为长时程终端智能体的研究提供有力支撑。

Abstract: AI agents have become capable of autonomously completing short, well-specified tasks. However, existing terminal benchmarks largely focus on simple problems that finish within minutes and are evaluated only by their final outcome. This setup overlooks intermediate progress and partial solutions, yielding sparse reward signals and an incomplete picture of agent capability. We introduce Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench, a terminal benchmark of 46 long-horizon tasks spanning nine categories, including experiment reproduction, software engineering, multimodal analysis, interactive games, and scientific computing. Each task follows a Terminal-Bench-style setup with a reference solution or simulation engine, but is further decomposed into fine-grained graded subtasks. This design enables dense intermediate rewards and partial credit, allowing evaluation to capture not only whether an agent reaches the final goal, but also how far it progresses on open-ended workflows. Tasks in Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench typically require hundreds of episodes and minutes to hours of execution, stressing long-horizon planning, long-context management, and iterative debugging rather than one-shot problem solving. We evaluate 15 frontier models and find that agents consume on average 9.9M tokens per task, with roughly 231 episodes and 85.3 minutes of execution time per run, making Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench more demanding than prior terminal-based benchmarks. Even the strongest tested model achieves 15.2% pass@1 at a partial-reward threshold of 0.95 and 10.9% at a perfect-reward threshold of 1.0, while the mean pass rate across models is 4.3% and 1.7% under the two thresholds, respectively. These results reveal headroom for improvement. We further analyze failure modes and error patterns, and release Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench to support future progress on long-horizon terminal agents.

</details>


### [189] [A Formalization of the Mean-Field Derivation of the Vlasov Equation: AI-Assisted Lean Formalization as a Strategy Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08986)
*Joseph K. Miller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将一个数学研究成果在 Lean 4 中形式化，并将其框架化为一场"形式化博弈"：由数学家指导 AI 系统将 LaTeX 文档转化为 Lean 代码。以 Vlasov 方程适定性（Dobrushin 平均场路径）为案例，完成了包括存在唯一性、稳定性估计、平均场极限及叠加原理的完整公理化形式化，同时分离出一层关于 Wasserstein-1 度量和 Kantorovich-Rubinstein 对偶定理的自包含通用数学层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种新的数学形式化范式：由人类数学家担任"导演"，AI 系统担任"执行者"，将形式化过程视为一场有明确胜负条件的博弈。旨在降低形式化门槛、加速大型数学成果的形式化验证，并探讨形式化过程中可复用数学层的自然涌现。

Method: 提出"形式化博弈"框架，胜负条件为：代码编译通过、无 `sorry`、目标定理仅依赖 Lean 基础公理。引入"复用性"第二重检验——形式化成果是否形成可被 Mathlib 吸收的自包含通用数学层。案例研究选取非线性 Vlasov 方程适定性的完整形式化。人类数学家负责范围界定、分解指导、库缺口分类；AI 代理负责生成证明。最终分离出仅依赖 Mathlib 的独立层（299 个声明中的 49 个，22 个声明接口，无反向依赖）。

Result: 头条定理在一周内完成形式化，整个开发约一个月。成功将完整的形式化证明分解为可复用的 Wasserstein-1 / Kantorovich-Rubinstein 层（约占开发量的六分之一），该层可独立编译于 Mathlib 之上。所有目标定理均通过机器检查，仅依赖 Lean 基础公理。

Conclusion: "形式化博弈"作为一种方法论框架具有工具无关性，可超越单次运行的特定系统。人类导演 + AI 执行者的协作模式在实际案例中展现出可行性，且形式化过程自然促进了可复用数学基础设施的识别与分离。

Abstract: We formalize a research result in the Lean 4 proof assistant by having a mathematician direct an AI system, and frame the activity as a formalization game. The objective is to turn a LaTeX document into Lean. The game is won when the development compiles, contains no sorry, and a machine check shows the target theorems rest on Lean's foundational axioms alone. Reuse is a second check, by a definition we introduce: whether the development yields a self-contained layer of general mathematics the wider library could absorb. The case study is a complete, axiom-clean formalization of well-posedness for the nonlinear Vlasov equation via Dobrushin's mean-field route -- existence, uniqueness, the stability estimate and mean-field limit, and a short-window superposition principle (weak solutions are Lagrangian). The human's role was to direct, not to write proofs: to scope the definitions, steer the decompositions, and triage the library's gaps; the AI agent executed. The formalization certifies the proof of each statement as written; whether the written statement is the intended theorem stays the mathematician's judgment. The optimal-transport machinery that fell out of the build (in particular, properties of the Wasserstein-1 metric and the Kantorovich-Rubinstein duality theorem) separates into a self-contained layer that compiles against Mathlib alone: about a sixth of the development (49 of 299 declarations), behind a 22-declaration interface with no reverse dependency. The headline theorems ran in about a week, the full development in about a month. We report the quantitative claims as observations of one game, not as general laws. The game's rules name no particular system, so the methodological framing is meant to outlast the tools of any one run.

</details>


### [190] [ARCANA: A Reflective Multi-Agent Program Synthesis Framework for ARC-AGI-2 Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09059)
*Kunbo Zhang,Lei Fu,Zeyu Wang,Zijing Liu,Kejian Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARCANA 是一个多智能体协作框架，通过感知、假设生成、符号执行和反思修正的迭代循环，结合结构化程序搜索和自适应多轮纠错，在严格约束下求解 ARC AGI-2 抽象推理任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在 ARC AGI-2 这类抽象变换任务上，面对严格的测试时间和硬件约束时，推理效率与求解质量不足，需要一种能高效进行结构化程序搜索并具备自适应纠错能力的框架。

Method: 提出 ARCANA 框架，包含四个专业智能体：(1) 感知锚定智能体从原始网格构建以对象为中心的场景图；(2) 潜在程序策略智能体提出多样化的 DSL 候选程序；(3) 符号执行器在示例上验证候选程序；(4) 反思智能体综合失败驱动的反馈供下一轮迭代。智能体间通过共享可微黑板通信，并由学习到的元控制器进行调度。

Result: 该框架在具有挑战性的抽象变换任务上，提升了推理效率和求解质量。

Conclusion: 通过将结构化程序搜索与自适应多轮修正相结合，ARCANA 有效提升了在严格约束下求解 ARC AGI-2 任务的能力，展示了多智能体协作在抽象推理中的优势。

Abstract: We present ARCANA, a collaborative multi agent framework for solving ARC AGI 2 tasks under strict test time and hardware constraints. ARCANA decomposes each task into iterative perception, hypothesis generation, symbolic execution, and reflective refinement. A perceptual grounding agent builds object centric scene graphs from raw grids, a latent program policy proposes diverse DSL programs, a symbolic executor verifies candidates on demonstrations, and a reflective agent synthesizes failure driven feedback for the next turn. These agents communicate through a shared differentiable blackboard and are scheduled by a learned meta controller. The design combines structured program search with adaptive multi turn correction, improving reasoning efficiency and solution quality on challenging abstract transformation tasks.

</details>


### [191] [Neuro-Agentic Control: A Deep Learning-based LLM-Powered Agentic AI Framework for Controlling Security Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09076)
*Saroj Gopali,Bipin Chhetri,Deepika Giri,Sima Siami-Namini,Akbar Siami Namin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经-智能体控制框架，将LLM规划器与预训练时序基础模型TimesFM耦合，通过"反事实物理注入"机制在动作执行前模拟LLM所提议干预措施的物理影响，从而拒绝幻觉或不安全行为。在SWaT数据集上评估，该框架防止了33.3%的违规行为，优于LSTM（26.7%）和TCN（13.3%），且零物理无效动作执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业物联网中针对操作技术的网络攻击日益造成昂贵的停机和物理损害，传统基于规则的监控存在局限。虽然LLM具备强大的语义推理能力可用于决策支持，但其幻觉特性对闭环控制构成不可接受的安全风险。因此需要一种将LLM推理能力与物理真实性保障相结合的自主防御架构。

Method: 提出"神经-智能体控制框架"（Neuro-Agentic Control Framework），核心创新包括：（1）将LLM规划器（如Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite）与预训练时序基础模型TimesFM耦合；（2）引入"反事实物理注入"机制，在LLM提议的干预措施实际执行前，在基础模型的数值隐空间内模拟其物理影响，使系统能够拒绝幻觉或不安全的动作。在SWaT数据集上以随机攻击场景进行评估，与LSTM和TCN基线对比。

Result: 在随机攻击场景下的SWaT数据集上，神经-智能体循环防止了33.3%的违规行为（低于阈值），优于LSTM基线（26.7%）和TCN基线（13.3%），且实现了零物理无效（幻觉）动作执行。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将基础模型用作确定性"哨兵"来保障关键基础设施中的智能体AI是有效的。该框架成功解决了LLM在闭环工业控制中的幻觉安全问题，为自主物理防御提供了新范式。

Abstract: Cyberattacks on operational technology are increasingly causing costly downtime and physical damage, exposing the limitations of traditional rule-based monitoring in industrial IoT environments. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have strong semantic reasoning abilities to assist in decision support, their hallucinatory nature presents unacceptable safety liabilities for closed-loop control. This paper introduces a neuro-agentic control framework, a novel architecture that couples an LLM-based planner (i.e., such as Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite) with a pre-trained Time-Series Foundation Model (TimesFM), to achieve physics-grounded autonomous defense. The paper introduces a ``Counterfactual Physics Injection'' mechanism that simulates the impact of LLM-proposed interventions within the numerical latent space of the foundation model before actuation, while allowing the system to reject hallucinatory or unsafe actions. Evaluated on an industrial dataset (e.g., the Secure Water Treatment (SWaT)) in the context of stochastic attack scenarios, the framework exhibited better performance compared to LSTM and TCN baselines. The Neuro-Agentic Loop prevented five breaches (33.3%) below the threshold versus LSTM (26.7%) and TCN (13.3%), with zero physically invalid (hallucinated) actions executed. These results demonstrate the efficacy of using foundation models as deterministic ``Sentinels'' to safeguard agentic AI in critical infrastructure.

</details>


### [192] [L-MAD: A Systematic Evaluation of Multi-Agent Debate Structures in Legal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09099)
*Tan-Minh Nguyen,Hoang-Trung Nguyen,Huu-Dong Nguyen,Dinh-Truong Do,Thi-Hai-Yen Vuong,Le-Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Legal Multi-Agent Debate (L-MAD)框架，在法律文本蕴涵任务中系统评估多智能体辩论结构与聚合方法，发现增加智能体数量可提升准确率，但延长辩论轮次会导致"过度审议漂移"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体辩论(MAD)框架在通用推理中表现出色，但在高度结构化、知识密集的法律领域尚未被充分探索，需要填补这一空白并理解其适用边界。

Method: 提出L-MAD框架，为多个智能体分配不同的专家角色(personas)，在法律文本蕴涵(Legal Textual Entailment)任务上系统性地对比不同的辩论结构和聚合方法，并分析辩论规模(智能体数量与讨论轮次)对性能的影响。

Result: L-MAD相比强单智能体基线最高提升8%；增加智能体数量可降低不一致性并提升准确率；但延长讨论轮次会导致"过度审议漂移"(over-deliberation drift)，即智能体相互强化彼此的错误。

Conclusion: 在高风险法律推理场景中部署协作式多智能体系统存在实际边界和安全边际，需要在智能体数量与辩论轮次之间取得平衡，避免过度审议带来的负面影响。

Abstract: While multi-agent debate (MAD) frameworks have shown significant potential in general reasoning, their effectiveness in highly structured, knowledge-heavy legal domains remains under-explored. In this work, we introduce the Legal Multi-Agent Debate (L-MAD) framework to systematically evaluate different debate structures and aggregation methods within Legal Textual Entailment. By assigning distinct expert personas to multiple agents, L-MAD improves upon strong single-agent baselines by up to 8\%. Furthermore, analyzing how debate scales reveals a clear trade-off: increasing the agent population reduces inconsistency and improves accuracy, whereas extending discussion rounds induces a detrimental \textit{over-deliberation drift} where agents reinforce each other's mistakes. Ultimately, our findings outline the practical boundaries and safety margins of deploying collaborative multi-agent systems in high-stakes legal reasoning environments.

</details>


### [193] [MedRealMM: A Real-World Multimodal Benchmark for Chinese Online Medical Consultation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09142)
*Runhan Shi,Quan Zhou,Yuqian Xu,Shuai Yang,Xin Wu,Zitong Zhou,Hui Liu,Bin Cha,Zheming Wang,Liya Li,Wei Wei,Haoyuan Hu,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedRealMM 是一个大规模多模态在线医疗问诊基准，基于中国互联网医院的真实医患交互数据构建，包含5,620个多模态病例（覆盖64个科室），通过MCCP框架提取临床挑战时刻并转化为标准化的下一轮回复生成任务，每个实例配有医师细化的评分标准，用于评估LLM在真实临床场景中的多模态推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗LLM基准存在三大缺陷：(1) 依赖合成对话或患者模拟器，脱离真实临床实践；(2) 忽略患者上传的医学影像等多模态信息；(3) 使用多选题或词汇重叠度等指标评估开放式临床回答，无法反映真实临床质量。因此需要构建一个贴近真实诊疗场景的多模态评估基准。

Method: (1) 从中国互联网医院收集去标识化的真实医患对话与患者上传影像，构建多模态数据集；(2) 提出MCCP（Multimodal Clinical Challenge Point）提取框架，从真实问诊轨迹中定位临床关键决策时刻，转换为保留前序图文上下文的标准化下一回复生成任务；(3) 每个实例配备由医师细化的病例特异性评分标准（rubric），奖励临床可取行为、惩罚不安全/无依据/矛盾的回答；(4) 在19个通用及医学专用大模型（含纯文本和多模态系统）上进行评估。

Result: (1) 图像信息对可靠的临床性能至关重要；(2) 当前前沿模型的表现仍低于在线医师回复质量；(3) 部分前沿模型在满足正向临床标准的数量上可比肩甚至超过医师，但同时触发更多负向标准，表明安全性敏感的错误规避仍是核心瓶颈。

Conclusion: MedRealMM 提供了一个真实、可复现的多模态医学推理评估基准，揭示了当前大模型在真实在线问诊场景中安全性与可靠性方面的关键瓶颈，数据集将在Hugging Face上公开发布。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in online medical consultation, yet existing benchmarks remain poorly aligned with real clinical practice. Many rely on synthetic conversations or patient simulators, omit patient-uploaded medical images, or evaluate open-ended clinical responses using multiple-choice or lexical-overlap metrics that poorly reflect clinical quality. We introduce \textbf{MedRealMM}, a large-scale benchmark for multimodal online medical consultation built from de-identified patient-doctor interactions collected from a nationwide Chinese internet hospital. MedRealMM uses a Multimodal Clinical Challenge Point (MCCP) extraction framework to identify clinically demanding moments in authentic consultation trajectories and converts each into a standardized next-response generation task while preserving the preceding text-image context. Each instance is paired with a case-specific rubric refined by physicians that rewards clinically desirable behaviors and penalizes unsafe, unsupported, or contradictory responses. The current release contains 5,620 real-world multimodal cases spanning 64 clinical departments. We evaluate 19 general-purpose and medical-specialized LLMs, including text-only and multimodal systems. Our results show that image information is critical for reliable clinical performance and that current frontier models remain below the online physician response. Although some frontier models satisfy as many or more positive clinical criteria than physicians, they trigger more negative criteria, indicating that safety-sensitive error avoidance remains a central bottleneck. MedRealMM offers a realistic and reproducible benchmark for evaluating multimodal medical reasoning in real-world online consultation. The dataset will be publicly available on Hugging Face at https://huggingface.co/datasets/jdh-algo/MedRealMM.

</details>


### [194] [KV-PRM: Efficient Process Reward Modeling via KV-Cache Transfer for Multi-Agent Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09153)
*Peng Kuang,Haibo Jin,Xiaoyu Han,Yanli Wang,Xiaopeng Yuan,Ye Yu,Kaidi Xu,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KV-PRM 提出一种高效的过程奖励模型，通过直接读取 LLM 生成阶段自然产生的 KV Cache 来替代文本重编码，将评分复杂度从 O(L²) 降至 O(L)，在多个数学推理基准上匹配或超越文本 PRM，同时实现高达 5000 倍的 FLOPs 减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本型过程奖励模型（text-PRM）在长上下文多智能体推演中，需要对整个轨迹文本从头重编码，评分成本随序列长度 L 呈平方级增长（O(L²)），构成严重的计算瓶颈，限制了 PRM 在长程场景中的应用。亟需一种能降低评分复杂度的新方法。

Method: 提出 KV-PRM：不再重编码完整轨迹文本，而是直接利用 LLM 生成过程中自然产生的 KV Cache。通过仅处理一个“验证 token”（verify token）对已有 KV Cache 进行读取，将评分复杂度降至 O(L)。论文还形式化证明了 KV Cache 比文本具有严格更大的信息容量，且对下游奖励建模更高效。

Result: 在 MATH、GSM8K、AIME 三个数学推理基准上，KV-PRM 在 Beam Search、MCTS、Weighted Voting 等多种测试时扩展方法下，性能匹配或严格超越 text-PRM。相比文本 PRM，评分 FLOPs 减少高达 5000 倍，延迟降低 37 倍，每序列内存占用减少 34 倍。

Conclusion: KV-PRM 通过跳过文本重编码、直接复用 KV Cache，从根本上解决了长序列下 PRM 评分的计算瓶颈问题，在保持或提升性能的同时实现数量级的效率提升，为 PRM 在长上下文多智能体系统中的大规模应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Process Reward Models (PRMs) have been proven to be highly effective in guiding test-time scaling (TTS) methods, which significantly boost the capabilities of LLM-based multi-agent systems. However, existing PRMs are text-based: they re-encode the entire trajectory text from scratch. In long multi-agent rollouts, the scoring cost, growing quadratically with respect to sequence length L, creates a severe computational bottleneck, severely limiting PRMs' application in long-context scenarios. To resolve this, we introduce KV-PRM, a highly efficient process reward model that eliminates the heavy text re-encoding by directly reading the KV cache produced naturally during the LLM's generation phase. By processing a single "verify token" against the pre-existing KV cache, KV-PRM reduces the scoring cost from O(L^2) to O(L). We formally prove that the KV cache contains strictly greater information capacity than text, and is more efficient for downstream reward modeling. Empirically, across the MATH, GSM8K, and AIME benchmarks, KV-PRM matches or strictly outperforms text-PRMs under various TTS methods such as Beam Search, MCTS, and Weighted Voting, with up to a 5,000x reduction in scoring FLOPs, a 37x reduction in latency, and a 34x reduction in per-sequence memory footprint compared to text-based PRMs.

</details>


### [195] [Scoped Verification for Reliable Long-Horizon Agentic Context Evolution under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09175)
*Dan C. Hsu,Luke Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRACE 提出用类型化语义图管理 LLM Agent 的持久系统指令，通过局部类型邻域验证实现可靠的长周期上下文演化，在电信 Agent 基准上将严格可靠性从 0.091 提升至 0.673。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署的 LLM Agent 依赖 agentic context（模型外部的文本控制内容），其中持久系统指令会随运营经验不断更新。在长期演化中，扁平文本维护导致累积指令日益增长且相互交织，使得验证变得越来越困难。

Method: 提出 GRACE（Graph-Regularized Agentic Context Evolution），将持久指令维护为类型化语义图，在修改节点的局部类型邻域内验证更新；通过图更新后重建为增量文本编辑，保持部署时文本指令检查点的可用性。

Result: 在基于 τ²-bench 的固定电信 Agent 框架及受控分布漂移协议下，经五次独立重复实验，GRACE 将严格可靠性（pass³）从 Gemini 2.5 Flash 零样本的 0.091 提升至最终检查点的 0.673±0.136，超过 Gemini 3.1 Pro 零样本参考值 0.242，而扁平文本 HCE 基线仅为 0.191±0.051。

Conclusion: 可靠的长周期上下文演化需要两个关键条件：一是使验证局部化的结构化基板（类型化语义图），二是使累积指令内容保持可用的整合机制。

Abstract: Deployed LLM agents rely on agentic context, the model-external textual control content assembled by an operational harness. In this work, the mutable component of that context is a persistent system-level instruction that is updated from operational experience while the model, tools, and harness remain fixed. Over long evolution horizons, flat-text maintenance makes verification increasingly difficult as accumulated instructions grow and interact. We propose Graph-Regularized Agentic Context Evolution (GRACE), which maintains the persistent instruction component as a typed semantic graph and validates proposed updates within the local typed neighborhoods of modified nodes. Accepted graph updates are reconstructed as incremental edits to the textual instruction checkpoint used at deployment. We evaluate GRACE within a fixed telecom agent harness derived from $τ^2$-bench under a controlled distribution-shift protocol. Across five independent replications, GRACE improves strict reliability, measured by pass^3, from the Gemini 2.5 Flash zero-shot value of 0.091 to 0.673$\pm$0.136 at the final checkpoint. This exceeds a Gemini 3.1 Pro zero-shot reference of 0.242 on the same held-out set, while the flat-text HCE baseline finishes at 0.191$\pm$0.051. These results identify two requirements for reliable long-horizon context evolution, a structural substrate that makes verification local and a consolidation mechanism that keeps accumulated instruction content usable.

</details>


### [196] [Toward Auditable AI Scientists: A Hypothesis Evolution Protocol for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09195)
*Izumi Takahara,Teruyasu Mizoguchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出假设演化协议（HEP），将LLM智能体的假设生成、评估和演化过程显式化、可审计化，在材料科学研究任务中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM科学发现智能体的假设、测试和信念更新过程隐藏在非结构化日志中，缺乏审计机制，导致其科学推理过程无法被检查、验证和复现。

Method: 提出Hypothesis Evolution Protocol (HEP)——一种智能体框架，将假设生成、评估和演化操作显式化为可审计的结构化操作。HEP驱动智能体执行"假设-测试-证据-信念"循环，使推理过程透明可追溯。

Result: 在材料科学研究任务上，配备HEP的智能体能够执行规划型智能体所缺乏的完整假设-测试-证据-信念循环，可跨研究问题泛化，且随着基座LLM能力提升，对协议利用更加充分。

Conclusion: HEP标志着向可审计AI科学家迈出重要一步，其科学推理过程可被检查、验证和构建，为AI驱动的科学发现提供了可信基础。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly expected to play a central role in AI-driven scientific discovery. Equipped with broad knowledge, flexible reasoning, and tool use, they have the potential to autonomously explore and solve scientific problems by repeatedly proposing hypotheses, testing them, and revising their beliefs in the light of the evidence. In current agents, however, these hypotheses, tests, and belief updates are buried in unstructured logs, and no mechanism lets the agent or the human researcher audit that process. Here we propose the Hypothesis Evolution Protocol (HEP), an agent harness that provides hypothesis generation, evaluation, and evolution as explicit, auditable operations. On materials-science research tasks, a HEP-equipped agent operates the hypothesis--test--evidence--belief cycle that planning-style agents lack, generalizes across research questions, and exploits the protocol more fully as the base LLM becomes more capable. These results mark a step toward auditable AI scientists, whose scientific reasoning can be inspected, verified, and built upon.

</details>


### [197] [OpenProver: Agentic and Interactive Theorem Proving with Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09217)
*Matěj Kripner,Milan Straka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenProver是一个开源的大模型驱动自动定理证明系统，采用Planner-Worker-Verifier架构，集成Lean 4形式化验证，支持自动验证和交互式证明搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的自动定理证明系统多闭源，缺乏可复现评估；同时，交互式代码生成中的人机协同优势尚未充分引入定理证明领域。

Method: OpenProver采用Planner-Worker-Verifier三层架构：Planner维护Whiteboard（紧凑草稿）和Repository（无界中间发现存储），将数学任务分解给并行Worker执行，最终通过Lean 4进行形式化验证。系统同时提供交互式终端界面供人工引导证明搜索。

Result: 在ProofNet基准上对OpenProver进行了评估，并与简单基线进行了对比，展示了系统支持定量消融实验的能力。

Conclusion: OpenProver作为全开源系统，为LLM驱动的自动定理证明提供了可复现的评估平台，其人机协同交互模式有望提升证明搜索效率。

Abstract: In this system paper, we present OpenProver, an open-source system for LLM-driven automated theorem proving (ATP) with integrated Lean 4 formal verification. OpenProver integrates a Planner-Worker-Verifier architecture inspired by recent ATP agentic systems such as Aletheia. A Planner agent maintains a compact Whiteboard scratchpad and an unbounded Repository of intermediate findings, and decomposes mathematical work into parallel Workers.
  OpenProver is fully open-source, offers reproducible evaluation through automatic formal verification of generated proofs, and provides an interactive terminal interface for human-guided proof search. In interactive mode, OpenProver allows the human operator to monitor and steer the proof search process, motivated by the established human-AI synergy in interactive code generation.
  To showcase the potential for quantitative ablation experiments enabled by automatic formal verification, we evaluate OpenProver on ProofNet and compare it with a simple baseline. OpenProver is publicly available at https://github.com/kripner/OpenProver.

</details>


### [198] [LongMedBench: Benchmarking Medical Agents for Long-Horizon Clinical Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09322)
*Yanzhen Chen,Zihan Xu,Xiaocheng Zhang,Zhiting Fan,Weiqi Zhai,Hongxia Xu,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LongMedBench，一个基于真实EHR的长程临床决策基准，包含335名患者、平均19.72次住院记录，通过事实问答、时序推理和长程决策三个评估维度，揭示当前LLM在隐式时间推断和长程决策方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM医疗Agent的评估多聚焦于短上下文的知识问答和工具使用，但真实医疗本质上是纵向的，临床医生需要跨多次就诊、检查和治疗来聚合证据。因此，需要构建支持长程、多会话交互的评估基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 通过可复现的流程将MIMIC-IV入院记录和临床笔记整合为时间序列事件流和长上下文记忆数据集，构建包含335名患者（平均19.72次住院、每次住院44.91个医疗事件）的LongMedBench基准；设计三套评估体系：基于事实的问答、时序推理和长程决策，系统衡量Agent对历史患者信息的理解和利用能力。

Result: 近期LLM能有效利用显式时间戳，但在隐式时间推断上存在困难；RAG和Agent记忆系统可提升信息检索任务表现，但决策任务性能高度依赖模型的即时上下文。

Conclusion: LongMedBench揭示了当前LLM在长程临床决策场景中的关键瓶颈，特别是隐式时序推理和上下文长度对决策质量的制约，为后续医学AI Agent研究提供了更贴近真实临床实践的评估框架。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce LongMedBench, a real-world EHR-based benchmark for long-horizon clinical decision-making. Prior evaluations of LLM-based medical agents have largely emphasized short-context knowledge QA and tool use. However, real-world medical care is inherently longitudinal, and clinicians must aggregate evidence across repeated visits, tests, and evolving treatments. Therefore, long-horizon interaction is essential for realistic assessment. LongMedBench is constructed via a reproducible pipeline that integrates MIMIC-IV admission records and clinical notes into time-series event streams and long-context memory datasets, enabling long-horizon, multi-session interactions between agents and a clinical environment. It comprises 335 patients, with 19.72 inpatient visits per patient on average and 44.91 medical events per visit. Guided by the long-horizon decision process, we propose an evaluation taxonomy with three suites: fact-based QA, temporal reasoning, and long-horizon decision-making. This taxonomy measures how agents understand and leverage historical patient information over extended horizons. Our experiments show that while recent LLMs can make good use of explicit timestamps, they have challenges in implicit time inference; The RAG and agent memory system can improve the performance of information retrieval tasks, but the performance of decision-making tasks is highly dependent on the model's immediate context.

</details>


### [199] [Communication-Efficient Digital-Twin Coordination for Heterogeneous LLM Embodied Agents over Computing Power Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09330)
*Nuocheng Yang,Sihua Wang,Zihan Chen,Tony Q. S. Quek,Changchuan Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LDT-Coord 是一种基于轻量级数字孪生的异构LLM智能体团队协调框架，通过将行动决策与自然语言推理解耦，结合规则化编排器和PPO-Lagrangian通信控制，在保持任务成功率的同时将通信开销降低70倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多轮自然语言对话的异构LLM智能体协调框架面临三个耦合挑战：(1) 智能体间对话的通信开销随团队规模快速增长；(2) 协调质量受限于异构LLM能力差异；(3) 迭代协商导致行动延迟。因此需要一个更高效的协调机制。

Method: 提出LDT-Coord框架：(1) 每个智能体独立选择意图行动，并将行动决策和结构化时序约束上报至数字孪生服务器，将协调性能与自然语言推理能力解耦；(2) DT执行免训练的规则化编排器算法解决跨智能体冲突并下发协调指令；(3) 将智能体上报控制建模为带约束的部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(C-POMDP)，采用PPO-Lagrangian算法求解以进一步降低通信开销。

Result: 仿真结果表明，LDT-Coord 在任务成功率上与传统协调方法相当，同时将通信开销降低超过70倍，并在LLM异构性下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LDT-Coord 通过数字孪生驱动的结构化协调机制，有效解决了异构LLM智能体团队在资源受限网络下的高效协作问题，实现了通信效率与协调性能的良好平衡。

Abstract: Embodied agent teams powered by heterogeneous large language models (LLMs) are being widely deployed in physical artificial intelligence such as smart factories, warehouses, and service robotics. To enable collaboration among such an agent team, efficient coordination mechanisms that operate reliably under limited network resources are required. However, existing heterogeneous LLM-agent coordination frameworks that rely on multi-round natural-language-based conversations introduce three coupled challenges. First, inter-agent dialogue incurs communication overhead that grows rapidly with team size. Second, the quality of coordination is constrained by the heterogeneous capabilities of the agent team's LLMs. Third, agents may suffer from action delays due to iterative negotiation. To address these challenges, we propose LDT-Coord, a networked coordination framework built upon a lightweight digital twin (DT). Specifically, each agent independently selects its intended action and reports both the action decision and a structured temporal constraint over shared resources to the DT server, thereby decoupling coordination performance from natural-language reasoning ability. Then, DT executes a training-free, rule-based orchestrator algorithm to resolve cross-agent conflicts and returns coordination instructions to prevent such conflicts. To further reduce communication overhead, we formulate agent reporting control as a constrained partially observable Markov decision process (C-POMDP) and solve it with the PPO-Lagrangian algorithm. Simulation results show that LDT-Coord achieves a task success rate comparable to conventional coordination methods while reducing communication overhead by more than 70x and maintaining robustness under LLM heterogeneity.

</details>


### [200] [Fictional Worldbuilding: Multi-Agent LLM Collaboration with Hierarchical Context Compression and Iterative Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09403)
*Jingbo Chen,He Wang,Wei Yuan,Yuqiao Lai,Zhenyan Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoWorldBuilder 是一个多智能体协作系统，通过结构化概念网络、DAG混合批调度、四层上下文压缩、迭代审查机制和技能驱动架构，解决了LLM在世界构建中面临的上下文爆炸、创意多样性vs内容一致性、及自动化质量保证三大挑战，在20项任务中实现95%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM应用于世界构建（游戏设计、文学创作）面临三个核心挑战：(1) 上下文随构建过程线性膨胀导致上下文爆炸；(2) 创意多样性与内容一致性之间存在张力；(3) 缺乏自动化质量保证机制。现有方法未能系统性解决这些问题。

Method: 提出AutoWorldBuilder多智能体协作系统，包含五大组件：(1) 带冲突检测的结构化概念网络；(2) 基于DAG的混合批调度器，按语义局部性分组任务；(3) 四层上下文压缩机制（约90% token缩减）；(4) 配备专门Auditor智能体的迭代审查系统，将提案通过率从42%提升至85%以上；(5) 支持零代码扩展与差异化温度配置的技能驱动架构。实验使用GPT-OSS 120B和DeepSeek v3.2后端，覆盖20个多样化世界构建任务。

Result: 系统实现95.0%成功率，每个世界生成56-103个自洽概念，耗时18-31分钟，实现零冲突交付。提案通过率从42%提升至85%以上，上下文压缩达约90% token缩减。

Conclusion: 验证的架构模式（层级预算压缩、语义局部性调度、生成与审查分离）可迁移至更广泛的知识密集型多智能体LLM应用。该系统为自动化世界构建提供了可工程化的解决方案。

Abstract: Worldbuilding, the construction of coherent fictional worlds, is a foundational task in game design and literary creation. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer new possibilities for automated content generation, but their application to worldbuilding faces three challenges: context explosion that grows linearly with the building process, the tension between creative diversity and content consistency, and the absence of automated quality assurance. This paper presents AutoWorldBuilder, a multi-agent collaborative system that addresses these challenges through five integrated components: a structured concept network with conflict detection; a DAG-based hybrid batch scheduler that groups tasks by semantic locality; a four-layer context compression mechanism achieving approximately 90% token reduction; an iterative review system with specialized Auditor agents that improves proposal pass rates from 42% to over 85%; and a skill-driven agent architecture supporting zero-code extension with differentiated temperature configuration. Two experiments across 20 diverse worldbuilding tasks, using GPT-OSS 120B and DeepSeek v3.2 as LLM backends, demonstrate a 95.0% success rate. The system generated 56-103 self-consistent concepts per world in 18-31 minutes with zero-conflict delivery. The architectural patterns validated here, including layer-as-budget compression, semantic-locality scheduling, and separation of generation and review, transfer to the broader class of knowledge-intensive, multi-agent LLM applications.

</details>


### [201] [How Does Bayesian Causal Discovery Fail? Characterising Structural Consequences in Linear Gaussian Networks under Latent Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09449)
*Debargha Ghosh,Silja Renooij,Anna Kononova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了线性高斯因果模型中存在潜在混杂时贝叶斯因果发现的后验行为，推导出虚假边出现的临界相关阈值，并发现该阈值随样本量增大而降低，进而刻画了两种后验失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯因果发现研究虽广泛用于量化DAG的认知不确定性，但对潜在混杂下后验行为如何响应缺乏系统刻画——通常仅指出混杂破坏可识别性，而未深入分析后验分布的具体表现。

Method: 在线性高斯因果模型框架下，考虑恰好两个观测变量之间存在加性潜在混杂；通过分析得分函数（score function）与后验分布，推导虚假边被偏好的临界相关阈值，并研究该阈值与样本量的关系，进而根据混杂变量周围的局部结构划分后验失效模式。

Result: 推导出临界相关阈值：当观测变量间相关性超过该阈值时，得分函数倾向于包含虚假边的图结构；该阈值随样本量增大而降低（即数据越多越容易出问题）；在阈值之上，识别出两种由局部结构决定的后验失效机制，并通过精确后验计算在多图上验证。

Conclusion: 潜在混杂会导致贝叶斯因果发现的后验分布出现系统性偏差：更多数据非但不能纠正虚假边，反而降低触发偏差的临界相关性阈值；失效模式取决于混杂变量周围的局部图结构，为理解混杂下的贝叶斯因果推断提供了理论刻画。

Abstract: Bayesian causal discovery is widely used for its ability to quantify epistemic uncertainty over directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) through posterior inference. However, its behaviour under latent confounding remains poorly understood, as existing work typically notes that confounding breaks identifiability without characterising how the posterior distribution over DAGs responds. In this work, we analyse posterior behaviour under latent confounding in linear Gaussian causal models, focusing on additive latent confounding between exactly two observed variables. We derive a critical correlation threshold above which the score function favours graphs with a spurious edge between the confounded variables, and show that this threshold decreases with sample size -- more data lowers the correlation required for the spurious edge to be favoured. Beyond this threshold, we characterize two distinct posterior failure regimes determined by the local structure around the confounded variables. Our findings are supported by exact posterior computations on multiple graph structures, demonstrating both the predicted failure regimes.

</details>


### [202] [ProofCouncil: An LLM Agent for Solving Open Mathematical Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09474)
*Johannes Schmitt,Tim Gehrunger,Jasper Dekoninck,Gergely Bérczi,Uri Kreitner,Liam Price,David Holmes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProofCouncil 是一个采用作者-评论家架构的数学智能体，专为自主解决开放数学问题设计。在 FirstProof 挑战赛中，它在10个问题中的6个上获得最优表现；在30个开放问题的评估中，5个被判定完全正确，2个有望通过最终验证，8个取得了有意义的局部进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在数学开放问题上展现出潜力，但通过模拟真实数学实践的智能体工作流，仍有进一步提升性能的空间。论文旨在开发一种更贴近数学家实际工作方式的智能体系统。

Method: 提出 ProofCouncil，一种基于作者-评论家（author-critic）架构的数学智能体。该架构模拟数学研究中"提出证明"与"审阅批评"的协作过程。同时开发了配套的智能体构建库并开源发布。

Result: 在 FirstProof 第二批挑战赛的10个问题中，ProofCouncil 在6个问题上提交的解答被评审判定为正确或仅需微调，表现居参赛队伍之首。在额外30个开放问题评估中，21个获得人工反馈：5个完全正确，2个前景良好待最终验证，8个包含有用的局部进展。

Conclusion: ProofCouncil 通过作者-评论家架构有效提升了LLM解决开放数学问题的能力，在多项基准上取得了领先性能，验证了将真实数学实践融入智能体工作流的可行性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown increasing promise in solving open problems in mathematics. However, their performance can be further improved through agentic workflows tailored to real-world mathematical practice. To this end, we introduce ProofCouncil, a mathematical agent that is designed to tackle open problems using an author-critic architecture. ProofCouncil served as a submission to the second batch of FirstProof, a challenge consisting of 10 real-world mathematical problems that agents must solve autonomously. Its submissions for 6 of the 10 problems were judged by the referees to be correct up to at most minor revisions, showing the best performance among participating teams. We also evaluate ProofCouncil on 30 open problems collected from mathematical researchers. Among the 21 solutions that received human feedback, 5 were judged completely correct, 2 more were judged promising pending final verification, and a further 8 contained useful partial progress. In this short paper, we describe the development of ProofCouncil and the agent-building library used to create it, which we release as open source to the community.

</details>


### [203] [Ceci n'est pas une pipe: AI systems as semantic abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09489)
*Jade Alglave,Patrick Cousot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种语义框架，用于分析AI系统输出的正确性，通过区分"领域知识所证实的"、"参考来源所说的"和"系统当前可使用的"来精确定义AI的常见失败模式（如外推、无依据断言、来源与知识不匹配等），为规范和检查AI系统的输出可信度提供了一套术语体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统的输出并非所描述的事实或世界状态，而是一种工程化的表征。现有评估方式缺乏对输出"正确性"的精确语义分析工具，需要一套框架来区分不同层次的依据（领域知识、参考来源、系统可用信息），从而系统化地诊断和描述AI输出的各种失败模式。

Method: 提出一个语义框架，将AI输出所依赖的依据划分为三个层次：①公认领域知识所证实的；②参考来源所说的；③系统当前可实际使用的。基于这三层区分，对常见的AI输出失败模式给出精确定义，包括：外推、被驳斥的断言、无依据断言、来源与知识不匹配、过时或被驳斥的来源、附加假设、无依据的使用等。

Result: 该框架成功为多种AI输出失败模式提供了精确的语义定义，使得这些失败可以被系统化地识别和描述，而非仅仅依赖"流畅但不准确"这类模糊判断。

Conclusion: 该语义框架为规范和检查AI系统提供了一套有用的词汇体系，尤其适用于那些输出、引用、工具调用和影响世界的行动必须由可靠主张和明确权威来证成（而非仅凭表面流畅性）的场景。

Abstract: An AI system's output is not the fact or world state it appears to describe, but rather an engineered representation. We propose a semantic framework to describe AI systems, to be able to examine the correctness of such representations. To do so, we distinguish what is justified by accepted domain knowledge, what reference sources say, and what the system can currently use. This allows us to give precise definitions to common failures: extrapolation, refuted or unsupported assertion, sources versus knowledge mismatch, stale or refuted source, added hypotheses, unsupported use... We hope our framework gives a useful vocabulary for specifying and checking AI systems whose outputs, citations, tool calls, and world-changing actions must be justified by reliable claims and explicit authority rather than apparent fluency.

</details>


### [204] [Multimodal Reward Hacking in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09492)
*Jiayu Yao,Yiwei Wang,Anmeng Zhang,Zhe Sun,Songsong Wang,Lingrui Mei,Yuyao Ge,Shenghua Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究了多模态大语言模型在强化学习对齐中的奖励黑客现象，提出新指标NRFR，系统分析了奖励设计、模型规模、算法等因素的影响，指出稳健对齐需依赖优化压力下依然可靠的奖励与验证器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习用于对齐多模态大语言模型时，更高的奖励并不总意味着更好的任务表现，尤其在视觉证据由纯文本或弱 grounding 奖励评估时风险加剧。现有研究缺乏对MLLM RL中奖励黑客现象的系统性分析。

Method: 在安全VQA、图表VQA和压力测试三个场景中，系统变化奖励设计（仅结果奖励、答案感知奖励、视觉证据奖励）、数据模糊度、模型规模（2B-32B）和RL算法（GRPO、RLOO、DAPO）。提出新指标NRFR（Newly Rewarded Failure Rate），衡量代理奖励提升但任务失败的样本比例，并与RHR（Reward Hacking Rate）对比以区分继承失败与新引入失败。

Result: 仅结果奖励导致严重黑客行为，RHR达48.1%，且NRFR超过RHR说明RL创造了新失败而非仅继承旧失败。缩放模型可减轻但无法消除黑客行为：32B模型在仅结果奖励下仍保持54.9%的恶化率。答案感知奖励在所有规模上改善oracle趋势。算法鲁棒性方面：GRPO最抗黑客，RLOO仍脆弱，DAPO从2B到8B显著提升。视觉证据奖励仅在验证可靠时有效：关键词检查增加黑客行为，VLM-as-judge语义验证可减少黑客行为。

Conclusion: 多模态奖励黑客是优化不完美奖励的系统性结果，稳健对齐需要奖励函数和验证器在优化压力下保持可靠性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly used to align multimodal large language models (MLLMs), but higher rewards do not always imply better task performance. This risk is amplified when visual evidence is evaluated by text-only or weakly grounded rewards. We study reward hacking in MLLM RL across safety VQA, chart VQA, and stress-test settings, varying reward design, data ambiguity, model scale (2B-32B), and RL algorithm (GRPO, RLOO, DAPO). We introduce Newly Rewarded Failure Rate (NRFR), which measures failures among samples whose proxy reward improves over the SFT baseline. Outcome-only rewards cause severe hacking, reaching 48.1% Reward Hacking Rate (RHR), while NRFR exceeding RHR shows that RL creates new failures rather than merely inheriting them. Scaling reduces but does not eliminate hacking: even the 32B model retains a 54.9% worse rate under outcome-only rewards, whereas answer-aware rewards improve the oracle trend at every scale. Robustness is also algorithm- and scale-dependent: GRPO is consistently most resistant, RLOO remains vulnerable, and DAPO improves substantially from 2B to 8B. Visual-evidence rewards help only with reliable verification: keyword-based checks increase hacking, while VLM-as-judge semantic verification reduces it. Overall, multimodal reward hacking is a systematic result of optimizing imperfect rewards, and robust alignment requires rewards and verifiers that remain reliable under optimization pressure.

</details>


### [205] [Shared Selective Persistent Memory for Agentic LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09493)
*Sanjana Pedada,Aditya Dhavala,Neelraj Patil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LLM智能体多轮工具调用中的上下文问题，提出共享选择性持久记忆架构，分类保留可复用上下文（任务规范、数据模式、工具配置、输出约束），丢弃会话推理痕迹。在协作工作区平台中实现，结合零令牌数据刷新机制，在三个企业场景中任务完成率达96%，显著优于无记忆（79%）和全历史（71%）方案，令牌成本降低97倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的智能体系统在多轮工具调用生成代码时，每次会话从零开始，丢弃了此前会话中积累的配置选择、领域约束、数据模式和工具使用模式等有价值上下文。简单持久化完整对话历史既浪费令牌，又会因无关上下文降低生成质量。因此需要一种智能的记忆机制来选择性保留可复用信息。

Method: 提出共享选择性持久记忆架构：（1）将可复用上下文分为四类——任务规范、数据模式、工具配置和输出约束，同时丢弃会话特定的推理痕迹；（2）记忆可共享，工作区封装选择性记忆并通过基于角色的访问控制跨用户传递，支持协作复用；（3）在部署的协作工作区平台中实现，LLM智能体从异构数据源（CSV、SQL、REST API、MCP服务器）生成、编辑和维护git版本化制品；（4）引入零令牌数据刷新机制，将生成程序与运行时数据解耦，无需重新调用LLM即可复用制品。

Result: 在三个企业场景中，共享选择性持久记忆实现96%任务完成率，对比无记忆79%、全历史记忆71%。零令牌刷新机制消除重复更新的LLM重调用，任务时间减少14倍；摘要驱动生成相比原始数据注入降低每次调用令牌成本97倍。在四个公共数据集上复现验证泛化性，零令牌刷新12/12全部成功。全历史持久化反而因陈旧痕迹偏差主动降低完成质量，选择性记忆优于两种极端方案。

Conclusion: 共享选择性持久记忆通过分类保留关键上下文、丢弃无关推理痕迹，有效解决了LLM智能体多会话间的知识复用问题。结合零令牌数据刷新和摘要驱动生成，在保证高任务完成率的同时大幅降低令牌消耗和任务时间，为企业级协作场景提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic LLM systems that generate code through multi-turn tool use face a fundamental context problem: each session starts from zero, discarding the configuration choices, domain constraints, data schemas, and tool-use patterns that made previous sessions productive. Naively persisting entire conversation histories is token-inefficient and counterproductive: irrelevant context degrades generation quality. We introduce shared selective persistent memory, an architecture that identifies and retains four categories of reusable context (task specifications, data schemas, tool configurations, and output constraints) while discarding session-specific reasoning traces. Crucially, this memory is shared: workspaces encapsulating selective memory can be transferred across users with role-based access control, enabling collaborative reuse without redundant specification. We implement it in a deployed collaborative workspace platform where LLM agents produce, edit, and maintain git-versioned artifacts (dashboards, reports, and data-driven documents) from heterogeneous sources (CSV, SQL, REST APIs, and MCP servers). A complementary zero-token data refresh mechanism decouples generated programs from runtime data, enabling artifact reuse without re-invocation. Across three enterprise scenarios, shared selective persistent memory achieves 96% task completion (vs. 79% without memory and 71% with full history). Zero-token refresh eliminates LLM re-invocation for recurring updates (14x task-time reduction), while summary-driven generation cuts per-invocation token cost by 97x versus raw data injection. A replication on four public datasets confirms generalizability, with zero-token refresh succeeding in 12/12 trials. Notably, naive full-history persistence actively degrades completion by biasing the agent with stale traces, while selective memory outperforms both extremes.

</details>


### [206] [SAGEAgent: A Self-Evolving Agent for Cost-Aware Modality Acquisition in Multimodal Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09521)
*Chongyu Qu,Can Cui,Zhengyi Lu,Junchao Zhu,Tianyuan Yao,Junlin Guo,Juming Xiong,Yanfan Zhu,Yuechen Yang,Bennett A. Landman,Yuankai Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAGEAgent是一个自进化的LLM临床智能体，将癌症诊断建模为序贯决策问题，在保证生存预测准确性的同时，平均减少55%的诊断采集负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态生存预测方法假设所有模态均可用或被动处理缺失数据，但临床上诊断模态遵循固定的负担递增顺序。核心问题是：是否每位患者都需要完整的诊断检查才能实现准确预测？需要一种能主动权衡预测准确性与临床侵入性的序贯决策方法。

Method: 提出SAGEAgent，将诊断采集建模为序贯决策问题。核心组件包括：(1) 将数值预测转化为文本的临床工具；(2) 检索相似历史案例的情景记忆；(3) 从经验中积累可复用决策模式的语义记忆。智能体根据患者不断演化的诊断状态，逐步决定是否获取下一模态。

Result: 在胶质瘤队列（TCGA-LGG、TCGA-GBM和BraTS，共四种诊断模态）上，SAGEAgent在保持竞争性生存预测准确性的同时，将平均采集负担降低了55%。

Conclusion: 并非每位癌症患者都需要完整诊断检查才能实现准确生存预测。通过主动的序贯采集决策，可以在大幅降低临床负担的同时维持预测性能，为精准肿瘤学中的资源优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Does every cancer patient truly need a complete diagnostic workup for accurate survival prediction? In multimodal clinical oncology, diagnostic modalities follow a clinically mandated order of escalating burden -- from demographics collected at intake to genomic profiling requiring specialized tissue analysis. Current multimodal survival methods either assume all modalities are available or passively handle missing data, but none actively reason about whether acquiring the next modality is justified for a given patient along this ordered workflow. We formulate this as a sequential decision problem and propose SAGEAgent (Sequential Acquisition Guided by Experience), a self-evolving LLM-based clinical agent that decides which diagnostic modalities to acquire for each patient, balancing predictive accuracy against clinical invasiveness. SAGEAgent reasons about each patient's evolving diagnostic state through clinical tools that translate numerical predictions into text, an episodic memory that retrieves similar past cases, and a semantic memory that accumulates reusable decision patterns from experience. Experiments on a glioma cohort combining TCGA-LGG, TCGA-GBM, and BraTS with four diagnostic modalities demonstrate that SAGEAgent achieves competitive survival prediction accuracy while reducing average acquisition burden by 55%.

</details>


### [207] [Beyond Fixed Representations: The Vocabulary and Verifier Gaps in Open-Ended AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09560)
*Yuan Cao,Haiqian Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出当前AI系统受限于固定的表征框架（概念词汇、可搜索的解空间、成功标准），主张构建真正开放式创新智能需要三类新操作：创建、稳定化和复用新的表征基元。作者通过"词汇缺口"和"验证者缺口"两个维度刻画当前AI与开放式智能的差距，并以"认知差异缩减"统一框架区分帧内变换与生成式变换，最终提出创新自主性阶梯及若干研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI在推理、编程、定理证明等任务上表现强大，但存在共同的结构性局限：模型运作所依赖的表征框架（概念词汇、可搜索的解空间、评价标准）是预先固定给定的。如果目标是构建能够进行开放式创新的强智能系统，就必须超越"在固定空间内搜索"的范式，转而探索能够改造搜索空间本身的操作机制。

Method: 作者提出两个核心缺口来刻画差距：（1）词汇缺口——发明并稳定新表征基元（而非仅重组已有基元）的困难；（2）验证者缺口——判断新基元价值的困难，因其全部收益可能仅在未来的复用中才能显现。在此基础上，作者以"智能即认知差异缩减"作为统一框架，将智能行为视为一系列认知变换，区分帧内变换（在固定表征框架内操作）和生成式变换（可能修改框架本身），并提出"创新自主性阶梯"模型，勾勒了奖励有用表征变化、持久记忆架构、自适应验证机制等研究方向。

Result: 论文通过概念分析得出：当前AI系统本质上执行的是帧内变换，缺乏稳定化和复用新表征基元的生成式变换能力。词汇缺口和验证者缺口的识别为理解开放式智能的瓶颈提供了清晰的诊断框架，而认知差异缩减的统一视角则为设计下一代AI系统提供了理论指引。

Conclusion: 构建开放式创新AI需要超越固定表征框架内的搜索，转向能够创建、稳定化和复用新表征基元的系统。关键在于弥合词汇缺口和验证者缺口，具体路径包括设计奖励有用表征变化的目标函数、构建支持发明基元持久化的记忆架构，以及开发能与表征共同演化的自适应验证机制。

Abstract: Modern AI systems are increasingly being evaluated for their ability to reason, code, prove theorems, use tools, and long-horizon research tasks. These are powerful capabilities, but they share a structural limitation: the representational frame within which the model operates, including its conceptual vocabulary, the space of admissible solutions it can search, and the criteria by which success is evaluated, is typically fixed and supplied in advance. This paper argues that building stronger intelligent systems capable of open-ended innovation requires additional classes of operations: the creation, stabilization, and reuse of new representational primitives, which alter the space being searched rather than simply searching within it.
  We characterize the distance between current AI systems and genuinely open-ended intelligence through two gaps. The first is the vocabulary gap, the difficulty of inventing and stabilizing new representational primitives rather than merely recombining existing ones. The second is the verifier gap, the difficulty of judging the value of a new primitive when its full payoff may be visible only after future reuse. We interpret both gaps through a unified framework of intelligence as cognitive discrepancy reduction. By viewing intelligent behaviors as a sequence of cognitive transformations, we distinguish intra-space transformations which operate within a fixed representational frame, from generative transformations which may modify the frame itself. On this basis, we propose a ladder of innovation autonomy and outline several directions for advancing open-ended AI, including objectives that reward useful representational change, persistent memory architectures for invented primitives, and adaptive verification mechanisms capable of evolving alongside the representations they evaluate.

</details>


### [208] [Knowledge Graphs and Explainable AI as Complementary Resources for Urban Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09578)
*Jan Gronewald,Andreas Emrich,Nijat Mehdiyev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从信息系统资源基础理论出发，提出知识图谱(KG)与可解释AI(XAI)集成的四种互补模式（提升、约束、类型化、修订），每种模式解锁可辩护性决策的不同属性，为城市采矿中的拆除前评估提供结构化的审计人工制品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拆除前评估作为城市采矿的核心受监管审计流程，要求AI支持的决策具备可辩护性——即可读性、合理性、可溯源性和可争议性，而非仅追求预测精度。现有文献虽已编目KG与XAI的集成方式，但缺乏结构性解释来说明为何特定集成能产生两者单独无法提供的产物。

Method: 基于IS资源基础理论，提出互补性理论解释框架；定义四种整合模式（Lifting/提升、Constraining/约束、Typing/类型化、Revising/修订），每种模式为对XAI人工制品与知识图谱基底结构的类型化操作；通过防火门案例，使用W3C关联建筑数据栈及估值扩展进行实例说明。

Result: 四种模式各自解锁一种可辩护性属性：提升模式增强可读性，约束模式增强合理性，类型化模式增强可溯源性，修订模式增强可争议性。这些模式共同产出拆除前评估所需的监管级人工制品。

Conclusion: KG-XAI的互补性整合并非简单叠加，而是通过类型化操作产生涌现性产物，为受监管的审计决策提供结构化支撑。该框架为城市采矿等需要人类审计师保持问责制的AI辅助场景提供了设计指南。

Abstract: Pre-demolition assessment, the regulated audit process at the heart of urban mining, is an information process in which AI support must serve qualified auditors who remain accountable for the decisions taken. The relevant unit of value is not prediction accuracy alone, but the defensibility of the supported decisions: their legibility, plausibility, sourcing, and contestability. Explainable AI techniques and domain knowledge graphs each address parts of this requirement, and existing taxonomies have catalogued their integration. The literature is descriptively rich but structurally under-specified: what remains less developed is a structural account of why specific integrations produce artefacts neither resource can provide alone. This paper offers a complementarity-theoretic interpretation grounded in the IS resource-based tradition. We propose four consolidated KG-XAI integration modes (Lifting, Constraining, Typing, and Revising), each defined as a typed operation over XAI artefacts and knowledge-graph substrate structures. Each mode unlocks a distinct property of defensibility and contributes to the kind of regulatory artefact pre-demolition assessment demands. A fire-door example from the urban-mining process illustrates the modes using the W3C Linked Building Data stack and valuation extensions.

</details>


### [209] [TrustX Agent Risk Classification Framework (ARC): Risk-Tiering Internally Created Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09586)
*Hannah M. Liu,Rhea Saxena,Shiv Asthana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TrustX代理风险分类框架（ARC），一个结构化、可复用的评分工具，针对七类代理AI系统，通过十二维度评分量表结合GPA+IAT分类模型和五级自主性框架，生成三级治理输出及配套控制建议，并包含编码助手扩展以处理特定场景的细微差别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理AI系统在企业与公共部门迅速普及，但通用AI风险框架在分类和治理这类系统方面已显不足，亟需一个专门针对代理AI的结构化风险分类与治理工具。

Method: 构建TrustX代理风险分类框架（ARC），核心为十二维度评分量表，结合GPA+IAT分类模型和基于现有文献的五级自主性框架，生成三级治理输出及映射控制建议；另设编码助手扩展以适配特定代理AI类型，并通过示例演示框架实践应用。

Result: 框架能够对七类代理AI系统进行稳健的风险量化，并输出三级治理等级及相应控制建议；编码助手扩展可捕捉该类系统的特有细微差别。框架面向AI治理从业者、风险官、开发者和监管者，可通过 https://arc.responsible.ai/ 交互访问。

Conclusion: ARC框架填补了通用AI风险框架在代理AI治理方面的空白，提供了一种结构化、可迭代的分类与治理方案，并将持续完善以增强稳健性和适用性。

Abstract: The proliferation of agentic AI systems across enterprise and public-sector contexts has outpaced the capacity of general-purpose AI risk frameworks to classify and govern them. In this paper, we introduce the TrustX Agent Risk Classification Framework, a structured, repeatable instrument that can be applied to seven types of agentic AI systems and is grounded in foundational pre-existing AI governance frameworks. At the core of the framework is a twelve-dimension scoring rubric that robustly quantifies the risk. This rubric is combined with other components, such as the GPA + IAT classification model and the five-level autonomy framework derived from existing literature. These inputs produce a three-tier governance output with mapped control recommendations. A specialised Coding Assistant extension is also included to account for nuances specific to this type of agentic AI system. We then use an illustrative example to show our framework in practice. ARC is intended for AI governance practitioners, risk officers, developers, and regulators, and it will regularly undergo iteration as we continue to expand it and make it more robust. The community can access the interactive framework here: https://arc.responsible.ai/

</details>


### [210] [Agora: Enhancing LLM Agent Reasoning Via Auction-Based Task Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09600)
*Kaiji Zhou,Ales Leonardis,Yue Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agora 提出一种基于激励相容拍卖机制的LLM推理框架，将推理步骤视为可交易物品，让专家模型根据修正后的能力竞标，从而将关键逻辑路由到最有能力的求解器，在五个基准上优于单一模型、路由和级联基线，并通过单一拍卖参数实现可控的成本-质量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM Agent框架在调用专家模型或工具时，仅基于任务与功能之间的粗粒度匹配，忽略了功能相似方案之间的性能差异和成本效率这两个关键因素，导致推理任务无法被路由到最优求解器。

Method: 提出Agora框架，引入激励相容的拍卖机制来动态分配推理任务。其核心创新在于：（1）将推理步骤建模为可交易物品；（2）专家模型基于"修正后的能力"（rectified competence）而非原始置信度进行竞标，从而避免过度自信模型胜出；（3）通过拍卖机制实现关键逻辑向最有能力求解器的路由。

Result: 在五个基准上的评估表明，在可比的候选模型池条件下，Agora优于匹配的单一模型基线、路由基线和级联基线，同时通过单一拍卖参数即可暴露可控的成本-质量权衡。

Conclusion: Agora通过拍卖机制有效解决了多专家模型协同推理中的动态任务分配问题，在性能与成本之间实现了灵活可控的平衡，为LLM Agent推理能力的增强提供了新范式。

Abstract: Enhancing the reasoning capabilities of large language model (LLM) agents requires effective orchestration of diverse expert models and tools.
